1770            "pv_atif_retention_panics_total",
1771            supervision_help::ATIF_RETENTION_PANICS,
1772        );
1773        metrics.counter(
1774            "pv_atif_retention_errors_total",
1775            supervision_help::ATIF_RETENTION_ERRORS,
1776        );
1777        // TCP_NODELAY setsockopt failure counter — written from the
1778        // axum `tap_io` per-accept hook when `set_nodelay(true)` on
1779        // the freshly-accepted socket fails (typically ENOTCONN /
1780        // EBADF on a SYN-flood / Slowloris torn-down socket, or
1781        // ENOPROTOOPT on an embedded target without full socket-
1782        // option support). The tracing warn is dampened via
1783        // `std::sync::Once` to avoid a per-accept log storm; this
1784        // counter keeps operator visibility of the failure rate.
1785        //
1786        // Help string MUST match the main.rs registration site
1787        // byte-exact: `Registry::counter` returns the pre-registered
1788        // Arc<Counter> and discards subsequent `help` strings, so
1789        // divergent HELP would silently drop the more informative
1790        // one from `/metrics`. Same discipline as every sibling
1791        // `*_panics_total` pre-registration.
1792        metrics.counter(
1793            "pv_tcp_nodelay_failures_total",
1794            "TCP_NODELAY setsockopt failed on an accepted connection; the \
1795             suboptimal-latency connection still serves. Rate-limited to a \
1796             single tracing warn per process to avoid a per-accept log storm \
1797             under half-open flood / SYN flood.",
1798        );
1799        // Drop-spawned close-session and admission-refund panics —
1800        // same rationale. Each is written only from a Drop-spawned
1801        // background task on the panic arm.
1802        metrics.counter(
1803            "pv_stream_abort_panics_total",
1804            "Background close after a stream abort panicked and was caught; \
1805             the session is left open until the idle sweeper reaps it",
1806        );
1807        metrics.counter(
1808            "pv_ephemeral_close_panics_total",
1809            "Auto-close of a completed ephemeral one-shot panicked and was \
1810             caught; the session is left open until the idle sweeper reaps it",
1811        );
1812        metrics.counter(
1813            "pv_admission_refund_panics_total",
1814            "Admission-debit refund closure panicked in Drop; the session \
1815             and principal ledgers retain the debit until operator repair",
1816        );
1817        // Idle-close per-session deadline counter. Same pre-
1818        // registration discipline: this counter is emitted only
1819        // from the timeout arm of `close_session` in the reconciler
1820        // idle-close sweep, so a lazy `rate() > 0` alert wouldn't
1821        // see the FIRST fire — exactly the incident the alert
1822        // exists to catch. See OPERATIONS.md.
1823        metrics.counter("pv_idle_close_timeouts_total", IDLE_CLOSE_TIMEOUTS_HELP);
1824        // Shutdown-time per-session close deadline counter. Fires
1825        // when the shutdown-path close_session (main.rs
1826        // finalize_sessions loop) hits its 3 s per-session deadline
1827        // — distinct from `pv_idle_close_timeouts_total` (steady-
1828        // state idle sweep) and `pv_http_shutdown_drain_timeouts_
1829        // total` (outer HTTP-drain phase). Pre-registration matches
1830        // the discipline for the rest of this block: a lazy
1831        // `rate() > 0` alert would miss the FIRST fire on the very
1832        // shutdown that surfaces the class.
1833        metrics.counter(
1834            "pv_shutdown_session_close_timeouts_total",
1835            SESSION_CLOSE_TIMEOUTS_HELP,
1836        );
1837        // Shutdown-time MCP-inflight drain-deadline counter. Fires when
1838        // the shutdown barrier waiting for detached mcp_call_inner
1839        // spawns to complete hits its 5 s deadline before `wait_idle`.
1840        // Without this barrier the detached body could reach
1841        // `worker.try_submit` AFTER `wait_idle` returned, race
1842        // `finalize_sessions`, and trip
1843        // `pv_shutdown_session_close_timeouts_total` on an otherwise-
1844        // recoverable session. Pre-registration matches the rest of
1845        // the block — any occurrence must be visible to `rate() > 0`.
1846        metrics.counter("pv_shutdown_mcp_drain_timeouts_total", MCP_DRAIN_TIMEOUTS_HELP);
1847        // Per-tick recovery-scan cap counter. Every recovery pass that
1848        // walks `read_dir` yields after `MAX_RECOVERY_ENTRIES_PER_TICK`
1849        // entries so a poisoned spool (millions of stale files) does
1850        // not starve legitimate recovery work. Pre-registering each
1851        // labelled series eagerly closes the same "lazy series that
1852        // rate()>0 never sees on the FIRST fire" defect the counters
1853        // above defeat. Keep this labels list in-sync with every call
1854        // to `bump_recovery_scan_cap`.
1855        for pass in [
1856            "adopt_strict_atif",
1857            "recover_signed_journals",
1858            "consolidate_step_journals",
1859            "retry_marked_promotions",
1860            "remove_acked_outboxes",
1861            "replay_lifecycle_outboxes",
1862            "quarantine_orphan_json",
1863        ] {
1864            metrics.counter(
1865                &format!("pv_recovery_scan_capped_total{{pass=\"{pass}\"}}"),
1866                "Recovery pass hit the per-tick entry-scan cap and returned \
1867                 early; the next tick will re-open the directory and resume. \
1868                 A steady rate > 0 indicates a poisoned spool (millions of \
1869                 stale files) that will starve legitimate recovery work \
1870                 until an operator quarantines it.",
1871            );
1872        }
1873        // Lifecycle-outbox backlog gauge. Rising baseline is the
1874        // earliest signal of a broker outage — outboxes persist
1875        // across restarts, so the disk-bounded queue grows until the
1876        // bridge is reachable again. Pre-registering the gauge (with
1877        // value 0) makes `absent(pv_lifecycle_outbox_pending)`
1878        // alerts distinguish "healthy, no backlog" from
1879        // "reconciler tick hasn't fired yet" — on a fresh boot the
1880        // gauge appears at 0 immediately, then updates on the first
1881        // `remove_acked_lifecycle_outboxes` tick.
1882        metrics.gauge(
1883            "pv_lifecycle_outbox_pending",
1884            crate::reconciler::LIFECYCLE_OUTBOX_PENDING_HELP,
1885        );
1886        // Reconciler ticks scan the ATIF spool dir, which can be large;
1887        // finalisation waits for worker drain + broker publish. Wide
1888        // bounds keep long-tail p99 useful under load.
1889        metrics.histogram_with_bounds(
1890            "pv_reconcile_duration_seconds",
1891            "Idle reconciliation duration",
1892            pv_core::metrics::WIDE_LATENCY_BOUNDS_US,
1893        );
1894        metrics.histogram_with_bounds(
1895            "pv_session_finalize_duration_seconds",
1896            "Session finalization latency",
1897            pv_core::metrics::WIDE_LATENCY_BOUNDS_US,
1898        );
1899        for endpoint in ["stats", "list", "detail"] {
1900            metrics.histogram(
1901                &format!("pv_dashboard_request_duration_seconds{{endpoint=\"{endpoint}\"}}"),
1902                "Dashboard endpoint latency",
1903            );
1904            metrics.counter(
1905                &format!("pv_dashboard_requests_total{{endpoint=\"{endpoint}\",status=\"ok\"}}"),
1906                "Dashboard endpoint requests, labeled by status",
1907            );
1908            metrics.counter(
1909                &format!("pv_dashboard_requests_total{{endpoint=\"{endpoint}\",status=\"not_found\"}}"),
1910                "Dashboard endpoint requests, labeled by status",
1911            );
1912        }
1913        let sessions = Arc::new(SessionRegistry::new());
1914        let journal_key = crate::journal::key_from_signer(signer.as_ref());
1915        let redaction = if !config.redaction_patterns.is_empty() || !config.redaction_paths.is_empty() {
1916            let redact_config = pv_redact::RedactionConfig {
1917                regex_patterns: config.redaction_patterns.clone(),
1918                pointer_paths: config.redaction_paths.clone(),
1919                include_builtin_patterns: true,
1920                ..pv_redact::RedactionConfig::default()
1921            };
1922            let engine = pv_redact::RedactionEngine::new(redact_config)
1923                .map_err(|e| PipelineError::bad_request(format!("redaction engine: {e}")))?;
1924            Some(Arc::new(engine))
1925        } else {
1926            None
1927        };
1928        let content_sink = crate::content::SharedContentSink::default();
1929        let judge_hooks = crate::judge::SharedJudgeHooks::default();
1930        let worker = crate::worker::spawn_worker_with_outputs(
1931            config.worker_channel_capacity,
1932            Arc::clone(&bridge),
1933            embedder,
1934            Arc::clone(&vector_sink),
1935            Some(std::path::PathBuf::from(&config.atif_spool_dir)),
1936            journal_key,
1937            Arc::clone(&metrics),
1938            crate::content::AuditOutputs {
1939                redaction: redaction.clone(),
1940                content: Arc::clone(&content_sink),
1941                judge: Arc::clone(&judge_hooks),
1942            },
1943        );
1944        let finalizer = Finalizer::with_bridge(
1945            signer.clone(),
1946            std::path::PathBuf::from(&config.atif_spool_dir),
1947            Arc::clone(&metrics),
1948            Arc::clone(&bridge),
1949        )
1950        .with_state_store(Arc::clone(&store))
1951        // Closes delete the session's vector-store
1952        // points (best-effort).
1953        .with_vector_sink(Arc::clone(&vector_sink));
1954        // One builder for every outbound client, so the backend client
1955        // differs from the shared one only by its resolver.
1956        let build_client = |private_only: bool| -> Result<reqwest::Client, PipelineError> {
1957            let mut client_builder = reqwest::Client::builder()
1958                .connect_timeout(HTTP_CONNECT_TIMEOUT)
1959                .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::none())
1960                // Providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Azure, Bedrock fronting)
1961                // downgrade or block empty / generic UAs; identifying
1962                // ourselves also gives operators a stable string for
1963                // provider-side triage. Version is baked in at compile
1964                // time so a rolling deploy makes the change visible.
1965                .user_agent(concat!("ProofVisor/", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")))
1966                // TCP keepalive so pooled connections behind NAT/L4 LBs
1967                // with short idle windows (AWS NLB 350 s, GCP TCP 600 s,
1968                // stateful FWs often 60-120 s) do not turn every first
1969                // request per pool cycle into a full TCP+TLS re-handshake
1970                // that manifests as a spurious 502/`connection reset`.
1971                .tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30))
1972                // Cap pool idle at 60 s: shorter than any realistic NAT
1973                // window, longer than any burst-of-requests batch. Bounds
1974                // the pool memory footprint and forces frequent-enough
1975                // TLS refresh under a rolling cert rotation.
1976                .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(60));
1977            // Reqwest 0.12 is built here
1978            // without decompression features, so the proxy cannot decode any
1979            // content-coding an origin might apply. RFC 9110 §12.5.3 says
1980            // an ABSENT Accept-Encoding permits any coding, so a CDN in
1981            // front of the upstream may legally gzip the response — which
1982            // the chat path then 502's on and the tool path (with the
1983            // decompression guard applied above) would also refuse. Advertise
1984            // `Accept-Encoding: identity` so the routine outcome is
1985            // "identity as requested" and the refusal guards are pure
1986            // defense-in-depth.
1987            let mut default_headers = reqwest::header::HeaderMap::new();
1988            default_headers.insert(
1989                reqwest::header::ACCEPT_ENCODING,
1990                reqwest::header::HeaderValue::from_static("identity"),
1991            );
1992            client_builder = client_builder.default_headers(default_headers);
1993            // Apply a read-timeout floor unconditionally so an
1994            // adversarial or merely broken upstream (chat OR tool) cannot pin
1995            // a session lease + WorkerPermit + tool-intent claim
1996            // indefinitely by accepting the request and then never
1997            // responding. TCP keepalive above only detects a hung
1998            // *connection*, not a slow/silent HTTP response. Operators can
1999            // widen or override via `upstream_read_timeout_s`; the shipped
2000            // default (60 s) is well past any realistic first-token
2001            // latency (Claude p99 ~15 s, GPT-4 p99 ~30 s) but firm enough
2002            // that a stalled provider surfaces as a definite 502 rather
2003            // than a resource-starving hang.
2004            const DEFAULT_UPSTREAM_READ_TIMEOUT_S: u64 = crate::config::DEFAULT_UPSTREAM_READ_TIMEOUT_S;
2005            let read_timeout_s = config
2006                .upstream_read_timeout_s
2007                .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_UPSTREAM_READ_TIMEOUT_S);
2008            client_builder = client_builder.read_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(read_timeout_s));
2009            if config.upstream_http2_prior_knowledge {
2010                client_builder = client_builder.http2_prior_knowledge();
2011            }
2012            if private_only {
2013                client_builder = client_builder.dns_resolver(Arc::new(crate::backend::PrivateOnlyResolver));
2014            }
2015            client_builder.build().map_err(PipelineError::upstream_source)
2016        };
2017        let client = build_client(false)?;
2018        // Tool backends get their own client when `require_private_backends`
2019        // is on: it refuses to connect to any address that is not private.
2020        // The LLM upstream and the AuthZEN PDP keep the shared client.
2021        let backend_client = if config.require_private_backends {
2022            build_client(true)?
2023        } else {
2024            client.clone()
2025        };
2026        let upstream_auth = resolve_upstream_auth(&config)?;
2027        let tool_auth = resolve_tool_auth(&config)?;
2028        let hot_metrics = Arc::new(HotMetrics::new(&metrics, config.strict_stage_budget));
2029        // S3: config validation already refused unsupported providers;
2030        // this defense-in-depth error covers direct constructors that
2031        // bypassed validate() (test builders).
2032        let provider = crate::provider::adapter_for(&config.provider).ok_or_else(|| {
2033            PipelineError::bad_request(format!("unsupported provider {:?}", config.provider))
2034        })?;
2035        tracing::info!(provider = provider.name(), "provider adapter selected");
2036        let mcp_admission = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Semaphore::new(config.mcp_concurrency));
2037        // R72 review F1 + F2 (landed R74): eager registration
2038        // stores the returned `Arc<Counter>` handle so the
2039        // refusal arm calls `.inc()` directly, bypassing the
2040        // registry mutex on the hot path. Prior R72 shape
2041        // eagerly registered but discarded the handle,
2042        // requiring a re-lookup per refusal.
2043        let mcp_admission_refusals_counter = metrics.counter(
2044            "pv_mcp_admission_refusals_total",
2045            "MCP admission was refused because `mcp_concurrency` was already at capacity — \
2046             each admitted call can buffer up to 16 MiB in `read_limited_tool_response`, \
2047             so the cap bounds worst-case MCP resident memory. Sustained > 0 indicates \
2048             tool-upstream slowdown, a burst-traffic event, or an undersized \
2049             `mcp_concurrency`.",
2050        );
2051        for pdp_code in ["UNMAPPED_TOOL", "MISSION_EXPIRED", "MISSION_DENIED", "PDP_DENIED"] {
2052            metrics.counter(
2053                &format!("pv_pdp_denials_total{{code=\"{pdp_code}\"}}"),
2054                "PDP denials by denial code",
2055            );
2056        }
2057        metrics.counter(
2058            crate::routes::TOKENS_REVOKED_METRIC,
2059            crate::routes::TOKENS_REVOKED_HELP,
2060        );
2061        let backend_router = crate::backend::BackendRouter::new(
2062            &config.backends,
2063            config.tool_upstream_url.as_deref(),
2064            tool_auth.clone(),
2065        )
2066        .map_err(|e| PipelineError::bad_request(format!("backend routing: {e}")))?
2067        .with_fallback_transport(config.tool_upstream_transport);
2068        let mcp_backends = Arc::new(crate::mcp_client::McpBackendClient::new(backend_client.clone()));
2069        let mcp_sessions = Arc::new(crate::mcp::SessionSigner::derive(&journal_key));
2070        let authzen = config
2071            .authzen
2072            .as_ref()
2073            .map(|authzen| crate::authzen::AuthzenClient::from_config(authzen, client.clone()).map(Arc::new))
2074            .transpose()
2075            .map_err(|e| PipelineError::bad_request(format!("authzen: {e}")))?;
2076        let workload = crate::workload::WorkloadIssuer::from_config(&config)
2077            .map_err(|e| PipelineError::bad_request(format!("workload identity: {e}")))?
2078            .map(Arc::new);
2079        let token_signer = if let Some(ref seed_path) = config.token_exchange_seed_file {
2080            let seed = load_exchange_seed(seed_path)
2081                .map_err(|e| PipelineError::bad_request(format!("exchange signing key: {e}")))?;
2082            let ts = Arc::new(crate::authz::TokenSigner::from_seed(&seed));
2083            if ts.kid() == signer.key_id() {
2084                return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(
2085                    "token_exchange_seed_file must use a different key than the receipt signer \
2086                     (both derive the same kid, which means the same seed)"
2087                        .to_owned(),
2088                ));
2089            }
2090            Some(ts)
2091        } else {
2092            None
2093        };
2094        let pdp = crate::authz::PolicyDecisionPoint::from_config(&config, token_signer.clone());
2095        let exchanged_revocations: Arc<dyn pv_identity::RevocationStore> = if config.state_backend == "redis"
2096        {
2097            Arc::new(crate::revocation::GuardedRevocationStore::new(
2098                Arc::new(crate::revocation::StateRevocationStore::with_namespace(
2099                    Arc::clone(&store),
2100                    "exchanged",
2101                )),
2102                &metrics,
2103                "exchanged",
2104            ))
2105        } else {
2106            Arc::new(pv_identity::InMemoryRevocationStore::new())
2107        };
2108        Ok(Self {
2109            config,
2110            store,
2111            sandbox,
2112            bridge,
2113            sessions,
2114            worker,
2115            identity,
2116            exchanged_revocations,
2117            revocation_audit_window: Arc::new(parking_lot::Mutex::new((Instant::now(), 0))),
2118            hot_metrics,
2119            metrics,
2120            client,
2121            backend_client,
2122            upstream_auth,
2123            tool_auth,
2124            tool_audit_gates: Arc::default(),
2125            tool_audits_emitted: Arc::default(),
2126            provider,
2127            finalizer,
2128            draining: Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false)),
2129            identity_rejection_window: Arc::new(parking_lot::Mutex::new((Instant::now(), 0))),
2130            journal_key,
2131            mcp_inflight: Arc::new(crate::inflight::InflightTracker::new()),
2132            pushed_rates: Arc::new(crate::policy_push::RateLimits::default()),
2133            mcp_admission,
2134            mcp_admission_refusals_counter,
2135            backend_router: Arc::new(backend_router),
2136            pdp: Arc::new(pdp),
2137            token_signer,
2138            redaction,
2139            mcp_backends,
2140            mcp_sessions,
2141            authzen,
2142            content_sink,
2143            workload,
2144            judge: None,
2145            judge_hooks,
2146        })
2147    }
2148
2149    /// Attach the semantic judge. Build the sandbox with
2150    /// `Sandbox::with_judge_policy(config.threshold_policy())` so the
2151    /// thresholds the judge's verdicts meet are the configured ones.
2152    #[must_use]
2153    pub fn with_judge(mut self, judge: Arc<crate::judge::JudgeService>) -> Self {
2154        // A store shared between instances also keeps the refusals the
2155        // judge sees, so delegated agents on other instances see them.
2156        judge.share_denials(Arc::clone(&self.store));
2157        // The refusals the judge sees outlive a restart: rebuilt from the
2158        // recorded tool-call events of an embedded Bridge on this machine.
2159        if matches!(self.config.bridge(), Ok(crate::config::BridgeBackend::Embedded)) {
2160            let events = crate::judge::recorded_tool_calls(
2161                std::path::Path::new(&self.config.bridge_data_dir),
2162                crate::judge::RESTORE_TAIL_BYTES,
2163            );
2164            let sandbox = Arc::clone(&self.sandbox);
2165            let known_tool = |tool: &str| sandbox.has_schema(tool) || judge.tasks_for(tool).is_some();
2166            let restored = judge.restore_denials(&events, &known_tool, pv_core::time::now_ms());
2167            if restored > 0 {
2168                tracing::info!(
2169                    restored,
2170                    "semantic judge: earlier refusals restored from the recorded events"
2171                );
2172            }
2173        }
2174        *self.judge_hooks.write() = Some(Arc::new(crate::judge::JudgeHooks::new(
2175            Arc::clone(&judge),
2176            self.worker.clone(),
2177            Arc::clone(&self.pdp),
2178            Arc::clone(&self.metrics),
2179        )));
2180        self.judge = Some(judge);
2181        self
2182    }
2183
2184    /// Install the sink that receives every journaled step in content form
2185    /// (used by `proofvisord` for full-content tenant telemetry).
2186    ///
2187    /// The sink runs on its own thread behind a bounded queue, so it can
2188    /// neither slow down nor fail the audit worker.
2189    pub fn set_content_sink(&self, sink: Arc<dyn crate::content::ContentSink>) -> std::io::Result<()> {
2190        let dispatcher = crate::content::ContentDispatcher::spawn(sink, &self.metrics)?;
2191        *self.content_sink.write() = Some(Arc::new(dispatcher));
2192        Ok(())
2193    }
2194
2195    /// Run identity, breaker, quota, sanitize, compression, and asynchronous
2196    /// dispatch in the mandated order without awaiting worker or upstream I/O.
2197    ///
2198    /// `identity_hint`, when `Some`, is trusted as the resolved identity for
2199    /// this request — the internal `resolve_identity` + audit-enqueue step
2200    /// is skipped. Used by `chat_completions` (R72 F3 pre-admission gate)
2201    /// which already resolved the identity to fail-fast on unauthenticated
2202    /// probes before the body parses. Without this hint, the R72 pre-check
2203    /// path would `enqueue_transient_failure` on a JWKS-rotation race
2204    /// (pre-check success + re-check failure between the two calls),
2205    /// double-counting `pv_identity_rejections_total` and burning two
2206    /// of the sampled full-audit slots per rejected request (R73 review
2207    /// F3). `None` preserves the historical behaviour for callers that
2208    /// do NOT pre-resolve.
2209    pub fn prepare_chat(
2210        &self,
2211        headers: &HeaderMap,
2212        payload: Value,
2213        identity_hint: Option<AgentIdentity>,
2214    ) -> Result<PreparedRequest, PipelineError> {
2215        self.prepare_chat_with_caller(headers, payload, identity_hint, None)
2216    }
2217
2218    fn prepare_chat_with_caller(
2219        &self,
2220        headers: &HeaderMap,
2221        mut payload: Value,
2222        identity_hint: Option<AgentIdentity>,
2223        caller: Option<&AuthenticatedCaller>,
2224    ) -> Result<PreparedRequest, PipelineError> {
2225        let total_started = Instant::now();
2226        let session_id = session_id(headers)?;
2227        let workflow = workflow(headers, &self.config.default_workflow)?;
2228        // Multi-choice completions (`n > 1`) are accepted up to
2229        // `MAX_CHOICES`: the capture path accumulates every choice
2230        // separately (text, tool calls and finish reason keyed by
2231        // `choices[].index`), so the ATIF step attests each choice as
2232        // its own message. Refuse anything that is not a plain integer
2233        // in range up front, before any budget debit: upstream
2234        // OpenAI-compatible servers lax-coerce `2.0` / `"2"` into real
2235        // multi-choice requests, so the check fails closed on those
2236        // encodings instead of forwarding them.
2237        crate::provider::choice_count(&payload).map_err(PipelineError::bad_request)?;
2238
2239        // Validate and extract the relayed headers BEFORE any budget
2240        // debit or admission journaling: `single_header` refuses
2241        // multi-valued occurrences, and its `?` used to fire from the
2242        // `PreparedRequest` construction AFTER both ledgers were
2243        // debited with no refund arm — a client repeating a duplicate
2244        // `openai-beta` header drained session + principal budgets
2245        // without a single upstream call.
2246        let client_authorization = if self.config.upstream_authorization_passthrough {
2247            single_header(headers, "authorization")?.cloned()
2248        } else {
2249            None
2250        };
2251        // Strict allowlist of provider-semantic headers. Same
2252        // single-value discipline as every other relayed header
2253        // (a multi-valued occurrence is refused at ingress).
2254        const UPSTREAM_HEADER_ALLOWLIST: [&str; 3] = ["openai-organization", "openai-project", "openai-beta"];
2255        let mut upstream_passthrough_headers = Vec::new();
2256        for name in UPSTREAM_HEADER_ALLOWLIST {
2257            if let Some(value) = single_header(headers, name)? {
2258                upstream_passthrough_headers.push((axum::http::HeaderName::from_static(name), value.clone()));
2259            }
2260        }
2261
2262        let stage = Instant::now();
2263        let identity = if let Some(pre_resolved) = identity_hint {
2264            // R73 review F3: pre-resolved from an earlier caller
2265            // (chat_completions pre-admission gate). Skip the
2266            // internal resolve + audit-enqueue so a rotation race
2267            // between the two calls cannot double-count
2268            // `pv_identity_rejections_total` or burn a second
2269            // full-audit sample slot per request.
2270            pre_resolved
2271        } else {
2272            match self.authenticate(headers).and_then(|caller| {
2273                let identity = caller.authorize(self, Some(&self.config.chat_scope))?;
2274                self.require_signed_workflow_scope(workflow, Some(&caller))?;
2275                Ok(identity)
2276            }) {
2277                Ok(identity) => identity,
2278                Err(error) => {
2279                    self.audit_authentication_error(headers, &error)?;
2280                    return Err(error);
2281                }
2282            }
2283        };
2284        if caller.is_some() {
2285            self.require_signed_workflow_scope(workflow, caller)?;
2286        }
2287        self.observe_stage("identity", stage);
2288
2289        let session = self
2290            .sessions
2291            .get_or_open(&session_id, workflow, &identity, &self.config.breaker);
2292        let admission = session.admission_guard();
2293        validate_session_binding(&session, workflow, &identity)?;
2294        if let Some(caller) = caller {
2295            caller.bind_session(&session)?;
2296        }
2297        session.refresh_identity(&identity);
2298        if session.is_closed() {
2299            // Distinguish the PERMANENT quarantine (empty-unsigned close
2300            // refusal — deliberately never recycled) from the TRANSIENT
2301            // closing window. `get_or_open` recycles completed-close
2302            // entries, so a session observed here as closed-but-not-
2303            // complete is mid-close: an SDK auto-retry (e.g. after a
2304            // read-timeout disconnect triggered the background close)
2305            // used to hit a terminal 400 "already closed" — misleading
2306            // (the client closed nothing) and never retried by SDKs,
2307            // even though the very next attempt would have succeeded
2308            // against the recycled id. 503 + Retry-After lets standard
2309            // retry policies ride out the window.
2310            // Sealed capture-failed quarantines are ALSO closed-but-
2311            // never-complete — permanently (never recycled, never
2312            // evicted). They keep the terminal 400 (permanent
2313            // refusals route to 400 with no Retry-After);
2314            // the transient 503 below would otherwise instruct SDK
2315            // retry policies to poll forever against a session that
2316            // will refuse forever. Checked BEFORE the empty-quarantine
2317            // predicate, which capture-failed conversions can also
2318            // match — the "no captured steps" message was inaccurate
2319            // for sessions that DID capture steps before failing.
2320            if session.capture_failed() {
2321                return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(
2322                    "session is quarantined (audit capture failed); use a new session id".to_owned(),
2323                ));
2324            }
2325            if session.is_empty_unsigned_quarantine() {
2326                return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(
2327                    "session is quarantined (closed with no captured steps); use a new session id".to_owned(),
2328                ));
2329            }
2330            // Enforcement-latched sessions are terminal for the id:
2331            // `get_or_open` deliberately refuses to recycle them (a
2332            // fresh incarnation would carry a fresh budget/breaker on
2333            // the same id). 403, matching the deliberate not-429/not-
2334            // transient policy for budget and breaker refusals. Keyed
2335            // on the sticky latch, not the overwritable last stop
2336            // reason.
2337            if session.close_complete_flag() && session.enforcement_tripped() {
2338                return Err(PipelineError::blocked(
2339                    "session was closed by budget or loop enforcement; use a new session id".to_owned(),
2340                ));
2341            }
2342            return Err(PipelineError::unavailable(
2343                "session close is completing; retry shortly or use a new session id".to_owned(),
2344            ));
2345        }
2346        if session.capture_failed() {
2347            return Err(PipelineError::unavailable(
2348                "session audit capture is incomplete".to_owned(),
2349            ));
2350        }
2351        session.touch();
2352        let mut injected_corrective = false;
2353        if session.loop_state.state() == BreakerState::Open {
2354            match session.loop_state.action() {
2355                BreakerAction::Reject => {
2356                    session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::LoopDetected);
2357                    return Err(PipelineError::blocked(
2358                        "semantic loop circuit breaker is open".to_owned(),
2359                    ));
2360                }
2361                BreakerAction::Abort => {
2362                    session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::LoopDetected);
2363                    return Err(PipelineError::Abort(
2364                        "semantic loop circuit breaker requested connection close".to_owned(),
2365                    ));
2366                }
2367                BreakerAction::Inject => {
2368                    inject_corrective_message(&mut payload)?;
2369                    injected_corrective = true;
2370                    session.loop_state.reset();
2371                }
2372                _ => {
2373                    return Err(PipelineError::blocked(
2374                        "unsupported semantic loop enforcement action".to_owned(),
2375                    ));
2376                }
2377            }
2378        }
2379
2380        // Fused acquire: one worker slot + one response slot atomically.
2381        // If the worker slot succeeds and the response slot fails, the
2382        // worker permit drops via RAII (its OwnedSemaphorePermit and
2383        // mpsc reservation release cleanly), so the caller never sees
2384        // an orphaned half-reservation. Distinct
2385        // `pv_events_dropped_total{stage=worker_queue|response_slot}`
2386        // counters let operators tell which one exhausted.
2387        let permits = self
2388            .worker
2389            .try_reserve_pair(&session_id)
2390            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?;
2391        let worker_permit = permits.worker;
2392        let response_permit = permits.response;
2393
2394        let stage = Instant::now();
2395        let raw_prompt_tokens = pv_core::tokens::approx_tokens_json(&payload);
2396        // Budget is charged AFTER compression on `compression.tokens_after`
2397        // (below). Deferring the debit closes the enforcement/attestation
2398        // gap the review flagged in §6.3: previously the ledger was debited
2399        // on the uncompressed payload while the receipt attested the
2400        // post-compression count, over-charging clients by ~40% on any
2401        // compressible history and making compression's savings invisible
2402        // to `max_tokens`. The genuine-cap-refusal latch (upstream's
2403        // enforcement-latch rounds 11-13) is applied at the deferred
2404        // charge site below.
2405        self.observe_stage("quota", stage);
2406
2407        let stage = Instant::now();
2408        if let Err(denial) = self.sandbox.sanitize("chat/completions", &payload) {
2409            let error = PipelineError::blocked(denial.reason);
2410            // No token refund needed: the budget debit happens AFTER
2411            // compression (below), so a sanitize block exits before
2412            // anything was charged. A console-pushed guardrail names its
2413            // policy, so the stop-reason event attributes the block.
2414            worker_permit.submit(self.policy_failure_job(
2415                Arc::clone(&session),
2416                identity.clone(),
2417                StopReason::PolicyBlocked,
2418                error.to_string(),
2419                denial.policy,
2420            ));
2421            return Err(error);
2422        }
2423        self.observe_stage("sanitize", stage);
2424
2425        // Console-pushed transforms (redaction) rewrite the messages after
2426        // the local chain and the guardrails have seen the original text,
2427        // and before compression, the budget and dispatch: what is
2428        // journaled and what is forwarded is the rewritten payload.
2429        let transforms = self.sandbox.transform_chat(&mut payload);
2430        let raw_prompt_tokens = if transforms.is_empty() {
2431            raw_prompt_tokens
2432        } else {
2433            for transform in &transforms {
2434                tracing::info!(
2435                    session = %session_id,
2436                    policy = %transform.policy,
2437                    replacements = transform.replacements,
2438                    "chat request rewritten by a console-pushed transform"
2439                );
2440            }
2441            self.metrics
2442                .counter(
2443                    "pv_pushed_transform_rewrites_total",
2444                    "Tool calls or chat requests rewritten by a console-pushed transform policy",
2445                )
2446                .inc();
2447            pv_core::tokens::approx_tokens_json(&payload)
2448        };
2449
2450        let stage = Instant::now();
2451        let (compression, repinned_rules) = if self.config.compression_enabled {
2452            let outcome = pv_compress::compress(
2453                &payload,
2454                &pv_compress::CompressionConfig {
2455                    // Authenticate compression stubs under a key derived
2456                    // from the journal key, so user text that quotes a
2457                    // stub cannot impersonate a machine-emitted one and
2458                    // disable the middle-history pass.
2459                    marker_key: Some(pv_compress::derive_marker_key(&self.journal_key)),
2460                    ..pv_compress::CompressionConfig::default()
2461                },
2462            );
2463            self.repin_standing_rules(&payload, outcome)
2464        } else {
2465            (
2466                pv_compress::CompressionOutcome {
2467                    payload,
2468                    tokens_before: raw_prompt_tokens,
2469                    tokens_after: raw_prompt_tokens,
2470                    changed: false,
2471                },
2472                Vec::new(),
2473            )
2474        };
2475        self.observe_stage("compression", stage);
2476
2477        // Console-pushed token-rate policy (per principal, sliding
2478        // window), checked before either budget ledger is debited so a
2479        // refusal debits nothing. Admitted tokens count against the
2480        // window even if a budget below then refuses the request, which
2481        // errs on the strict side.
2482        if let Err(refusal) = self
2483            .pushed_rates
2484            .admit(&principal_id_for_budget(&identity), compression.tokens_after)
2485        {
2486            let error = PipelineError::blocked(refusal.message);
2487            worker_permit.submit(self.policy_failure_job(
2488                Arc::clone(&session),
2489                identity.clone(),
2490                StopReason::PolicyBlocked,
2491                error.to_string(),
2492                Some(refusal.policy),
2493            ));
2494            return Err(error);
2495        }
2496
2497        // Post-compression budget charge. Both the principal and session
2498        // ledgers spend `compression.tokens_after` — the same number
2499        // the signed receipt attests, so budget refusals and the audit
2500        // artifact always agree. On refusal, if the principal debit
2501        // landed but the session cap refuses, the principal amount is
2502        // refunded to keep failed requests non-consumptive.
2503        let stage = Instant::now();
2504        let billed_tokens = compression.tokens_after;
2505        let principal_billing_id = self
2506            .config
2507            .principal_budget
2508            .as_ref()
2509            .map(|_| principal_id_for_budget(&identity));
2510        if let (Some(spec), Some(principal_id)) = (
2511            self.config.principal_budget.as_ref(),
2512            principal_billing_id.as_deref(),
2513        ) {
2514            let quota = match ActionBudget::for_principal(self.store.as_ref(), principal_id, spec)
2515                .try_tokens(billed_tokens)
2516            {
2517                Ok(quota) => quota,
2518                Err(error) => {
2519                    let error = PipelineError::Blocked {
2520                        context: format!("quota backend failed closed (principal): {error}"),
2521                        source: Some(Box::new(error)),
2522                    };
2523                    worker_permit.submit(self.failure_job(
2524                        Arc::clone(&session),
2525                        identity.clone(),
2526                        StopReason::BudgetExceeded,
2527                        error.to_string(),
2528                    ));
2529                    return Err(error);
2530                }
2531            };
2532            if let BudgetDecision::Refused { limit, cap } = quota {
2533                // Genuine cap exhaustion — latch (upstream rounds 11-13;
2534                // the backend-ERROR arms deliberately do not: a store
2535                // outage is not enforcement).
2536                session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::BudgetExceeded);
2537                let error = PipelineError::blocked(format!("principal.{limit} exceeded (cap {cap})"));
2538                worker_permit.submit(self.failure_job(
2539                    Arc::clone(&session),
2540                    identity.clone(),
2541                    StopReason::BudgetExceeded,
2542                    error.to_string(),
2543                ));
2544                return Err(error);
2545            }
2546        }
2547        let quota = match ActionBudget::new(self.store.as_ref(), &session_id, &self.effective_budget())
2548            .try_tokens(billed_tokens)
2549        {
2550            Ok(quota) => quota,
2551            Err(error) => {
2552                if let (Some(spec), Some(principal_id)) = (
2553                    self.config.principal_budget.as_ref(),
2554                    principal_billing_id.as_deref(),
2555                ) {
2556                    ActionBudget::for_principal(self.store.as_ref(), principal_id, spec)
2557                        .refund_tokens(billed_tokens);
2558                }
2559                let error = PipelineError::Blocked {
2560                    context: format!("quota backend failed closed: {error}"),
2561                    source: Some(Box::new(error)),
2562                };
2563                worker_permit.submit(self.failure_job(
2564                    Arc::clone(&session),
2565                    identity.clone(),
2566                    StopReason::BudgetExceeded,
2567                    error.to_string(),
2568                ));
2569                return Err(error);
2570            }
2571        };
2572        if let BudgetDecision::Refused { limit, cap } = quota {
2573            if let (Some(spec), Some(principal_id)) = (
2574                self.config.principal_budget.as_ref(),
2575                principal_billing_id.as_deref(),
2576            ) {
2577                ActionBudget::for_principal(self.store.as_ref(), principal_id, spec)
2578                    .refund_tokens(billed_tokens);
2579            }
2580            // Genuine cap exhaustion — latch (upstream rounds 11-13).
2581            session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::BudgetExceeded);
2582            // When the binding cap came from a console-pushed budget
2583            // policy, the refusal names it, as the tool path does.
2584            let policy = self
2585                .sandbox
2586                .pushed()
2587                .budget_policy_for(&self.config.budget, &limit, cap);
2588            let error = PipelineError::blocked(match &policy {
2589                Some(name) => format!("policy {name:?}: {limit} exceeded (cap {cap})"),
2590                None => format!("{limit} exceeded (cap {cap})"),
2591            });
2592            worker_permit.submit(self.policy_failure_job(
2593                Arc::clone(&session),
2594                identity.clone(),
2595                StopReason::BudgetExceeded,
2596                error.to_string(),
2597                policy,
2598            ));
2599            return Err(error);
2600        }
2601        self.observe_stage("quota_post_compression", stage);
2602
2603        let stage = Instant::now();
2604        // The audit records the caller's turn, not the breaker's
2605        // corrective message appended after it.
2606        let caller_view = caller_turn_view(&compression.payload, injected_corrective);
2607        let captured = caller_view.as_ref().unwrap_or(&compression.payload);
2608        let text = last_message_text(captured);
2609        let atif = match atif_capture_from_request(captured) {
2610            Ok(atif) => atif,
2611            Err(error) => {
2612                // Never forwarded — refund the post-compression debit on
2613                // BOTH ledgers (see the refusal arms above).
2614                ActionBudget::new(self.store.as_ref(), &session_id, &self.effective_budget())
2615                    .refund_tokens(billed_tokens);
2616                if let (Some(spec), Some(principal_id)) = (
2617                    self.config.principal_budget.as_ref(),
2618                    principal_billing_id.as_deref(),
2619                ) {
2620                    ActionBudget::for_principal(self.store.as_ref(), principal_id, spec)
2621                        .refund_tokens(billed_tokens);
2622                }
2623                worker_permit.submit(self.failure_job(
2624                    Arc::clone(&session),
2625                    identity.clone(),
2626                    StopReason::Other,
2627                    error.to_string(),
2628                ));
2629                return Err(error);
2630            }
2631        };
2632        let analyze_loop = atif.source == pv_atif::Source::Agent && !text.is_empty();
2633        let response_attempt_id = pv_core::new_event_uid();
2634        let job = WorkerJob {
2635            session: Arc::clone(&session),
2636            identity: identity.clone(),
2637            class: EventClass::Compression,
2638            payload: {
2639                let mut record = serde_json::json!({
2640                    "changed": compression.changed,
2641                    "tokens_before": compression.tokens_before,
2642                    "tokens_after": compression.tokens_after,
2643                });
2644                // Standing rules compression had cut and that were
2645                // appended back.
2646                if let (false, Some(object)) = (repinned_rules.is_empty(), record.as_object_mut()) {
2647                    object.insert("repinned_rules".to_owned(), serde_json::json!(repinned_rules));
2648                }
2649                // Which pushed transforms rewrote the request, and how
2650                // many matches each replaced. The matched text is gone.
2651                if let (false, Some(object)) = (transforms.is_empty(), record.as_object_mut()) {
2652                    object.insert(
2653                        "transforms".to_owned(),
2654                        Value::Array(
2655                            transforms
2656                                .iter()
2657                                .map(|t| serde_json::json!({"policy": t.policy, "replacements": t.replacements}))
2658                                .collect(),
2659                        ),
2660                    );
2661                }
2662                record
2663            },
2664            text,
2665            analyze_loop,
2666            status: StatusId::Success,
2667            stop_reason: None,
2668            native_stop_reason: None,
2669            metrics: EventMetrics {
2670                prompt_tokens: Some(compression.tokens_after),
2671                completion_tokens: Some(0),
2672                cached_tokens: Some(0),
2673                pruned_tokens: Some(compression.pruned_tokens()),
2674                pruning_ratio_millis: Some(compression.pruning_ratio_millis()),
2675            },
2676            cost_usd_micros: 0,
2677            prompt_token_correction: 0,
2678            atif: Some(atif),
2679            response_marker: None,
2680            response_attempt: Some(crate::worker::ResponseAttempt {
2681                id: response_attempt_id.clone(),
2682                terminal: false,
2683            }),
2684        };
2685        worker_permit.submit(job);
2686        self.observe_stage("dispatch", stage);
2687        let lease = SessionLease::new(Arc::clone(&session));
2688        // Armed from here on: any early exit that drops the request —
2689        // including the `?` on the passthrough header below and, above
2690        // all, the request future being cancelled by a client disconnect
2691        // at any later await — submits the terminal failure record that
2692        // closes the journalled attempt (and retires the marker, once
2693        // `forward_chat` sets one).
2694        let capture_guard = ResponseCaptureGuard::new(
2695            self.worker.clone(),
2696            Arc::clone(&session),
2697            identity.clone(),
2698            response_attempt_id,
2699            Some(response_permit),
2700        );
2701        drop(admission);
2702
2703        let admission_debit = AdmissionDebit {
2704            store: Arc::clone(&self.store),
2705            session_id: session.id.clone(),
2706            budget: self.effective_budget(),
2707            principal: self
2708                .config
2709                .principal_budget
2710                .as_ref()
2711                .map(|spec| (principal_id_for_budget(&identity), spec.clone())),
2712            tokens: billed_tokens,
2713            metrics: Arc::clone(&self.metrics),
2714        };
2715        Ok(PreparedRequest {
2716            session,
2717            identity,
2718            payload: compression.payload,
2719            middleware_us: elapsed_us(total_started),
2720            admission_debit,
2721            lease,
2722            capture_guard,
2723            client_authorization,
2724            upstream_passthrough_headers,
2725        })
2726    }
2727
2728    /// Run synchronous local gates without waiting for off-path journal,
2729    /// embedding, or broker work.
2730    ///
2731    /// The inline/blocking decision keys off PAYLOAD
2732    /// SIZE, not off whether a token budget happens to be configured.
2733    /// The cost of `prepare_chat` (parse-adjacent token scanning,
2734    /// sanitize, compression) is a function of body size — measured
2735    /// 1.9 ms p50 of uninterruptible CPU at a 128 KB body, which
2736    /// stalls every in-flight SSE stream's poll on that tokio worker.
2737    /// Previously a configured `budget.max_tokens` (the one thing that
2738    /// makes quotas work) was what moved the work to `spawn_blocking`,
2739    /// which was backwards. Small bodies stay inline (spawn_blocking
2740    /// costs ~10-20 µs of handoff — 2× the total gate cost at 75 B).
2741    ///
2742    /// The `budget.max_tokens.is_none()` clause below is load-bearing,
2743    /// not a leftover: with a budget configured, `prepare_chat` calls
2744    /// `ActionBudget::try_tokens` → `StateStore::try_spend`, which on
2745    /// the redis backend is a synchronous network round-trip — that
2746    /// must never run inline on a tokio worker, whatever the body size.
2747    /// The same applies to `[principal_budget]`: it drives its own
2748    /// `for_principal(...).try_tokens` store round-trip in
2749    /// `prepare_chat` even when the session `max_tokens` is unset, so
2750    /// it must equally force the blocking pool.
2751    /// Async wrapper over [`Self::prepare_chat`] that spawn-blocks on
2752    /// bodies large enough to stall the reactor. See `prepare_chat`
2753    /// for the `identity_hint` contract.
2754    pub async fn prepare_chat_nonblocking(
2755        &self,
2756        headers: &HeaderMap,
2757        payload: Value,
2758        body_bytes: usize,
2759        identity_hint: Option<AgentIdentity>,
2760    ) -> Result<PreparedRequest, PipelineError> {
2761        if self.chat_preparation_can_run_inline(body_bytes)
2762            // Without a trusted hint, a configured validator may perform
2763            // synchronous shared-revocation I/O even for a tiny request.
2764            && (identity_hint.is_some() || self.identity.is_none())
2765        {
2766            return self.prepare_chat(headers, payload, identity_hint);
2767        }
2768        let state = self.clone();
2769        let headers = headers.clone();
2770        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload, identity_hint))
2771            .await
2772            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?
2773    }
2774
2775    fn chat_preparation_can_run_inline(&self, body_bytes: usize) -> bool {
2776        // Above 16 KiB, CPU-bound gates can stall the reactor. Any token
2777        // budget can also call a synchronous state backend, regardless of size.
2778        body_bytes <= 16 * 1024
2779            && self.effective_budget().max_tokens.is_none()
2780            && self.config.principal_budget.is_none()
2781    }
2782
2783    pub(crate) async fn authenticate_chat_nonblocking(
2784        &self,
2785        headers: &HeaderMap,
2786    ) -> Result<AuthenticatedCaller, PipelineError> {
2787        if self.identity.is_none() {
2788            // Preserve all anonymous/header checks without queueing local work
2789            // behind durable filesystem writes in the shared blocking pool.
2790            let caller = self.authenticate(headers)?;
2791            caller.authorize(self, Some(&self.config.chat_scope))?;
2792            return Ok(caller);
2793        }
2794        let state = self.clone();
2795        let headers = headers.clone();
2796        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
2797            let caller = state.authenticate(&headers)?;
2798            caller.authorize(&state, Some(&state.config.chat_scope))?;
2799            Ok(caller)
2800        })
2801        .await
2802        .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?
2803    }
2804
2805    pub(crate) async fn prepare_authenticated_chat(
2806        &self,
2807        headers: &HeaderMap,
2808        payload: Value,
2809        body_bytes: usize,
2810        caller: AuthenticatedCaller,
2811    ) -> Result<PreparedRequest, PipelineError> {
2812        if self.chat_preparation_can_run_inline(body_bytes) {
2813            // Authentication already finished. This rechecks only local
2814            // expiry/scope and retains principal binding during preparation.
2815            let identity = caller.authorize(self, Some(&self.config.chat_scope))?;
2816            return self.prepare_chat_with_caller(headers, payload, Some(identity), Some(&caller));
2817        }
2818        let state = self.clone();
2819        let headers = headers.clone();
2820        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
2821            let identity = caller.authorize(&state, Some(&state.config.chat_scope))?;
2822            state.prepare_chat_with_caller(&headers, payload, Some(identity), Some(&caller))
2823        })
2824        .await
2825        .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?
2826    }
2827
2828    /// Prepare a request and wait until its audit record is durably captured
2829    /// before the provider can observe it.
2830    pub async fn prepare_chat_durable(
2831        &self,
2832        headers: &HeaderMap,
2833        payload: Value,
2834    ) -> Result<PreparedRequest, PipelineError> {
2835        if let Ok(id) = session_id(headers) {
2836            if let Some(session) = self.sessions.get(&id) {
2837                session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
2838                if session.capture_failed() {
2839                    return Err(PipelineError::unavailable(
2840                        "session audit capture is incomplete".to_owned(),
2841                    ));
2842                }
2843            }
2844        }
2845        let state = self.clone();
2846        let headers = headers.clone();
2847        let mut prepared = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload, None))
2848            .await
2849            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)??;
2850        prepared.session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
2851        if prepared.session.capture_failed() {
2852            let error = PipelineError::unavailable(
2853                "request audit capture failed before provider dispatch".to_owned(),
2854            );
2855            self.abandon_prepared(&mut prepared, StopReason::Other, &error.to_string());
2856            return Err(error);
2857        }
2858        if prepared.session.loop_state.state() == BreakerState::Open {
2859            let action = prepared.session.loop_state.action();
2860            // Latch the terminal enforcement actions only: Inject's
2861            // refusal here is genuinely transient (the client retry
2862            // re-enters prepare_chat, which injects the corrective
2863            // message and proceeds), so it must not make the id
2864            // terminal. Without this latch, a breaker trip delivered
2865            // from THIS site (opened during the audit wait) left the
2866            // id recyclable with a fresh breaker.
2867            if matches!(action, BreakerAction::Abort | BreakerAction::Reject) {
2868                prepared.session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::LoopDetected);
2869            }
2870            let error = match action {
2871                BreakerAction::Abort => {
2872                    PipelineError::Abort("semantic loop circuit breaker opened during audit".to_owned())
2873                }
2874                _ => PipelineError::blocked(
2875                    "semantic loop circuit breaker opened during audit; retry required".to_owned(),
2876                ),
2877            };
2878            self.abandon_prepared(&mut prepared, StopReason::LoopDetected, &error.to_string());
2879            return Err(error);
2880        }
2881        Ok(prepared)
2882    }
2883
2884    /// Close out a prepared request that will never reach `forward_chat`.
2885    ///
2886    /// `prepare_chat` journals a non-terminal [`crate::worker::ResponseAttempt`]
2887    /// with its admission record; the matching terminal record normally comes
2888    /// from `forward_chat`'s failure path or the response relay. A caller that
2889    /// abandons the request after admission must submit the terminal failure
2890    /// record itself — otherwise the journal ends with a dangling non-terminal
2891    /// attempt and a later crash-recovery scan quarantines the whole session
2892    /// over a request the client already saw fail.
2893    fn abandon_prepared(&self, prepared: &mut PreparedRequest, stop_reason: StopReason, reason: &str) {
2894        // Every abandon site runs strictly before upstream dispatch
2895        // (durable-capture failure, breaker-open, marker-write failure),
2896        // so the admission token debit provably bought no LLM work —
2897        // refund it exactly once on BOTH ledgers (the guard zeroes
2898        // itself, so a hypothetical double abandon — or the guard's own
2899        // Drop — cannot double-refund).
2900        prepared.admission_debit.refund_now();
2901        let Some(permit) = prepared.capture_guard.take_permit() else {
2902            // Defensive: no permit means the guard's Drop is the only
2903            // resolver left. Leave it armed so the terminal record lands
2904            // through the plain worker queue on drop.
2905            return;
2906        };
2907        // Take the marker so the terminal job below retires it too.
2908        let response_marker = prepared.capture_guard.defuse();
2909        let mut job = self.failure_job(
2910            Arc::clone(&prepared.session),
2911            prepared.identity.clone(),
2912            stop_reason,
2913            reason.to_owned(),
2914        );
2915        job.response_marker = response_marker;
2916        job.response_attempt = Some(crate::worker::ResponseAttempt {
2917            id: prepared.capture_guard.attempt_id().to_owned(),
2918            terminal: true,
2919        });
2920        // Every sibling refusal path (`forward_chat` Err, both
2921        // `chat_completions` arms, the guard's own Drop) fails the
2922        // session closed on submit failure so the dangling attempt
2923        // cannot silently strand. Mirror that here: without it,
2924        // defusing the guard first would lose the safety net this whole
2925        // guard machinery exists to provide.
2926        if permit.submit(&self.worker, job).is_err() {
2927            prepared.session.mark_capture_failed();
2928        }
2929    }
2930
2931    /// Forward a prepared OpenAI-compatible request to the configured provider.
2932    ///
2933    /// For the `openai` dialect the compressed payload is sent as is to
2934    /// `upstream_url` + `upstream_chat_path` and the response is relayed
2935    /// unchanged. For `anthropic` and `gemini` the payload is translated
2936    /// into the native request first (`n` choices become `n` concurrent
2937    /// Anthropic requests, or Gemini's `candidateCount`), and the native
2938    /// response is translated back into the OpenAI shape before the
2939    /// relay sees it, so capture and accounting run one parser.
2940    pub async fn forward_chat(
2941        &self,
2942        mut request: PreparedRequest,
2943    ) -> Result<ForwardedResponse, PipelineError> {
2944        // Digest the request payload so the in-flight marker can be
2945        // matched to the observed response bytes at recovery time.
2946        //
2947        // `serde_json::to_vec` on a `Value` is effectively infallible
2948        // (Value can only carry JSON-serialisable data), but we handle
2949        // the theoretical error path anyway: falling back to
2950        // `sha256(b"")` — the well-known empty digest — would make
2951        // every concurrent failed serialisation collide on the same
2952        // request_digest, silently violating the marker's
2953        // one-to-one-with-request invariant. Fall back to a
2954        // session-id-derived digest so a hypothetical failure at
2955        // least keeps distinct sessions distinct.
2956        // Serialize the payload exactly ONCE — the
2957        // bytes feed both the request digest and (for the OpenAI
2958        // dialect) the upstream body.
2959        let payload_bytes = match serde_json::to_vec(&request.payload) {
2960            Ok(bytes) => Some(bytes),
2961            Err(error) => {
2962                tracing::error!(
2963                    %error,
2964                    session = %request.session.id,
2965                    "failed to serialise chat payload for request digest; falling back to session-derived digest"
2966                );
2967                None
2968            }
2969        };
2970        let request_digest = match &payload_bytes {
2971            Some(bytes) => pv_core::digest::sha256_hex(bytes),
2972            None => pv_core::digest::sha256_hex(request.session.id.as_bytes()),
2973        };
2974        // Plan the upstream request(s) before the marker write: a
2975        // request the native adapter cannot express is a client error
2976        // that must be refused before any upstream I/O, with the
2977        // admission debit refunded like every other pre-dispatch abort.
2978        let plan = match self.plan_upstream(
2979            &request.payload,
2980            payload_bytes,
2981            request.client_authorization.take(),
2982            std::mem::take(&mut request.upstream_passthrough_headers),
2983        ) {
2984            Ok(plan) => plan,
2985            Err(client_error) => {
2986                self.abandon_prepared(&mut request, StopReason::Other, &client_error.to_string());
2987                return Err(client_error);
2988            }
2989        };
2990        // Cancellation fix: arm the guard with the marker id
2991        // BEFORE the awaited disk write. If the client disconnects
2992        // between here and the write completing, `spawn_blocking` still
2993        // runs the write to durability; the guard's terminal-failure
2994        // job then retires the marker via `clear_response_marker`,
2995        // which now treats an absent file as success — so a never-written
2996        // marker is a clean no-op and a written one is deleted. Under
2997        // the previous ordering, cancellation between the write and
2998        // `set_marker` left the marker stranded, poisoning quarantine
2999        // sets and (across restart) bricking receipts.
3000        let attempt_id = crate::worker::reserve_response_attempt_id();
3001        request.capture_guard.set_marker(attempt_id.clone());
3002        if let Err(error) = crate::worker::write_response_marker(
3003            std::path::Path::new(&self.config.atif_spool_dir),
3004            &self.journal_key,
3005            &request.session.id,
3006            &attempt_id,
3007            request_digest,
3008        )
3009        .await
3010        {
3011            // Fail closed: the marker is what lets a restart-time scan
3012            // (`inflight_response_sessions`) quarantine sessions whose
3013            // provider response may have been observed but never
3014            // captured. Dispatching without it would silently drop that
3015            // crash-durability guarantee, so refuse before upstream I/O
3016            // — terminating the journaled response attempt like every
3017            // other post-admission abort (see `abandon_prepared`).
3018            tracing::warn!(
3019                %error,
3020                session = %request.session.id,
3021                "could not write in-flight response marker; refusing upstream dispatch"
3022            );
3023            let client_error =
3024                PipelineError::unavailable("in-flight response marker could not be persisted".to_owned());
3025            self.abandon_prepared(&mut request, StopReason::Other, &client_error.to_string());
3026            return Err(client_error);
3027        }
3028        let PreparedRequest {
3029            session,
3030            identity,
3031            lease,
3032            capture_guard,
3033            mut admission_debit,
3034            ..
3035        } = request;
3036        let UpstreamPlan {
3037            url,
3038            bodies,
3039            headers,
3040            auth,
3041            translation,
3042        } = plan;
3043        // Upstream latency (time-to-response-headers)
3044        // and error counters — the two failure-mode-table rows the
3045        // review marked "logs only, no metric".
3046        let upstream_started = Instant::now();
3047        // Last pre-dispatch point: from here the request may reach the
3048        // provider, so the admission debit stands (the `is_connect` arm
3049        // of `upstream_send_failed` refunds the one provably-not-sent
3050        // case). Disarming here also ends the cancellation-refund
3051        // window — a disconnect during `send()` keeps the debit,
3052        // deliberately.
3053        let debited_tokens = admission_debit.take_for_dispatch();
3054        let sends = bodies.into_iter().map(|body| {
3055            let mut upstream_request = self
3056                .client
3057                .post(&url)
3058                .header(reqwest::header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json")
3059                .body(body);
3060            for (name, value) in &headers {
3061                upstream_request = upstream_request.header(name.clone(), value.clone());
3062            }
3063            if let Some((name, value)) = &auth {
3064                upstream_request = upstream_request.header(name.clone(), value.clone());
3065            }
3066            upstream_request.send()
3067        });
3068        // Every choice of a fan-out is in flight at once; the slowest
3069        // one bounds the time to headers.
3070        let results = futures::future::join_all(sends).await;
3071        self.hot_metrics
3072            .upstream_latency_histogram
3073            .observe_us(elapsed_us(upstream_started));
3074        let mut responses = Vec::with_capacity(results.len());
3075        let mut first_error = None;
3076        for result in results {
3077            match result {
3078                Ok(response) => responses.push(response),
3079                Err(error) => {
3080                    if first_error.is_none() {
3081                        first_error = Some(error);
3082                    }
3083                }
3084            }
3085        }
3086        if let Some(error) = first_error {
3087            // Sibling responses of a fan-out drop here, which cancels
3088            // them: a request that only partly reached the provider is
3089            // reported as one upstream failure. A sibling that answered
3090            // proves the provider saw (and may bill) this request, so a
3091            // connect failure on another sibling must not refund it.
3092            let reached_provider = !responses.is_empty();
3093            drop(responses);
3094            return Err(self.upstream_send_failed(
3095                error,
3096                session,
3097                identity,
3098                capture_guard,
3099                debited_tokens,
3100                reached_provider,
3101            ));
3102        }
3103        let Some(translation) = translation else {
3104            let Some(response) = responses.pop() else {
3105                // Unreachable by construction (the OpenAI plan carries
3106                // exactly one body); keep the guard armed so its drop
3107                // retires the marker and closes the attempt.
3108                drop(capture_guard);
3109                self.enqueue_failure(
3110                    session,
3111                    identity,
3112                    StopReason::Other,
3113                    "upstream_no_response".to_owned(),
3114                )?;
3115                return Err(PipelineError::upstream("no upstream response".to_owned()));
3116            };
3117            if response.status().is_server_error() {
3118                #[allow(clippy::indexing_slicing)]
3119                {
3120                    self.hot_metrics.upstream_errors_counters[UpstreamErrorKind::Http5xx.index()].inc();
3121                }
3122            }
3123            return Ok(ForwardedResponse {
3124                status: response.status(),
3125                headers: response.headers().clone(),
3126                body: response
3127                    .bytes_stream()
3128                    .map(|chunk| chunk.map_err(std::io::Error::other))
3129                    .boxed(),
3130                billed_prompt_tokens: debited_tokens,
3131                lease,
3132                capture_guard,
3133            });
3134        };
3135        Ok(self
3136            .translate_native_responses(translation, responses, debited_tokens, lease, capture_guard)
3137            .await)
3138    }
3139
3140    /// Build the upstream request(s) for one prepared payload: URL,
3141    /// bodies (one per Anthropic choice, otherwise exactly one), extra
3142    /// headers, the credential header, and — for the native dialects —
3143    /// how to translate the answer back.
3144    fn plan_upstream(
3145        &self,
3146        payload: &Value,
3147        payload_bytes: Option<Vec<u8>>,
3148        client_authorization: Option<HeaderValue>,
3149        upstream_passthrough_headers: Vec<(HeaderName, HeaderValue)>,
3150    ) -> Result<UpstreamPlan, PipelineError> {
3151        use crate::provider::{self, ProviderKind};
3152        let base = self.config.upstream_url.trim_end_matches('/');
3153        let auth = self.auth_for_dispatch(client_authorization)?;
3154        match self.provider {
3155            ProviderKind::OpenAi => {
3156                let body = match payload_bytes {
3157                    Some(bytes) => bytes,
3158                    // Reuse the digest's serialization for the upstream
3159                    // body; the theoretical to_vec failure above gets a
3160                    // second attempt here.
3161                    None => serde_json::to_vec(payload).map_err(|error| {
3162                        PipelineError::bad_request(format!("chat payload is not serializable: {error}"))
3163                    })?,
3164                };
3165                Ok(UpstreamPlan {
3166                    url: format!("{base}{}", self.config.upstream_chat_path),
3167                    bodies: vec![body],
3168                    // Allowlisted provider-semantic headers (org/project/
3169                    // beta) relay verbatim so org-scoped keys and
3170                    // beta-gated features keep working through the proxy.
3171                    headers: upstream_passthrough_headers,
3172                    auth,
3173                    translation: None,
3174                })
3175            }
3176            ProviderKind::Anthropic => {
3177                let body =
3178                    provider::anthropic::build_request(payload, self.config.provider_default_max_tokens)
3179                        .map_err(|reason| {
3180                            PipelineError::bad_request(format!(
3181                                "request cannot be expressed in the Anthropic Messages API: {reason}"
3182                            ))
3183                        })?;
3184                let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&body).map_err(|error| {
3185                    PipelineError::bad_request(format!("translated request is not serializable: {error}"))
3186                })?;
3187                let choices = provider::choice_count(payload).map_err(PipelineError::bad_request)?;
3188                let count = usize::try_from(choices).unwrap_or(1);
3189                Ok(UpstreamPlan {
3190                    url: format!("{base}{}", provider::anthropic::MESSAGES_PATH),
3191                    bodies: vec![bytes; count],
3192                    headers: vec![(
3193                        HeaderName::from_static("anthropic-version"),
3194                        HeaderValue::from_static(provider::ANTHROPIC_VERSION),
3195                    )],
3196                    auth,
3197                    translation: Some(Translation {
3198                        dialect: NativeDialect::Anthropic,
3199                        stream: provider::wants_stream(payload),
3200                        include_usage: provider::wants_usage_chunk(payload),
3201                        id: provider::fresh_completion_id(),
3202                        model: payload
3203                            .get("model")
3204                            .and_then(Value::as_str)
3205                            .unwrap_or_default()
3206                            .to_owned(),
3207                        created: provider::created_now(),
3208                    }),
3209                })
3210            }
3211            ProviderKind::Gemini => {
3212                let model = payload
3213                    .get("model")
3214                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
3215                    .filter(|model| !model.is_empty())
3216                    .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("model is required".to_owned()))?;
3217                let stream = provider::wants_stream(payload);
3218                let path =
3219                    provider::gemini::request_path(model, stream).map_err(PipelineError::bad_request)?;
3220                let body = provider::gemini::build_request(payload).map_err(|reason| {
3221                    PipelineError::bad_request(format!(
3222                        "request cannot be expressed in the Gemini generateContent API: {reason}"
3223                    ))
3224                })?;
3225                let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&body).map_err(|error| {
3226                    PipelineError::bad_request(format!("translated request is not serializable: {error}"))
3227                })?;
3228                Ok(UpstreamPlan {
3229                    url: format!("{base}{path}"),
3230                    bodies: vec![bytes],
3231                    headers: Vec::new(),
3232                    auth,
3233                    translation: Some(Translation {
3234                        dialect: NativeDialect::Gemini,
3235                        stream,
3236                        include_usage: provider::wants_usage_chunk(payload),
3237                        id: provider::fresh_completion_id(),
3238                        model: model.to_owned(),
3239                        created: provider::created_now(),
3240                    }),
3241                })
3242            }
3243        }
3244    }
3245
3246    /// The credential header for one dispatch: the static key resolved
3247    /// at boot, else (passthrough mode) the client's own credential.
3248    /// For a native dialect the client's bearer token travels under the
3249    /// provider's key header without the `Bearer` scheme, which is what
3250    /// those APIs read.
3251    fn auth_for_dispatch(
3252        &self,
3253        client_authorization: Option<HeaderValue>,
3254    ) -> Result<Option<(HeaderName, HeaderValue)>, PipelineError> {
3255        if let Some(auth) = &self.upstream_auth {
3256            return Ok(Some(auth.clone()));
3257        }
3258        let Some(client) = client_authorization else {
3259            return Ok(None);
3260        };
3261        // Passthrough mode: the client's own credential travels to the
3262        // upstream. `validate()` guarantees this is mutually exclusive
3263        // with static keys and with NHI identity enforcement.
3264        let Some(header) = self.provider.native_auth_header() else {
3265            return Ok(Some((axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION, client)));
3266        };
3267        let raw = client
3268            .to_str()
3269            .map_err(|_| PipelineError::bad_request("authorization header is not valid text".to_owned()))?;
3270        let token = raw
3271            .strip_prefix("Bearer ")
3272            .or_else(|| raw.strip_prefix("bearer "))
3273            .unwrap_or(raw)
3274            .trim();
3275        let mut value = HeaderValue::from_str(token).map_err(|_| {
3276            PipelineError::bad_request("authorization token is not a valid header value".to_owned())
3277        })?;
3278        value.set_sensitive(true);
3279        Ok(Some((HeaderName::from_static(header), value)))
3280    }
3281
3282    /// Turn the native responses of one request into an OpenAI-shaped
3283    /// [`ForwardedResponse`]: a non-success status fails the whole
3284    /// request with that status and the OpenAI error envelope; a
3285    /// streaming request becomes one merged `chat.completion.chunk`
3286    /// stream; a buffered request becomes one `chat.completion` object
3287    /// (choices merged across the fan-out, usage summed).
3288    async fn translate_native_responses(
3289        &self,
3290        translation: Translation,
3291        mut responses: Vec<reqwest::Response>,
3292        debited_tokens: u64,
