3293        lease: SessionLease,
3294        capture_guard: ResponseCaptureGuard,
3295    ) -> ForwardedResponse {
3296        use crate::provider::{self, StreamTranslator};
3297        let Translation {
3298            dialect,
3299            stream,
3300            include_usage,
3301            id,
3302            model,
3303            created,
3304        } = translation;
3305        let provider_kind = match dialect {
3306            NativeDialect::Anthropic => provider::ProviderKind::Anthropic,
3307            NativeDialect::Gemini => provider::ProviderKind::Gemini,
3308        };
3309        if let Some(position) = responses
3310            .iter()
3311            .position(|response| !response.status().is_success())
3312        {
3313            let failed = responses.swap_remove(position);
3314            // Dropping the siblings cancels them.
3315            drop(responses);
3316            let status = failed.status();
3317            if status.is_server_error() {
3318                #[allow(clippy::indexing_slicing)]
3319                {
3320                    self.hot_metrics.upstream_errors_counters[UpstreamErrorKind::Http5xx.index()].inc();
3321                }
3322            }
3323            let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
3324            headers.insert(
3325                axum::http::header::CONTENT_TYPE,
3326                HeaderValue::from_static("application/json"),
3327            );
3328            if let Some(retry_after) = failed.headers().get(axum::http::header::RETRY_AFTER) {
3329                headers.insert(axum::http::header::RETRY_AFTER, retry_after.clone());
3330            }
3331            // A failed error-body read still answers with the status;
3332            // the envelope then carries a generic message.
3333            let body = read_bounded(failed, MAX_ERROR_BODY_BYTES)
3334                .await
3335                .unwrap_or_default();
3336            let envelope = provider::openai_error_envelope(provider_kind, status.as_u16(), &body);
3337            return ForwardedResponse {
3338                status,
3339                headers,
3340                body: futures::stream::once(async move { Ok(Bytes::from(envelope.to_string())) }).boxed(),
3341                billed_prompt_tokens: debited_tokens,
3342                lease,
3343                capture_guard,
3344            };
3345        }
3346        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
3347        let body: futures::stream::BoxStream<'static, Result<Bytes, std::io::Error>> = if stream {
3348            headers.insert(
3349                axum::http::header::CONTENT_TYPE,
3350                HeaderValue::from_static("text/event-stream"),
3351            );
3352            let streams = responses
3353                .into_iter()
3354                .enumerate()
3355                .map(|(position, response)| {
3356                    let index = u64::try_from(position).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
3357                    let translator: Box<dyn StreamTranslator> = match dialect {
3358                        NativeDialect::Anthropic => {
3359                            Box::new(provider::anthropic::AnthropicStreamTranslator::new(
3360                                index,
3361                                id.clone(),
3362                                model.clone(),
3363                                created,
3364                            ))
3365                        }
3366                        NativeDialect::Gemini => Box::new(provider::gemini::GeminiStreamTranslator::new(
3367                            id.clone(),
3368                            model.clone(),
3369                            created,
3370                        )),
3371                    };
3372                    provider::TranslatingStream::new(
3373                        response
3374                            .bytes_stream()
3375                            .map(|chunk| chunk.map_err(std::io::Error::other))
3376                            .boxed(),
3377                        translator,
3378                    )
3379                })
3380                .collect();
3381            provider::MergedOpenAiStream::new(streams, include_usage, id, model, created).boxed()
3382        } else {
3383            headers.insert(
3384                axum::http::header::CONTENT_TYPE,
3385                HeaderValue::from_static("application/json"),
3386            );
3387            futures::stream::once(async move {
3388                let mut completions = Vec::with_capacity(responses.len());
3389                for (position, response) in responses.into_iter().enumerate() {
3390                    let bytes = read_bounded(response, MAX_NATIVE_BODY_BYTES).await?;
3391                    let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|error| {
3392                        std::io::Error::other(format!("upstream body is not JSON: {error}"))
3393                    })?;
3394                    let index = u64::try_from(position).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
3395                    let completion = match dialect {
3396                        NativeDialect::Anthropic => {
3397                            provider::anthropic::translate_message(&value, &id, created, index)
3398                        }
3399                        NativeDialect::Gemini => {
3400                            provider::gemini::translate_response(&value, &id, created, &model)
3401                        }
3402                    }
3403                    .map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
3404                    completions.push(completion);
3405                }
3406                let merged = if completions.len() == 1 {
3407                    completions
3408                        .pop()
3409                        .ok_or_else(|| std::io::Error::other("no upstream completion"))?
3410                } else {
3411                    provider::merge_completions(completions).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?
3412                };
3413                Ok(Bytes::from(merged.to_string()))
3414            })
3415            .boxed()
3416        };
3417        ForwardedResponse {
3418            status: StatusCode::OK,
3419            headers,
3420            body,
3421            billed_prompt_tokens: debited_tokens,
3422            lease,
3423            capture_guard,
3424        }
3425    }
3426
3427    /// Account for and report one failed upstream `send()`: metrics,
3428    /// the connect-failure refund, redacted logging, and the terminal
3429    /// failure capture that retires the in-flight marker.
3430    fn upstream_send_failed(
3431        &self,
3432        error: reqwest::Error,
3433        session: Arc<Session>,
3434        identity: AgentIdentity,
3435        mut capture_guard: ResponseCaptureGuard,
3436        debited_tokens: u64,
3437        reached_provider: bool,
3438    ) -> PipelineError {
3439        let kind = UpstreamErrorKind::from_reqwest(&error);
3440        #[allow(clippy::indexing_slicing)]
3441        {
3442            self.hot_metrics.upstream_errors_counters[kind.index()].inc();
3443        }
3444        // `reqwest::Error::Display` embeds the
3445        // request URL (see the redaction rule at
3446        // routes.rs::read_limited_tool_response — the same
3447        // rule applies here). `%error` on `reqwest::Error`
3448        // would render `error sending request for url
3449        // (https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions): dns
3450        // error: ...` into every OTLP sink. In multi-tenant
3451        // / air-gapped deployments the upstream URL is not
3452        // something the operator wants leaked to the
3453        // customer's SIEM (it may be an internal LiteLLM
3454        // router, an on-prem Azure resource name, a
3455        // per-tenant model deployment path). Log the
3456        // stable classifier + reqwest's structured
3457        // predicates only.
3458        //
3459        // Also propagate the classifier — NOT
3460        // `error.to_string()` — into the persisted failure
3461        // event so the URL doesn't land in the on-disk
3462        // journal or in the Bridge-published failure
3463        // record either.
3464        let client_reason = classify_upstream_error(&error);
3465        // `is_connect()` proves the request never left this
3466        // process (TCP/TLS establishment failed) — the admission
3467        // token debit bought no LLM work; refund it on BOTH
3468        // ledgers, in the same order they were spent. Any other
3469        // send error (timeout, body, reset mid-response) may have
3470        // reached the provider, so the debit conservatively
3471        // stands. Best-effort; a store blip must never turn a
3472        // 502 into a 5xx cascade.
3473        // A sibling of an Anthropic fan-out that got an answer counts
3474        // as "may have reached the provider" for the whole request.
3475        if error.is_connect() && !reached_provider {
3476            ActionBudget::new(self.store.as_ref(), &session.id, &self.effective_budget())
3477                .refund_tokens(debited_tokens);
3478            if let Some(spec) = self.config.principal_budget.as_ref() {
3479                let principal_id = principal_id_for_budget(&identity);
3480                ActionBudget::for_principal(self.store.as_ref(), &principal_id, spec)
3481                    .refund_tokens(debited_tokens);
3482            }
3483        }
3484        tracing::warn!(
3485            session = %session.id,
3486            category = client_reason,
3487            error.status = ?error.status(),
3488            error.is_timeout = error.is_timeout(),
3489            error.is_connect = error.is_connect(),
3490            error.is_body = error.is_body(),
3491            "upstream forwarding failed"
3492        );
3493        // Route timeouts (upstream accepted the connection but
3494        // never finished responding) to 504 Gateway Timeout
3495        // instead of the generic 502 Bad Gateway. Operator
3496        // dashboards keying off HTTP status can now distinguish
3497        // "provider slow" (504) from "provider unreachable"
3498        // (502) without joining against the metric label.
3499        let client_error = if error.is_timeout() {
3500            PipelineError::upstream_timeout(client_reason.to_owned())
3501        } else {
3502            PipelineError::upstream(client_reason.to_owned())
3503        };
3504        let persisted_reason = format!("upstream_{client_reason}");
3505        if let Some(permit) = capture_guard.take_permit() {
3506            let (response_marker, response_attempt_id) = capture_guard.disarm();
3507            let capture_session = Arc::clone(&session);
3508            let mut job = self.failure_job(session, identity, StopReason::Other, persisted_reason);
3509            job.response_marker = response_marker;
3510            job.response_attempt = Some(crate::worker::ResponseAttempt {
3511                id: response_attempt_id,
3512                terminal: true,
3513            });
3514            // This terminal failure capture is the only thing that
3515            // retires the durable in-flight marker and the
3516            // journalled response attempt. If the shard is full,
3517            // fail the session closed instead of dropping the
3518            // capture silently: otherwise the marker strands, no
3519            // terminal response event exists, and the session can
3520            // still close "cleanly" with a receipt over an
3521            // unresolved response attempt.
3522            if permit.submit(&self.worker, job).is_err() {
3523                capture_session.mark_capture_failed();
3524            }
3525        } else {
3526            // Defensive (a permit always exists here): keep the
3527            // guard armed so its drop retires the marker and
3528            // closes the attempt through the plain worker queue.
3529            drop(capture_guard);
3530            if let Err(error) = self.enqueue_failure(session, identity, StopReason::Other, persisted_reason) {
3531                return error;
3532            }
3533        }
3534        client_error
3535    }
3536
3537    /// Intercept one MCP JSON-RPC tool call, emit its OCSF verdict
3538    /// asynchronously, and return the immediate authorization decision.
3539    pub fn intercept_tool(&self, headers: &HeaderMap, raw: &[u8]) -> Result<ToolVerdict, PipelineError> {
3540        self.intercept_tool_with_session(headers, raw)
3541            .map(|(verdict, _session)| verdict)
3542    }
3543
3544    /// Cheap pre-parse identity gate for the tool paths. When identity
3545    /// is REQUIRED, a request with no Authorization header can never be
3546    /// admitted — refuse before `parse_tool_call` /
3547    /// `ToolExecution::from_request` run their duplicate-key scan, JSON
3548    /// parse, canonicalization, and hashing. The chat path resolves
3549    /// identity before any body processing; the tool paths parsed FIRST
3550    /// (the required scope depends on the parsed tool name), handing an
3551    /// unauthenticated flood the expensive pre-auth work — bounded by
3552    /// the MCP admission cap, but blocking-pool cycles anonymous
3553    /// garbage shouldn't get. A present-but-garbage bearer still costs
3554    /// one signature verification before the parse (the full
3555    /// scope-aware `resolve_identity` still runs afterwards) — the same
3556    /// cost order as the chat path.
3557    pub(crate) fn refuse_unauthenticated_tool_call(&self, headers: &HeaderMap) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
3558        if !self.config.require_identity {
3559            return Ok(());
3560        }
3561        if single_header(headers, "authorization")?.is_none() {
3562            // Mirrors resolve_identity's (None, _) require_identity arm,
3563            // including the identity-rejection audit event.
3564            let session_id = session_id(headers)?;
3565            let error = PipelineError::Unauthorized("missing bearer token".to_owned());
3566            self.enqueue_transient_failure(&session_id, StopReason::IdentityRejected, error.to_string())?;
3567            return Err(error);
3568        }
3569        Ok(())
3570    }
3571
3572    /// Core of [`Self::intercept_tool`] that also returns the bound session.
3573    ///
3574    /// Callers that must await audit durability need the same session the
3575    /// verdict was recorded under: re-deriving it from headers would mint a
3576    /// fresh random id for a header-less request (see [`session_id`]) and
3577    /// look up a session that does not exist.
3578    fn intercept_tool_with_session(
3579        &self,
3580        headers: &HeaderMap,
3581        raw: &[u8],
3582    ) -> Result<(ToolVerdict, Arc<Session>), PipelineError> {
3583        let caller = match self.authenticate(headers) {
3584            Ok(caller) => caller,
3585            Err(error) => {
3586                self.audit_authentication_error(headers, &error)?;
3587                return Err(error);
3588            }
3589        };
3590        self.intercept_tool_authenticated(headers, raw, &caller, None)
3591    }
3592
3593    fn intercept_tool_authenticated(
3594        &self,
3595        headers: &HeaderMap,
3596        raw: &[u8],
3597        caller: &AuthenticatedCaller,
3598        preflight_denial: Option<ToolVerdict>,
3599    ) -> Result<(ToolVerdict, Arc<Session>), PipelineError> {
3600        let session_id = session_id(headers)?;
3601        let workflow = workflow(headers, &self.config.default_workflow)?;
3602        self.require_signed_workflow_scope(workflow, Some(caller))?;
3603        let parsed_call = pv_sandbox::parse_tool_call(raw).ok();
3604        let required_scope = parsed_call
3605            .as_ref()
3606            .map(|request| tool_scope(&request.tool))
3607            .unwrap_or_else(|| TOOL_INVOKE_SCOPE.to_owned());
3608        let identity = match caller.authorize(self, Some(&required_scope)) {
3609            Ok(identity) => identity,
3610            Err(error) => {
3611                self.enqueue_transient_failure(&session_id, StopReason::IdentityRejected, error.to_string())?;
3612                return Err(error);
3613            }
3614        };
3615        // Tool interception must NOT resurrect a closed session: a
3616        // tool call extends an in-progress conversation, so silently
3617        // opening a fresh session under the same id would let the
3618        // client accumulate tool calls past a signed receipt boundary
3619        // (chain-of-custody split). Chat requests use `get_or_open`
3620        // (recycles closed ids); the strict `_no_reopen` variant here
3621        // makes the `is_closed()` check below fire.
3622        // Ask the semantic judge before taking the session's admission
3623        // lock: its wait (up to the adapter deadline) must not hold up a
3624        // concurrent session close or the session's other calls.
3625        // The session checks below run under the admission lock, after the
3626        // judge. Read them here first, so a call they will refuse (another
3627        // identity's session, a closed or broken session, an open loop
3628        // breaker) never reaches the judge: its arguments would leave for
3629        // a cloud judge and use judge capacity for nothing.
3630        let session_admits = self.sessions.get(&session_id).is_none_or(|existing| {
3631            validate_session_binding(&existing, workflow, &identity).is_ok()
3632                && !existing.is_closed()
3633                && !existing.capture_failed()
3634                && !(existing.loop_state.state() == BreakerState::Open
3635                    && matches!(
3636                        existing.loop_state.action(),
3637                        BreakerAction::Reject | BreakerAction::Abort
3638                    ))
3639        });
3640        let mut prejudged = self.prejudge(
3641            preflight_denial.is_none() && session_admits,
3642            parsed_call.as_ref(),
3643            workflow,
3644            raw,
3645            &session_id,
3646            &identity,
3647            caller,
3648        );
3649        let session =
3650            self.sessions
3651                .get_or_open_no_reopen(&session_id, workflow, &identity, &self.config.breaker);
3652        let admission = session.admission_guard();
3653        validate_session_binding(&session, workflow, &identity)?;
3654        caller.bind_session(&session)?;
3655        session.refresh_identity(&identity);
3656        if session.is_closed() {
3657            return Err(PipelineError::bad_request("session is already closed".to_owned()));
3658        }
3659        if session.capture_failed() {
3660            return Err(PipelineError::unavailable(
3661                "session audit capture is incomplete".to_owned(),
3662            ));
3663        }
3664        session.touch();
3665        // The semantic-loop breaker gates CHAT admission (both the
3666        // prepare arm above and the streaming re-check), but tool
3667        // execution ignored it entirely: a runaway agent whose chat is
3668        // already refused/aborted could keep firing otherwise-permitted
3669        // MCP tool calls on the same session until idle close — the
3670        // "stop the runaway agent" decision only stopped half the
3671        // agent. Gate the hard-stop actions here. `Inject` deliberately
3672        // passes: its remedy is a corrective CHAT message (the next
3673        // chat turn injects and resets the breaker); blocking tools
3674        // under Inject would strand tool-driven agents the operator
3675        // chose to steer rather than stop.
3676        if session.loop_state.state() == BreakerState::Open {
3677            match session.loop_state.action() {
3678                BreakerAction::Reject | BreakerAction::Abort => {
3679                    session.latch_enforcement(StopReason::LoopDetected);
3680                    return Err(PipelineError::blocked(
3681                        "semantic loop circuit breaker is open".to_owned(),
3682                    ));
3683                }
3684                _ => {}
3685            }
3686        }
3687        let worker_permit = self
3688            .worker
3689            .try_reserve(&session_id)
3690            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?;
3691        let mut budgeted_check = || -> (ToolVerdict, Option<pv_judge::JudgeRecord>) {
3692            let principal_binding = self
3693                .config
3694                .principal_budget
3695                .as_ref()
3696                .map(|spec| (principal_id_for_budget(&identity), spec));
3697            let principal_ref = principal_binding.as_ref().map(|(id, spec)| (id.as_str(), *spec));
3698            let (call, scored) = match prejudged.take() {
3699                Some(prejudged) => (
3700                    prejudged.call,
3701                    Some((prejudged.outcomes, prejudged.judge_us, prejudged.inputs)),
3702                ),
3703                None => match self.sandbox.precheck(raw) {
3704                    Ok(call) => (call, None),
3705                    Err(verdict) => return (verdict, None),
3706                },
3707            };
3708            // Untagged tools never reach the judge: same path as before.
3709            let judged = self
3710                .judge
3711                .as_ref()
3712                .and_then(|judge| judge.tasks_for(&call.request.tool).map(|tasks| (judge, tasks)));
3713            match judged {
3714                None if self.judge.is_none() && self.judge_configured_for(&call.request.tool) => {
3715                    // `[judge]` tags this tool but no judge is attached to
3716                    // this state: refuse rather than skip the review.
3717                    let reason = format!(
3718                        "tool {:?} is tagged for semantic review but the judge is not running",
3719                        call.request.tool
3720                    );
3721                    (
3722                        ToolVerdict::Blocked {
3723                            tool: call.request.tool.clone(),
3724                            stage: "policy",
3725                            policy: Some(pv_sandbox::JUDGE_POLICY_NAME.to_owned()),
3726                            denial_code: pv_sandbox::DenialCode::JudgeUnavailable,
3727                            response: pv_sandbox::rpc::denial_error(
3728                                call.request.id.as_ref(),
3729                                pv_sandbox::DenialCode::JudgeUnavailable,
3730                                &reason,
3731                            ),
3732                            reason,
3733                            elapsed_us: 0,
3734                        },
3735                        None,
3736                    )
3737                }
3738                None => (
3739                    self.sandbox
3740                        .admit(self.store.as_ref(), &session_id, principal_ref, call, None),
3741                    None,
3742                ),
3743                Some((judge, tasks)) => {
3744                    // Normally scored before the admission lock; only a
3745                    // call whose eligibility changed in between is scored here.
3746                    let (outcomes, judge_us, inputs) = scored.unwrap_or_else(|| {
3747                        self.run_judge(judge, tasks, &call, &session_id, &identity, caller)
3748                    });
3749                    let (verdict, record) = self.judged_admission(
3750                        judge,
3751                        tasks,
3752                        call,
3753                        outcomes,
3754                        judge_us,
3755                        inputs,
3756                        &session_id,
3757                        principal_ref,
3758                        &principal_id_for_budget(&identity),
3759                    );
3760                    (verdict, Some(record))
3761                }
3762            }
3763        };
3764        let (verdict, judge_record) = if let Some(denial) = preflight_denial {
3765            (denial, None)
3766        } else {
3767            match parsed_call.as_ref() {
3768                // `sandbox.check`'s budget gate spends before we return, so we must
3769                // veto workflow-mismatched consequential tools before it runs.
3770                Some(request)
3771                    if workflow == Workflow::Unsigned
3772                        && self
3773                            .config
3774                            .consequential_tools
3775                            .iter()
3776                            .any(|required| required == &request.tool) =>
3777                {
3778                    let reason = format!("tool {:?} requires a signed workflow", request.tool);
3779                    ToolVerdict::Blocked {
3780                        tool: request.tool.clone(),
3781                        stage: "policy",
3782                        // Built-in workflow gate, named so console-side
3783                        // attribution has a stable handle an operator can
3784                        // create a matching policy row for.
3785                        policy: Some("workflow.signed_required".to_owned()),
3786                        denial_code: pv_sandbox::DenialCode::PolicyDenied,
3787                        reason: reason.clone(),
3788                        response: pv_sandbox::rpc::denial_error(
3789                            request.id.as_ref(),
3790                            pv_sandbox::DenialCode::PolicyDenied,
3791                            &reason,
3792                        ),
3793                        elapsed_us: 0,
3794                    }
3795                    .into_unjudged()
3796                }
3797                // Same pre-spend veto for the intent map and the active
3798                // mission: a denied call must neither consume budget nor be
3799                // audited as allowed. This also covers verdict-only mode, where
3800                // no forwarding branch ever runs.
3801                Some(request) => {
3802                    match self
3803                        .pdp
3804                        .decide_for(&request.tool, pv_core::time::now_ms() / 1000, &caller.facts())
3805                    {
3806                        crate::authz::AuthzDecision::Deny { code, reason } => {
3807                            self.metrics
3808                                .counter(
3809                                    &format!("pv_pdp_denials_total{{code=\"{}\"}}", code.as_str()),
3810                                    "PDP denials by denial code",
3811                                )
3812                                .inc();
3813                            ToolVerdict::Blocked {
3814                                tool: request.tool.clone(),
3815                                stage: "policy",
3816                                policy: Some(pdp_policy_name(code).to_owned()),
3817                                denial_code: code,
3818                                response: pv_sandbox::rpc::denial_error(request.id.as_ref(), code, &reason),
3819                                reason,
3820                                elapsed_us: 0,
3821                            }
3822                            .into_unjudged()
3823                        }
3824                        crate::authz::AuthzDecision::Permit { .. } => budgeted_check(),
3825                    }
3826                }
3827                None => budgeted_check(),
3828            }
3829        };
3830        let (status, payload) = match &verdict {
3831            ToolVerdict::Allowed {
3832                tool,
3833                budget_remaining,
3834                elapsed_us,
3835                ..
3836            } => (
3837                StatusId::Success,
3838                serde_json::json!({
3839                    "tool": tool,
3840                    "allowed": true,
3841                    "budget_remaining": (*budget_remaining != u64::MAX)
3842                        .then_some(*budget_remaining),
3843                    "budget_unlimited": *budget_remaining == u64::MAX,
3844                    "decision_us": elapsed_us,
3845                }),
3846            ),
3847            ToolVerdict::Blocked {
3848                tool,
3849                stage,
3850                policy,
3851                denial_code,
3852                reason,
3853                elapsed_us,
3854                ..
3855            } => (
3856                StatusId::Failure,
3857                serde_json::json!({
3858                    "tool": tool,
3859                    "allowed": false,
3860                    "stage": stage,
3861                    // Present only for named policy-chain denials —
3862                    // consumed by console-sync as the per-policy
3863                    // attribution (`payload.policy` → ingest policyName).
3864                    "policy": policy,
3865                    "denial_code": denial_code.as_str(),
3866                    "reason": reason,
3867                    "decision_us": elapsed_us,
3868                }),
3869            ),
3870            // `ToolVerdict` is `#[non_exhaustive]` (R56 SemVer
3871            // hardening) — a future variant (e.g. `Warn` for shadow-
3872            // mode, `Deferred` for async policy evaluation) must NOT
3873            // be silently forwarded to the tool server. Fail-closed:
3874            // emit a Blocked-shaped audit payload and log at ERROR
3875            // level so operators discover the unhandled variant
3876            // during rollout. Same defensive posture the sandbox's
3877            // own `is_allowed` uses (whitelist Allowed, treat
3878            // everything else as blocked).
3879            _ => {
3880                tracing::error!(
3881                    "unhandled ToolVerdict variant reached the harness matcher; \
3882                     failing closed. This is a bug — extend the match to handle the new variant."
3883                );
3884                (
3885                    StatusId::Failure,
3886                    serde_json::json!({
3887                        "tool": "<unknown>",
3888                        "allowed": false,
3889                        "stage": "unhandled_verdict",
3890                        "reason": "unhandled ToolVerdict variant; fail-closed",
3891                    }),
3892                )
3893            }
3894        };
3895        let mut payload = payload;
3896        if let Some(record) = &judge_record {
3897            if let (Some(map), Ok(value)) = (payload.as_object_mut(), serde_json::to_value(record)) {
3898                map.insert("judge".to_owned(), value);
3899            }
3900        }
3901        if let (
3902            Some(judge),
3903            ToolVerdict::Blocked {
3904                tool,
3905                stage,
3906                policy,
3907                denial_code,
3908                ..
3909            },
3910        ) = (&self.judge, &verdict)
3911        {
3912            // Parse and protocol refusals say nothing about intent, and a
3913            // tool name the operator never defined (no schema, no judge
3914            // tag) is caller-chosen text that must not reach the judge.
3915            // Only policy refusals (thresholds, WASM policies, allow
3916            // lists, the judge) say what an agent was refused. Schema and
3917            // budget refusals are free for a caller to provoke, and would
3918            // otherwise push a real refusal out of the judge's view.
3919            let known_tool = self.sandbox.has_schema(tool) || judge.tasks_for(tool).is_some();
3920            if *stage == "policy" && known_tool {
3921                let predicate = (policy.as_deref() == Some(pv_sandbox::JUDGE_POLICY_NAME))
3922                    .then(|| {
3923                        judge_record
3924                            .as_ref()
3925                            .and_then(|record| record.decision_predicate.clone())
3926                    })
3927                    .flatten();
3928                judge.record_denial(
3929                    &session_id,
3930                    &identity.instance_uid,
3931                    tool,
3932                    denial_code.as_str(),
3933                    predicate,
3934                );
3935            }
3936        }
3937        // Shadow mode: a would-be block is also its own `agent.judge`
3938        // event, so a SIEM can follow the rollout on that topic alone.
3939        if let Some(record) = judge_record
3940            .as_ref()
3941            .filter(|record| record.mode == pv_judge::JudgeMode::Shadow && record.would_block)
3942        {
3943            if let Some(hooks) = self.judge_hooks.read().clone() {
3944                hooks.emit(
3945                    &session,
3946                    serde_json::json!({
3947                        "kind": "shadow",
3948                        "tool": parsed_call.as_ref().map(|request| request.tool.clone()),
3949                        "record": record,
3950                    }),
3951                );
3952            }
3953        }
3954        let tool_call_id = parsed_call
3955            .as_ref()
3956            .and_then(|request| request.id.as_ref())
3957            .map(|value| value.as_str().map_or_else(|| value.to_string(), str::to_owned))
3958            .unwrap_or_else(pv_core::new_event_uid);
3959        let tool_calls = parsed_call.as_ref().map(|request| {
3960            vec![pv_atif::ToolCall {
3961                tool_call_id: tool_call_id.clone(),
3962                function_name: request.tool.clone(),
3963                arguments: request.arguments.clone(),
3964                // Same record as the event payload, so a retroactive
3965                // receipt over the ATIF file signs it too.
3966                extra: judge_record
3967                    .as_ref()
3968                    .and_then(|record| serde_json::to_value(record).ok())
3969                    .map(|record| serde_json::json!({ "judge": record })),
3970            }]
3971        });
3972        let observation = parsed_call.as_ref().map(|_| pv_atif::Observation {
3973            results: vec![pv_atif::ObservationResult {
3974                source_call_id: Some(tool_call_id),
3975                content: Some(Value::String(payload.to_string())),
3976                subagent_trajectory_ref: None,
3977                extra: None,
3978            }],
3979        });
3980        let stop_reason = match &verdict {
3981            ToolVerdict::Allowed { .. } => None,
3982            ToolVerdict::Blocked { stage: "budget", .. } => Some(StopReason::BudgetExceeded),
3983            ToolVerdict::Blocked { .. } => Some(StopReason::PolicyBlocked),
3984            // See the sibling `match &verdict` above for the rationale.
3985            // R58 correction: use `StopReason::Other` (the ships-with-
3986            // `#[serde(other)]` forward-compat sink documented for
3987            // exactly this "unrecognised upstream variant during
3988            // heterogeneous rolling upgrade" case) rather than
3989            // `PolicyBlocked`. Returning `PolicyBlocked` here would
3990            // contaminate policy-block counters / alerts / dashboards
3991            // with a HARNESS CODE BUG signal, and disagree with the
3992            // sibling `stage: "unhandled_verdict"` payload label that
3993            // is honestly synthetic. `Other` preserves fail-closed
3994            // semantics (non-None, terminates the turn, seals the
3995            // receipt) while telling the truth on the wire.
3996            _ => Some(StopReason::Other),
3997        };
3998        worker_permit.submit(WorkerJob {
3999            session: Arc::clone(&session),
4000            identity,
4001            class: EventClass::ToolCall,
4002            payload,
4003            text: String::from_utf8_lossy(raw).into_owned(),
4004            analyze_loop: false,
4005            status,
4006            stop_reason,
4007            native_stop_reason: None,
4008            metrics: EventMetrics::default(),
4009            cost_usd_micros: 0,
4010            prompt_token_correction: 0,
4011            atif: Some(AtifCapture {
4012                source: pv_atif::Source::Agent,
4013                message: Value::String("MCP tool authorization decision".to_owned()),
4014                reasoning_content: None,
4015                model_name: None,
4016                tool_calls,
4017                observation,
4018                llm_call_count: Some(0),
4019            }),
4020            response_marker: None,
4021            response_attempt: None,
4022        });
4023        drop(admission);
4024        Ok((verdict, session))
4025    }
4026
4027    /// Context integrity after compression: a standing rule whose text the
4028    /// original request carried but the compressed one no longer does is
4029    /// appended back as a system message, and the token count is
4030    /// recomputed so budgets and the receipt charge what is forwarded.
4031    /// Exact text, deterministic, no judge call on the chat path.
4032    fn repin_standing_rules(
4033        &self,
4034        original: &Value,
4035        mut outcome: pv_compress::CompressionOutcome,
4036    ) -> (pv_compress::CompressionOutcome, Vec<String>) {
4037        let Some(judge) = &self.judge else {
4038            return (outcome, Vec::new());
4039        };
4040        if !outcome.changed || judge.standing_rules().is_empty() {
4041            return (outcome, Vec::new());
4042        }
4043        let mut repinned = Vec::new();
4044        for rule in judge.standing_rules() {
4045            if !messages_contain(original, &rule.text) || messages_contain(&outcome.payload, &rule.text) {
4046                continue;
4047            }
4048            if let Some(messages) = outcome.payload.get_mut("messages").and_then(Value::as_array_mut) {
4049                // After the leading system messages, never at the end: the
4050                // caller's last message is what the audit record captures
4051                // as this turn, and what decides whether the model answers.
4052                let at = messages
4053                    .iter()
4054                    .take_while(|message| matches!(message["role"].as_str(), Some("system" | "developer")))
4055                    .count();
4056                messages.insert(at, serde_json::json!({"role": "system", "content": rule.text}));
4057                repinned.push(rule.id.clone());
4058            }
4059        }
4060        if !repinned.is_empty() {
4061            outcome.tokens_after = pv_core::tokens::approx_tokens_json(&outcome.payload);
4062            self.metrics
4063                .counter(
4064                    "pv_judge_rules_repinned_total",
4065                    "Standing rules pinned back after context compression cut them",
4066                )
4067                .add(u64::try_from(repinned.len()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
4068        }
4069        (outcome, repinned)
4070    }
4071
4072    /// Score a tagged tool's call before the session admission lock, when
4073    /// the call can reach the judge at all: no route-level refusal, not a
4074    /// consequential tool on the unsigned workflow, permitted by the local
4075    /// policy decision point, and schema-valid. Anything else returns
4076    /// `None` and is refused by the regular gates without a judge call.
4077    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
4078    fn prejudge(
4079        &self,
4080        eligible: bool,
4081        request: Option<&pv_sandbox::ToolCallRequest>,
4082        workflow: Workflow,
4083        raw: &[u8],
4084        session_id: &str,
4085        identity: &AgentIdentity,
4086        caller: &AuthenticatedCaller,
4087    ) -> Option<Prejudged> {
4088        let judge = self.judge.as_ref()?;
4089        let request = request.filter(|_| eligible)?;
4090        let tasks = judge.tasks_for(&request.tool)?;
4091        if workflow == Workflow::Unsigned
4092            && self
4093                .config
4094                .consequential_tools
4095                .iter()
4096                .any(|required| required == &request.tool)
4097        {
4098            return None;
4099        }
4100        let permitted = matches!(
4101            self.pdp
4102                .decide_for(&request.tool, pv_core::time::now_ms() / 1000, &caller.facts()),
4103            crate::authz::AuthzDecision::Permit { .. }
4104        );
4105        if !permitted {
4106            return None;
4107        }
4108        let call = self.sandbox.precheck(raw).ok()?;
4109        let (outcomes, judge_us, inputs) = self.run_judge(judge, tasks, &call, session_id, identity, caller);
4110        Some(Prejudged {
4111            call,
4112            outcomes,
4113            judge_us,
4114            inputs,
4115        })
4116    }
4117
4118    /// True when `[judge]` tags `tool`, whether or not a judge is attached.
4119    fn judge_configured_for(&self, tool: &str) -> bool {
4120        self.config
4121            .judge
4122            .as_ref()
4123            .is_some_and(|judge| judge.tools.iter().any(|tagged| tagged.tool == tool))
4124    }
4125
4126    /// Build the judge's context for a call and score it.
4127    fn run_judge(
4128        &self,
4129        judge: &Arc<crate::judge::JudgeService>,
4130        tasks: &[pv_judge::JudgeTask],
4131        call: &pv_sandbox::ValidatedCall,
4132        session_id: &str,
4133        identity: &AgentIdentity,
4134        caller: &AuthenticatedCaller,
4135    ) -> (Vec<pv_judge::JudgeOutcome>, u64, Option<pv_judge::SessionContext>) {
4136        let tool = &call.request.tool;
4137        let facts = caller.facts();
4138        let validated = caller.validated.as_ref();
4139        let context = judge.context(&crate::judge::ContextInputs {
4140            session_id,
4141            instance_uid: &identity.instance_uid,
4142            tool,
4143            intent: self.pdp.intent_for(tool),
4144            missions: self.pdp.applicable_missions(&facts),
4145            charter: &identity.charter.name,
4146            delegation_depth: validated.map_or(0, |validated| validated.total_delegation_depth),
4147            parents: validated.map(delegation_parents).unwrap_or_default(),
4148        });
4149        let inputs = judge.records_inputs().then(|| context.clone());
4150        let (outcomes, judge_us) = judge.evaluate_blocking(
4151            tasks,
4152            tool,
4153            &call.request.arguments,
4154            context,
4155            // Only a validated identity tells callers apart; anonymous
4156            // callers share just the overall limit.
4157            if validated.is_some() {
4158                &identity.instance_uid
4159            } else {
4160                ""
4161            },
4162        );
4163        (outcomes, judge_us, inputs)
4164    }
4165
4166    /// What a judged call's record keeps of the judge's request, with
4167    /// `record_inputs`: redacted as the rest of the audit record is (the
4168    /// arguments are kept as JSON text, which redaction paths could no
4169    /// longer reach), and left out when the arguments are longer than
4170    /// [`MAX_RECORDED_ARGUMENT_BYTES`].
4171    fn judge_inputs(
4172        &self,
4173        judge: &crate::judge::JudgeService,
4174        tool: &str,
4175        arguments: &Value,
4176        context: pv_judge::SessionContext,
4177    ) -> Option<pv_judge::JudgeInputs> {
4178        let mut arguments = arguments.clone();
4179        let mut context_value = serde_json::to_value(&context).ok()?;
4180        if let Some(engine) = &self.redaction {
4181            engine.redact_user_value(&mut arguments);
4182            engine.redact_value(&mut context_value);
4183        }
4184        let arguments_json = arguments.to_string();
4185        if arguments_json.len() > MAX_RECORDED_ARGUMENT_BYTES {
4186            judge.count_inputs_left_out();
4187            return None;
4188        }
4189        Some(pv_judge::JudgeInputs {
4190            tool: tool.to_owned(),
4191            arguments_json,
4192            context: serde_json::from_value(context_value).unwrap_or(context),
4193        })
4194    }
4195
4196    /// Run the policy and budget gates on a judged call with its verdicts.
4197    ///
4198    /// Shadow mode: the verdicts are recorded with what judge-aware
4199    /// policy would have done, and the call is admitted without them.
4200    /// Enforce mode: policy applies them. Under the pilot cap, a
4201    /// score-based block beyond the day's cap is recorded and let
4202    /// through; a judge failure, or any predicate in ask-by-default mode,
4203    /// always keeps its block.
4204    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
4205    fn judged_admission(
4206        &self,
4207        judge: &Arc<crate::judge::JudgeService>,
4208        tasks: &[pv_judge::JudgeTask],
4209        call: pv_sandbox::ValidatedCall,
4210        outcomes: Vec<pv_judge::JudgeOutcome>,
4211        judge_us: u64,
4212        inputs: Option<pv_judge::SessionContext>,
4213        session_id: &str,
4214        principal: Option<(&str, &pv_state::BudgetSpec)>,
4215        caller: &str,
4216    ) -> (ToolVerdict, pv_judge::JudgeRecord) {
4217        let tool = call.request.tool.clone();
4218        let inputs =
4219            inputs.and_then(|context| self.judge_inputs(judge, &tool, &call.request.arguments, context));
4220        let evidence = pv_sandbox::JudgeEvidence {
4221            outcomes,
4222            drift_tripped: judge.drift_tripped(),
4223        };
4224        let decision = self.sandbox.judge_decision(&evidence);
4225        let review_ids = judge.sample_reviews(&evidence.outcomes);
4226        let verdicts = evidence
4227            .outcomes
4228            .iter()
4229            .zip(tasks)
4230            .map(|(outcome, task)| {
4231                let mut record = pv_judge::OutcomeRecord::from_outcome(
4232                    outcome,
4233                    &task.adapter,
4234                    judge.adapter_kind(&task.adapter),
4235                );
4236                record.review_id = review_ids.get(outcome.predicate_id()).cloned();
4237                record
4238            })
4239            .collect();
4240        let with_verdicts = self.sandbox.policy_verdict(&call, Some(&evidence));
4241        let (policy_decision, deciding_policy) = match &with_verdicts {
4242            Some(ToolVerdict::Blocked {
4243                denial_code, policy, ..
4244            }) => (
4245                if *denial_code == pv_sandbox::DenialCode::ApprovalRequired {
4246                    pv_judge::JudgeAction::Ask
4247                } else {
4248                    pv_judge::JudgeAction::Deny
4249                },
4250                policy.clone(),
4251            ),
4252            _ => (pv_judge::JudgeAction::Allow, None),
4253        };
4254        let would_block = with_verdicts.is_some() && self.sandbox.policy_verdict(&call, None).is_none();
4255        let store = self.store.as_ref();
4256        let (verdict, enforced, pilot_cap_reached) = match judge.mode() {
4257            pv_judge::JudgeMode::Shadow => (
4258                self.sandbox.admit(store, session_id, principal, call, None),
4259                false,
4260                false,
4261            ),
4262            pv_judge::JudgeMode::Enforce => {
4263                let all_scored = evidence
4264                    .outcomes
4265                    .iter()
4266                    .all(|outcome| matches!(outcome, pv_judge::JudgeOutcome::Scored(_)));
4267                let verdict = self
4268                    .sandbox
4269                    .admit(store, session_id, principal, call.clone(), Some(&evidence));
4270                let judge_caused = !verdict.is_allowed() && would_block;
4271                // The pilot cap relaxes score-based blocks only: never a
4272                // judge failure, never the calibration drift gate.
4273                let relaxable = all_scored
4274                    && !evidence.outcomes.iter().any(|outcome| {
4275                        evidence
4276                            .drift_tripped
4277                            .iter()
4278                            .any(|id| id == outcome.predicate_id())
4279                    });
4280                if judge_caused && relaxable && !judge.pilot_admits_block(store, &tool, caller) {
4281                    // Nothing was spent by the refused admission (the
4282                    // policy gate runs before the budget gate).
4283                    (
4284                        self.sandbox.admit(store, session_id, principal, call, None),
4285                        false,
4286                        true,
4287                    )
4288                } else {
4289                    (verdict, true, false)
4290                }
4291            }
4292        };
4293        let mut record = pv_judge::JudgeRecord::new(judge.mode(), judge_us, verdicts, &decision, enforced);
4294        record.would_block = would_block;
4295        record.policy_decision = policy_decision;
4296        record.policy = deciding_policy;
4297        record.pilot_cap_reached = pilot_cap_reached;
4298        record.drift_tripped = evidence.drift_tripped;
4299        record.inputs = inputs;
4300        judge.count_decision(decision.action, enforced);
4301        (verdict, record)
4302    }
4303
4304    /// Authorize a tool call and wait for its verdict event to become durable.
4305    pub async fn intercept_tool_durable(
4306        &self,
4307        headers: &HeaderMap,
4308        raw: &[u8],
4309    ) -> Result<ToolVerdict, PipelineError> {
4310        let state = self.clone();
4311        let owned_headers = headers.clone();
4312        let raw = raw.to_vec();
4313        let (verdict, session) =
4314            tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || state.intercept_tool_with_session(&owned_headers, &raw))
4315                .await
4316                .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)??;
4317        session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
4318        if session.capture_failed() {
4319            return Err(PipelineError::unavailable(
4320                "tool authorization audit capture failed".to_owned(),
4321            ));
4322        }
4323        Ok(verdict)
4324    }
4325
4326    /// Authorize a tool call on the blocking pool without waiting for the
4327    /// off-path event journal or broker publication.
4328    pub async fn intercept_tool_nonblocking(
4329        &self,
4330        headers: &HeaderMap,
4331        raw: &[u8],
4332    ) -> Result<ToolVerdict, PipelineError> {
4333        let state = self.clone();
4334        let owned_headers = headers.clone();
4335        let raw = raw.to_vec();
4336        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || state.intercept_tool(&owned_headers, &raw))
4337            .await
4338            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?
4339    }
4340
4341    pub(crate) async fn intercept_authenticated_nonblocking(
4342        &self,
4343        headers: &HeaderMap,
4344        raw: &[u8],
4345        caller: AuthenticatedCaller,
4346        preflight_denial: Option<ToolVerdict>,
4347    ) -> Result<ToolVerdict, PipelineError> {
4348        let state = self.clone();
4349        let headers = headers.clone();
4350        let raw = raw.to_vec();
4351        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
4352            state
4353                .intercept_tool_authenticated(&headers, &raw, &caller, preflight_denial)
4354                .map(|(verdict, _)| verdict)
4355        })
4356        .await
4357        .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)?
4358    }
