4360    pub(crate) fn lease_session(&self, headers: &HeaderMap) -> Result<SessionLease, PipelineError> {
4361        let id = session_id(headers)?;
4362        let session = self
4363            .sessions
4364            .get(&id)
4365            .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("unknown session".to_owned()))?;
4366        session
4367            .try_lease()
4368            .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("session is already closed".to_owned()))
4369    }
4370
4371    pub(crate) fn enqueue_transient_failure(
4372        &self,
4373        session_id: &str,
4374        stop_reason: StopReason,
4375        reason: String,
4376    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4377        // Every identity rejection
4378        // used to mint a DISTINCT signed audit session — two fsynced
4379        // spool files, a bridge publish, a reconciler adoption and an
4380        // Ed25519 signature per credential-free 401 probe. A curl loop
4381        // against a hardened deployment turned each rejection into
4382        // durable server-side work that nothing ever coalesced. Keep
4383        // the full per-rejection audit record for a bounded number of
4384        // rejections per minute (enough for forensic sampling), and
4385        // beyond that budget count-only: the metric and a rate-limited
4386        // WARN preserve the signal while the signed-receipt lifecycle
4387        // is no longer attacker-drivable.
4388        self.metrics
4389            .counter(
4390                "pv_identity_rejections_total",
4391                "Requests refused by identity validation",
4392            )
4393            .inc();
4394        const MAX_AUDITED_REJECTIONS_PER_MINUTE: u32 = 60;
4395        {
4396            let now = Instant::now();
4397            let mut window = self.identity_rejection_window.lock();
4398            if now.duration_since(window.0) >= std::time::Duration::from_secs(60) {
4399                *window = (now, 0);
4400            }
4401            if window.1 >= MAX_AUDITED_REJECTIONS_PER_MINUTE {
4402                self.metrics
4403                    .counter(
4404                        "pv_identity_rejections_unaudited_total",
4405                        "Identity rejections beyond the per-minute audit-record budget (counted only)",
4406                    )
4407                    .inc();
4408                return Ok(());
4409            }
4410            window.1 += 1;
4411        }
4412        let audit_session_id = format!("identity-rejected-{}", pv_core::new_event_uid());
4413        let identity = AgentIdentity {
4414            version: "unknown".to_owned(),
4415            charter: "identity-rejected".into(),
4416            instance_uid: audit_session_id.clone(),
4417            ttl_remaining_s: None,
4418        };
4419        let session = Arc::new(Session::new(
4420            audit_session_id,
4421            Workflow::Signed,
4422            identity.clone(),
4423            self.config.breaker.clone(),
4424        ));
4425        // Cap the attacker-controlled `session_id` echo so a
4426        // maliciously long header cannot bloat every audit record.
4427        // 64 bytes is enough to keep well-formed UUIDs and legitimate
4428        // client-chosen ids intact; anything larger is truncated with
4429        // a marker so operators can still tell what the caller sent.
4430        const MAX_ECHO: usize = 64;
4431        let mut boundary = MAX_ECHO.min(session_id.len());
4432        while boundary < session_id.len() && !session_id.is_char_boundary(boundary) {
4433            boundary -= 1;
4434        }
4435        let truncated = boundary < session_id.len();
4436        let echo = &session_id[..boundary];
4437        let echo_marker = if truncated { "…(truncated)" } else { "" };
4438        self.enqueue_failure(
4439            session,
4440            identity,
4441            stop_reason,
4442            format!("requested session {echo:?}{echo_marker}: {reason}"),
4443        )
4444    }
4445
4446    fn enqueue_failure(
4447        &self,
4448        session: Arc<Session>,
4449        identity: AgentIdentity,
4450        stop_reason: StopReason,
4451        reason: String,
4452    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4453        self.worker
4454            .try_submit(self.failure_job(session, identity, stop_reason, reason))
4455            .map_err(PipelineError::unavailable_source)
4456    }
4457
4458    /// A failure job for a request a console-pushed policy refused: the
4459    /// stop-reason payload also names the policy, so the console can
4460    /// attribute the block to it (the tool path does the same through
4461    /// `ToolVerdict::Blocked::policy`).
4462    fn policy_failure_job(
4463        &self,
4464        session: Arc<Session>,
4465        identity: AgentIdentity,
4466        stop_reason: StopReason,
4467        reason: String,
4468        policy: Option<String>,
4469    ) -> WorkerJob {
4470        let mut job = self.failure_job(session, identity, stop_reason, reason);
4471        if let (Some(policy), Value::Object(payload)) = (policy, &mut job.payload) {
4472            payload.insert("policy".to_owned(), Value::String(policy));
4473        }
4474        job
4475    }
4476
4477    fn failure_job(
4478        &self,
4479        session: Arc<Session>,
4480        identity: AgentIdentity,
4481        stop_reason: StopReason,
4482        reason: String,
4483    ) -> WorkerJob {
4484        let atif = (session.workflow == Workflow::Unsigned).then(|| AtifCapture {
4485            source: pv_atif::Source::Agent,
4486            message: Value::String(reason.clone()),
4487            reasoning_content: None,
4488            model_name: None,
4489            tool_calls: None,
4490            observation: None,
4491            llm_call_count: Some(0),
4492        });
4493        WorkerJob {
4494            session,
4495            identity,
4496            class: EventClass::StopReason,
4497            payload: serde_json::json!({"reason": reason}),
4498            text: String::new(),
4499            analyze_loop: false,
4500            status: StatusId::Failure,
4501            stop_reason: Some(stop_reason),
4502            native_stop_reason: None,
4503            metrics: EventMetrics::default(),
4504            cost_usd_micros: 0,
4505            prompt_token_correction: 0,
4506            atif,
4507            response_marker: None,
4508            response_attempt: None,
4509        }
4510    }
4511
4512    #[cfg(test)]
4513    pub(crate) fn authorize_session(
4514        &self,
4515        headers: &HeaderMap,
4516        session: &Session,
4517        required_scope: &str,
4518    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4519        self.authenticate(headers)?
4520            .authorize_session(self, session, required_scope)
4521    }
4522
4523    /// `signed_workflow_scope` gate: a token that lacks the configured
4524    /// scope may not open a signed workflow. Without it the
4525    /// `consequential_tools` refusal ("requires a signed workflow") is
4526    /// only an audit-mode selector, since any caller can send
4527    /// `X-PV-Workflow: signed`. Scope checks follow
4528    /// `enforce_identity_scopes`, exactly like `chat_scope`.
4529    pub(crate) fn require_signed_workflow_scope(
4530        &self,
4531        workflow: Workflow,
4532        caller: Option<&AuthenticatedCaller>,
4533    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4534        if workflow != Workflow::Signed {
4535            return Ok(());
4536        }
4537        let Some(scope) = self.config.signed_workflow_scope.as_deref() else {
4538            return Ok(());
4539        };
4540        match caller {
4541            Some(caller) => caller.authorize(self, Some(scope)).map(|_| ()),
4542            None => Ok(()),
4543        }
4544    }
4545
4546    /// Authenticate and authorize in one step. Production paths keep the
4547    /// `AuthenticatedCaller` (the signed-workflow gate needs it), so this
4548    /// shorthand only serves the unit tests.
4549    #[cfg(test)]
4550    pub(crate) fn resolve_identity(
4551        &self,
4552        headers: &HeaderMap,
4553        required_scope: Option<&str>,
4554    ) -> Result<AgentIdentity, PipelineError> {
4555        self.authenticate(headers)?.authorize(self, required_scope)
4556    }
4557
4558    pub(crate) fn authenticate(&self, headers: &HeaderMap) -> Result<AuthenticatedCaller, PipelineError> {
4559        let bearer = single_header(headers, "authorization")?
4560            .and_then(|value| value.to_str().ok())
4561            .and_then(strip_bearer_scheme);
4562        if let (Some(token), Some(validator)) = (bearer, &self.identity) {
4563            self.metrics
4564                .counter(
4565                    "pv_identity_validations_total",
4566                    "Full inbound identity validations",
4567                )
4568                .inc();
4569            let validated = validator.validate(token).map_err(identity_error_to_pipeline)?;
4570            return Ok(AuthenticatedCaller {
4571                identity: validated.agent_identity(),
4572                validated: Some(validated),
4573            });
4574        }
4575        Ok(AuthenticatedCaller {
4576            identity: self.resolve_anonymous_identity(headers, None)?,
4577            validated: None,
4578        })
4579    }
4580
4581    pub(crate) fn audit_authentication_error(
4582        &self,
4583        headers: &HeaderMap,
4584        error: &PipelineError,
4585    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4586        if matches!(
4587            error,
4588            PipelineError::Unauthorized(_) | PipelineError::Blocked { .. }
4589        ) {
4590            self.enqueue_transient_failure(
4591                &session_id(headers).unwrap_or_default(),
4592                StopReason::IdentityRejected,
4593                error.to_string(),
4594            )?;
4595        } else if matches!(error, PipelineError::Unavailable { .. }) {
4596            self.metrics
4597                .counter(
4598                    "pv_identity_unavailable_total",
4599                    "Identity validation dependency failures",
4600                )
4601                .inc();
4602        }
4603        Ok(())
4604    }
4605
4606    fn resolve_anonymous_identity(
4607        &self,
4608        headers: &HeaderMap,
4609        required_scope: Option<&str>,
4610    ) -> Result<AgentIdentity, PipelineError> {
4611        // Reuse the duplicate-header refusal
4612        // pattern for `Authorization` on the identity hot path.
4613        // Previously `HeaderMap::get(AUTHORIZATION)` returned the
4614        // first value while the `PreparedRequest.client_authorization`
4615        // capture and header-smuggling proxies could observe a
4616        // merged `A, B` form — auth split-brain: the harness
4617        // authenticates as A while log aggregators / WAFs / OTLP
4618        // exporters attribute the request to B. Refuse the multi-
4619        // value case at ingress, symmetric with the X-PV-Session /
4620        // X-PV-Workflow guards.
4621        // RFC 7235 §2.1 declares the auth-scheme token
4622        // case-insensitive. Historically `strip_prefix("Bearer ")`
4623        // silently dropped `bearer eyJ...` / `BEARER eyJ...` /
4624        // `Bearer\teyJ...` to `None`, then the outer match arm
4625        // returned anonymous (when require_identity=false, the
4626        // shipped default) — the caller believed they had
4627        // authenticated while the audit trail attributed the
4628        // request to `anonymous`. That's the exact repudiation
4629        // vector the surrounding refuse-anonymous-with-token
4630        // guard was written to close. Now strip the scheme
4631        // case-insensitively.
4632        let bearer = single_header(headers, "authorization")?
4633            .and_then(|value| value.to_str().ok())
4634            .and_then(strip_bearer_scheme);
4635        match (bearer, &self.identity) {
4636            (Some(token), Some(validator)) => {
4637                let validated = validator.validate(token).map_err(identity_error_to_pipeline)?;
4638                if self.config.enforce_identity_scopes {
4639                    if let Some(required) = required_scope {
4640                        if !scope_allows(&validated.claims.scopes, required) {
4641                            // RFC 6750 §3.1: a syntactically valid, correctly
4642                            // signed token that lacks the required scope is an
4643                            // AUTHORIZATION failure — 403 insufficient_scope —
4644                            // not a 401. Returning 401 (the old behavior) told
4645                            // SDK token-refreshers to re-authenticate, which
4646                            // cannot help: the grant itself lacks the scope.
4647                            return Err(PipelineError::blocked(format!(
4648                                "identity scope {required:?} is required"
4649                            )));
4650                        }
4651                    }
4652                }
4653                Ok(validated.agent_identity())
4654            }
4655            // Client presented a bearer but the validator is not
4656            // configured. Two legitimate cases and one attack surface:
4657            //  (a) `upstream_authorization_passthrough = true`: the
4658            //      client's bearer is the CREDENTIAL FOR THE UPSTREAM
4659            //      PROVIDER, not an identity claim for us. Accept
4660            //      anonymous locally so the request proceeds and the
4661            //      relay layer forwards the header. This is the
4662            //      documented dev-mode BYO-key posture.
4663            //  (b) Neither passthrough nor validator: a bearer sent to
4664            //      a no-op harness is a configuration mismatch —
4665            //      REJECTED with a diagnostic 401 (see the closing
4666            //      paragraph below and the arm's else branch) rather
4667            //      than silently recorded as anonymous.
4668            //  (c) A validator EXISTS but this arm ran because
4669            //      match fell through: unreachable because arm 1
4670            //      catches (Some, Some). No action.
4671            //
4672            // The attack case — a caller sends a scoped identity token
4673            // and we silently attribute the request as `anonymous`
4674            // producing a repudiation vector — was closed at commit
4675            // e56... by rejecting here. But that broke passthrough
4676            // mode. Restore acceptance when passthrough is on so the
4677            // BYO-key path works, and continue rejecting only when
4678            // NO passthrough is configured and identity was not
4679            // required (i.e. the client presenting a bearer to a
4680            // no-op harness).
4681            (Some(_), None) => {
4682                let has_static_key =
4683                    self.config.upstream_api_key_env.is_some() || self.config.upstream_api_key_file.is_some();
4684                if self.config.upstream_authorization_passthrough {
4685                    Ok(AgentIdentity {
4686                        version: "dev".to_owned(),
4687                        charter: "anonymous".into(),
4688                        instance_uid: "anonymous".to_owned(),
4689                        ttl_remaining_s: None,
4690                    })
4691                } else if has_static_key || self.config.ignore_client_authorization {
4692                    // Static-key posture (the wizard's default): the
4693                    // operator has declared that THIS PROXY owns the
4694                    // upstream credential, and OpenAI-compatible SDKs
4695                    // unconditionally send a placeholder bearer (the
4696                    // client library refuses to start without one).
4697                    // Rejecting here made the flagship onboarding fail
4698                    // on request 1 with the official SDK. The
4699                    // repudiation rationale for the rejection below
4700                    // does not apply: exactly like the passthrough arm
4701                    // above, the config itself assigns the client
4702                    // bearer a non-identity meaning (a placeholder to
4703                    // be REPLACED by the static key — it is never
4704                    // forwarded). Accept anonymous; the placeholder
4705                    // never reaches the provider.
4706                    //
4707                    // `ignore_client_authorization` is the
4708                    // explicit opt-in covering the same posture for
4709                    // KEYLESS upstreams (Ollama, LM Studio, vLLM — no
4710                    // static key configured, so the implicit rule above
4711                    // cannot fire). validate() refuses the flag in
4712                    // combination with require_identity (the validator
4713                    // must see the header) and with passthrough (cannot
4714                    // both discard and forward).
4715                    tracing::debug!(
4716                        "client bearer ignored: static upstream key posture (placeholder \
4717                         SDK credential, not an identity claim)"
4718                    );
4719                    Ok(AgentIdentity {
4720                        version: "dev".to_owned(),
4721                        charter: "anonymous".into(),
4722                        instance_uid: "anonymous".to_owned(),
4723                        ttl_remaining_s: None,
4724                    })
4725                } else {
4726                    Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized(
4727                        "bearer token presented but identity validator is not configured — \
4728                         refusing to silently record the request as anonymous (either \
4729                         configure identity_jwks_url / identity_hmac_secret_file, or set \
4730                         upstream_authorization_passthrough=true if the bearer is meant \
4731                         for the upstream provider)"
4732                            .to_owned(),
4733                    ))
4734                }
4735            }
4736            (None, _) if self.config.require_identity => {
4737                Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized("missing bearer token".to_owned()))
4738            }
4739            _ => Ok(AgentIdentity {
4740                version: "dev".to_owned(),
4741                charter: "anonymous".into(),
4742                instance_uid: "anonymous".to_owned(),
4743                ttl_remaining_s: None,
4744            }),
4745        }
4746    }
4747
4748    fn observe_stage(&self, stage: &str, started: Instant) {
4749        let elapsed = elapsed_us(started);
4750        // Prefer the pre-resolved handle when the caller's stage name
4751        // maps to a known Stage variant — that path is O(1) with no
4752        // mutex or allocation. Callers still passing legacy or
4753        // unrecognized labels fall through to the lazy registry path
4754        // so the metric remains discoverable (the lazy Registry call
4755        // uses the same shared Arc<Histogram> the hot cache points
4756        // to for known stages).
4757        if let Some(known) = Stage::from_label(stage) {
4758            // `Stage::index()` is a closed 0..5 map over the same
4759            // `Stage::ORDER` array both HotMetrics arrays are built
4760            // from — `.get()` keeps clippy's indexing lint satisfied
4761            // without a reachable None arm.
4762            if let Some(histogram) = self.hot_metrics.stage_histograms.get(known.index()) {
4763                histogram.observe_us(elapsed);
4764            }
4765            if self.hot_metrics.strict_stage_budget && elapsed > 2_000 {
4766                if let Some(counter) = self.hot_metrics.stage_strict_budget_counters.get(known.index()) {
4767                    counter.inc();
4768                }
4769                tracing::warn!(stage, elapsed_us = elapsed, "strict stage budget exceeded");
4770            }
4771            return;
4772        }
4773        self.metrics
4774            .histogram(
4775                &format!("pv_stage_duration_seconds{{stage=\"{stage}\"}}"),
4776                "Harness stage latency",
4777            )
4778            .observe_us(elapsed);
4779        if self.hot_metrics.strict_stage_budget && elapsed > 2_000 {
4780            self.metrics
4781                .counter(
4782                    &format!("pv_strict_budget_breaches_total{{stage=\"{stage}\"}}"),
4783                    "Middleware stages that exceeded the strict per-stage budget",
4784                )
4785                .inc();
4786            tracing::warn!(stage, elapsed_us = elapsed, "strict stage budget exceeded");
4787        }
4788    }
4789}
4790
4791/// Authentication result confined to one request. It cannot be constructed by callers.
4792#[derive(Clone)]
4793pub(crate) struct AuthenticatedCaller {
4794    pub(crate) identity: AgentIdentity,
4795    pub(crate) validated: Option<pv_identity::ValidatedIdentity>,
4796}
4797
4798impl AuthenticatedCaller {
4799    pub(crate) fn authorize(
4800        &self,
4801        state: &AppState,
4802        scope: Option<&str>,
4803    ) -> Result<AgentIdentity, PipelineError> {
4804        if let Some(validated) = &self.validated {
4805            let now = pv_core::time::now_ms() / 1000;
4806            let leeway = state.identity.as_ref().map_or(0, |v| v.leeway_secs());
4807            if now > validated.claims.exp.saturating_add(leeway) {
4808                return Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized("identity validation failed".into()));
4809            }
4810            if state.config.enforce_identity_scopes {
4811                if let Some(required) = scope {
4812                    if !scope_allows(&validated.claims.scopes, required) {
4813                        return Err(PipelineError::blocked(format!(
4814                            "identity scope {required:?} is required"
4815                        )));
4816                    }
4817                }
4818            }
4819            let mut identity = self.identity.clone();
4820            identity.ttl_remaining_s = Some(validated.claims.exp.saturating_sub(now));
4821            return Ok(identity);
4822        }
4823        Ok(self.identity.clone())
4824    }
4825
4826    pub(crate) fn authorize_session(
4827        &self,
4828        state: &AppState,
4829        session: &Session,
4830        scope: &str,
4831    ) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4832        let identity = self.authorize(state, Some(scope))?;
4833        validate_session_binding(session, session.workflow, &identity)?;
4834        self.bind_session(session)?;
4835        session.refresh_identity(&identity);
4836        Ok(())
4837    }
4838
4839    /// What the policy decision point may know about this caller.
4840    pub(crate) fn facts(&self) -> crate::authz::CallerFacts<'_> {
4841        match &self.validated {
4842            Some(validated) => crate::authz::CallerFacts {
4843                instance_uid: &validated.claims.instance_uid,
4844                charter: &validated.claims.charter,
4845                subject: Some(&validated.claims.sub),
4846            },
4847            None => crate::authz::CallerFacts {
4848                instance_uid: &self.identity.instance_uid,
4849                charter: "anonymous",
4850                subject: None,
4851            },
4852        }
4853    }
4854
4855    pub(crate) fn principal_digest(&self) -> Option<String> {
4856        self.validated.as_ref().map(|validated| {
4857            let pair = serde_json::json!([validated.claims.iss, validated.claims.sub]);
4858            pv_core::digest::sha256_hex(pair.to_string().as_bytes())
4859        })
4860    }
4861
4862    fn bind_session(&self, session: &Session) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
4863        if !session.bind_principal(self.principal_digest().as_deref()) {
4864            return Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized(
4865                "session is bound to a different principal".into(),
4866            ));
4867        }
4868        Ok(())
4869    }
4870}
4871
4872fn truthy_env(name: &str) -> bool {
4873    match std::env::var(name) {
4874        Ok(value) => matches!(
4875            value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(),
4876            "1" | "true" | "yes" | "on"
4877        ),
4878        Err(_) => false,
4879    }
4880}
4881
4882/// Map a `reqwest::Error` to a client-safe reason (CWE-209): the raw
4883/// `Display` includes the request URL, which leaks the operator-configured
4884/// upstream URL — potentially an internal hostname — to the caller. Return a
4885/// stable, non-identifying category instead; the detailed message is logged
4886/// server-side by the caller.
4887pub(crate) fn classify_upstream_error(error: &reqwest::Error) -> &'static str {
4888    if error.is_timeout() {
4889        "upstream timed out"
4890    } else if error.is_connect() {
4891        "upstream unreachable"
4892    } else if error.is_request() {
4893        "upstream request rejected"
4894    } else if error.is_body() || error.is_decode() {
4895        "upstream response malformed"
4896    } else if error.is_status() {
4897        "upstream returned error status"
4898    } else {
4899        "upstream forwarding failed"
4900    }
4901}
4902
4903/// Map an [`IdentityError`] to a client-safe reason (CWE-209).
4904///
4905/// The `Display` impl on `IdentityError` embeds attacker-influenced strings
4906/// (the presented `kid`, `iss`, and structural detail from the underlying
4907/// JWT parser) and distinguishes between at least sixteen distinct failure
4908/// modes. Returning that verbatim to the client turns each 401 response
4909/// into an *enumeration oracle* for the validator's configured JWKS and
4910/// issuer allowlist: an attacker can iterate candidate `kid`s and issuers
4911/// and read the response body to discover which values are registered
4912/// (`UnknownKid` vs `AlgorithmRejected` vs `Verification` vs `BadIssuer`
4913/// all produce distinguishable text).
4914///
4915/// We collapse every attacker-reachable variant to a single stable string.
4916/// The detailed cause is preserved server-side by the caller via
4917/// `tracing::warn!` and the failure-job audit chain, so operators keep
4918/// full diagnostic detail without exposing it to the network.
4919/// An unreachable revocation list is an availability fault: answer 503 so
4920/// clients back off and retry, instead of a 401 that tells them to throw
4921/// away a credential that may be perfectly valid.
4922pub(crate) fn identity_error_to_pipeline(error: pv_identity::IdentityError) -> PipelineError {
4923    if matches!(error, pv_identity::IdentityError::RevocationUnavailable(_)) {
4924        tracing::error!(error = %error, "identity revocation list unavailable; refusing the request");
4925        return PipelineError::unavailable(
4926            "identity revocation list is unavailable; retry shortly".to_owned(),
4927        );
4928    }
4929    tracing::warn!(error = %error, "identity validation failed");
4930    PipelineError::Unauthorized(classify_identity_error(&error).to_owned())
4931}
4932
4933pub(crate) fn classify_identity_error(error: &pv_identity::IdentityError) -> &'static str {
4934    match error {
4935        // Server-side misconfiguration is not attacker-reachable in the
4936        // normal request path (validator construction rejects a bad JWKS
4937        // at startup); report it distinctly so operator dashboards can
4938        // surface it if it ever appears.
4939        pv_identity::IdentityError::Jwks(_) => "identity validator misconfigured",
4940        // Every other variant is at least partially attacker-influenced —
4941        // collapse to one opaque message. Any finer distinction leaks
4942        // configured `kid`s, issuers, or acceptable algorithms.
4943        _ => "identity validation failed",
4944    }
4945}
4946
4947/// Extract the single value for a control header, refusing duplicates.
4948///
4949/// `HeaderMap::get(name)` returns only the first entry when a header
4950/// appears multiple times. A client sending
4951/// `X-PV-Session: sessA` followed by `X-PV-Session: sessB` would then
4952/// have `sessA` used for state while an intermediary log-aggregator
4953/// might observe `sessA, sessB` (some proxies merge on the wire). The
4954/// resulting session-desync is hard to diagnose and is a header
4955/// smuggling primitive at boundaries where our proxy is behind
4956/// another one. Refuse the multi-value case at ingress rather than
4957/// silently accept the first.
4958pub(crate) fn single_header<'a>(
4959    headers: &'a HeaderMap,
4960    name: &'static str,
4961) -> Result<Option<&'a axum::http::HeaderValue>, PipelineError> {
4962    let mut iter = headers.get_all(name).into_iter();
4963    let first = iter.next();
4964    if iter.next().is_some() {
4965        return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(format!(
4966            "request carries more than one {name} header; provide exactly one"
4967        )));
4968    }
4969    Ok(first)
4970}
4971
4972/// Strip the RFC 7235 §2.1 `Bearer` scheme from an Authorization header
4973/// value, case-insensitively (per the RFC), and allow one or more
4974/// whitespace characters (`SP` / `HTAB`) between the scheme token and
4975/// the credential (per `token1` production in §2.1).
4976///
4977/// `str::strip_prefix("Bearer ")` used to be the sole
4978/// parser. It missed `bearer`, `BEARER`, and (per the RFC's own
4979/// grammar) `Bearer\t...`, silently dropping to `None` and — when
4980/// `require_identity = false` — letting the request execute as
4981/// anonymous while the caller believed they had authenticated.
4982pub(crate) fn strip_bearer_scheme(value: &str) -> Option<&str> {
4983    let (scheme, rest) = value.split_at_checked(6)?;
4984    if !scheme.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Bearer") {
4985        return None;
4986    }
4987    let rest = rest.trim_start_matches([' ', '\t']);
4988    if rest.len() == value.len() - scheme.len() {
4989        // No whitespace at all after `Bearer` — the credential MUST
4990        // be separated per the ABNF (`token1 = 1*( SP / HTAB )
4991        // token`). Refuse `BearerXYZ` — that's a scheme other than
4992        // Bearer's credential, not a Bearer credential.
4993        return None;
4994    }
4995    if rest.is_empty() {
4996        // Empty credential.
4997        return None;
4998    }
4999    Some(rest)
5000}
5001
5002pub(crate) fn session_id(headers: &HeaderMap) -> Result<String, PipelineError> {
5003    match single_header(headers, SESSION_HEADER)? {
5004        Some(value) => {
5005            let value = value
5006                .to_str()
5007                .map_err(|_| PipelineError::bad_request("X-PV-Session is not valid text".to_owned()))?;
5008            pv_core::SessionId::parse(value)
5009                .map(|id| id.to_string())
5010                .map_err(PipelineError::bad_request_source)
5011        }
5012        None => Ok(pv_core::new_session_id().to_string()),
5013    }
5014}
5015
5016/// Stable id used as the principal-scoped budget key.
5017///
5018/// For authenticated requests this is the JWT's `instance_uid` (the
5019/// per-instance principal name — a single agent workload can hold several
5020/// concurrent tokens under one uid). For requests without a validator
5021/// (dev mode / passthrough) the identity's `instance_uid` is already the
5022/// literal `"anonymous"` sentinel, so this function is a straight
5023/// pass-through; the shipping default is a single shared bucket and the
5024/// config validator refuses `principal_budget` while `require_identity =
5025/// false` unless the operator explicitly acknowledges that shape.
5026/// Stable policy handle for a PDP denial, following the
5027/// `workflow.signed_required` naming: the console attributes denials by
5028/// this name, and it points the operator at the config block responsible.
5029fn pdp_policy_name(code: pv_sandbox::DenialCode) -> &'static str {
5030    match code {
5031        pv_sandbox::DenialCode::UnmappedTool => "pdp.intent_map",
5032        pv_sandbox::DenialCode::MissionExpired | pv_sandbox::DenialCode::MissionDenied => "pdp.mission",
5033        pv_sandbox::DenialCode::PdpDenied => "pdp.authzen",
5034        _ => "pdp",
5035    }
5036}
5037
5038/// The agents above the caller in its delegation chain, nearest first:
5039/// the `parent_token` chain, then the agents named in nested `act`
5040/// claims. An `act` entry that is a client id rather than an agent
5041/// instance simply matches no recorded refusal.
5042fn delegation_parents(validated: &pv_identity::ValidatedIdentity) -> Vec<String> {
5043    let caller = &validated.claims.instance_uid;
5044    let mut parents: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
5045    let mut push = |uid: &str| {
5046        if uid != caller && !parents.iter().any(|known| known == uid) {
5047            parents.push(uid.to_owned());
5048        }
5049    };
5050    for token in validated.chain_tokens.iter().skip(1) {
5051        push(&token.instance_uid);
5052    }
5053    let mut actor = validated.claims.act.as_ref();
5054    while let Some(claim) = actor {
5055        push(&claim.sub);
5056        actor = claim.act.as_deref();
5057    }
5058    parents
5059}
5060
5061/// True when any chat message's text contains `needle` (plain string
5062/// content, or the `text` of content parts).
5063fn messages_contain(payload: &Value, needle: &str) -> bool {
5064    payload
5065        .get("messages")
5066        .and_then(Value::as_array)
5067        .into_iter()
5068        .flatten()
5069        .any(|message| match message.get("content") {
5070            Some(Value::String(text)) => text.contains(needle),
5071            Some(Value::Array(parts)) => parts.iter().any(|part| {
5072                part.get("text")
5073                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
5074                    .is_some_and(|text| text.contains(needle))
5075            }),
5076            _ => false,
5077        })
5078}
5079/// Longest arguments a judged call's record keeps with `record_inputs`.
5080const MAX_RECORDED_ARGUMENT_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
5081
5082/// A tagged call scored before the session admission lock.
5083struct Prejudged {
5084    call: pv_sandbox::ValidatedCall,
5085    outcomes: Vec<pv_judge::JudgeOutcome>,
5086    judge_us: u64,
5087    /// The context the judge saw, when records keep it.
5088    inputs: Option<pv_judge::SessionContext>,
5089}
5090
5091/// A verdict reached without consulting the semantic judge.
5092trait Unjudged {
5093    fn into_unjudged(self) -> (ToolVerdict, Option<pv_judge::JudgeRecord>);
5094}
5095
5096impl Unjudged for ToolVerdict {
5097    fn into_unjudged(self) -> (ToolVerdict, Option<pv_judge::JudgeRecord>) {
5098        (self, None)
5099    }
5100}
5101
5102pub(crate) fn principal_id_for_budget(identity: &AgentIdentity) -> String {
5103    if identity.instance_uid.is_empty() {
5104        "anonymous".to_owned()
5105    } else {
5106        identity.instance_uid.clone()
5107    }
5108}
5109
5110fn workflow(headers: &HeaderMap, default: &str) -> Result<Workflow, PipelineError> {
5111    let value = single_header(headers, WORKFLOW_HEADER)?
5112        .map(|header| {
5113            header
5114                .to_str()
5115                .map_err(|_| PipelineError::bad_request("X-PV-Workflow is not valid text".to_owned()))
5116        })
5117        .transpose()?
5118        .unwrap_or(default);
5119    Workflow::parse(value).ok_or_else(|| {
5120        PipelineError::bad_request(format!("X-PV-Workflow must be signed or unsigned, got {value:?}"))
5121    })
5122}
5123
5124fn last_message_text(payload: &Value) -> String {
5125    let Some(message) = payload
5126        .get("messages")
5127        .and_then(Value::as_array)
5128        .and_then(|messages| messages.last())
5129    else {
5130        return String::new();
5131    };
5132    // Primary source: string `content`.
5133    if let Some(text) = message.get("content").and_then(Value::as_str) {
5134        if !text.is_empty() {
5135            return text.to_owned();
5136        }
5137    }
5138    // Mirror of the response-side fix: a
5139    // tool-call-only assistant message carries `content: null` and a
5140    // `tool_calls` array. `last_message_text` returning "" here would
5141    // feed a zero-vector embedding into the breaker in worker.rs and
5142    // false-trip legitimate tool-driven agents on the request side.
5143    // Synthesize `tool_name(arguments)` for each tool call, in
5144    // wire-order — same shape as the response-side synthesis in
5145    // routes.rs::AbortFinalizingStream::submit_response_capture.
5146    //
5147    // Beware: `function.arguments` reaches the
5148    // wire in TWO shapes: a JSON-encoded string (OpenAI reference)
5149    // OR a bare JSON object (some clients / most Anthropic-shaped
5150    // gateways). `.as_str()` on the object variant returned None and
5151    // the synthesis collapsed to `tool_name()` — worse than the
5152    // pre-fix behavior, because now two different-argument calls to
5153    // the same tool produced IDENTICAL synthesized text, causing
5154    // false loop trips. Match the tolerant pattern from
5155    // `atif_capture_from_request` in the same file: render the raw
5156    // Value directly when it isn't a stringified JSON. Empty and
5157    // missing collapse to "" and get gated off analyze_loop
5158    // downstream.
5159    if let Some(tool_calls) = message.get("tool_calls").and_then(Value::as_array) {
5160        let synthesized: Vec<String> = tool_calls
5161            .iter()
5162            .filter_map(|call| {
5163                let name = call
5164                    .get("function")
5165                    .and_then(|f| f.get("name"))
5166                    .and_then(Value::as_str)?;
5167                let arguments = call.get("function").and_then(|f| f.get("arguments"));
5168                let args_rendered = match arguments {
5169                    Some(Value::String(s)) => s.clone(),
5170                    Some(other) => serde_json::to_string(other).unwrap_or_default(),
5171                    None => String::new(),
5172                };
5173                Some(format!("{name}({args_rendered})"))
5174            })
5175            .collect();
5176        if !synthesized.is_empty() {
5177            return synthesized.join("\n");
5178        }
5179    }
5180    // Content-parts array (multimodal). Concatenate every text part
5181    // so an assistant reply built from mixed text+image parts still
5182    // feeds a non-zero vector to the breaker rather than "".
5183    if let Some(parts) = message.get("content").and_then(Value::as_array) {
5184        let text: String = parts
5185            .iter()
5186            .filter_map(|part| part.get("text").and_then(Value::as_str))
5187            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
5188            .join("\n");
5189        if !text.is_empty() {
5190            return text;
5191        }
5192    }
5193    String::new()
5194}
5195
5196/// Human-readable JSON type name for diagnostics — mirrors `typeof` in
5197/// JS. Used by [`atif_capture_from_request`] to say
5198/// `'messages' must be a JSON array, got object` rather than the
5199/// historical opaque `chat payload has no messages`.
5200fn json_type_name(value: &Value) -> &'static str {
5201    match value {
5202        Value::Null => "null",
5203        Value::Bool(_) => "boolean",
5204        Value::Number(_) => "number",
5205        Value::String(_) => "string",
5206        Value::Array(_) => "array",
5207        Value::Object(_) => "object",
5208    }
5209}
5210
5211fn atif_capture_from_request(payload: &Value) -> Result<AtifCapture, PipelineError> {
5212    // Split the two failure classes so support engineers can tell
5213    // "field missing" from "field is the wrong shape" — chasing a
5214    // phantom "no messages" ticket for a caller who typed
5215    // `"messages": {}` used to be the top diagnostic-quality
5216    // complaint. The OpenAI Responses API also uses `input`
5217    // instead of `messages`; the missing-field message now points
5218    // directly at the correct fix.
5219    let messages_value = payload
5220        .get("messages")
5221        .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("chat payload is missing 'messages'".to_owned()))?;
5222    let messages = messages_value.as_array().ok_or_else(|| {
5223        PipelineError::bad_request(format!(
5224            "'messages' must be a JSON array, got {}",
5225            json_type_name(messages_value)
5226        ))
5227    })?;
5228    let message = messages
5229        .last()
5230        .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("chat payload 'messages' is empty".to_owned()))?;
5231    let role = message.get("role").and_then(Value::as_str);
5232    let source = match role {
5233        // "function" is the legacy OpenAI function-calling spelling of
5234        // "tool" (a tool result fed back to the model) — still emitted
5235        // by older LangChain, AutoGen and llama-index versions.
5236        // Refusing it 400'd legitimate agent traffic.
5237        Some("system" | "developer" | "tool" | "function") => pv_atif::Source::System,
5238        Some("user") => pv_atif::Source::User,
5239        Some("assistant") => pv_atif::Source::Agent,
5240        Some(other) => {
5241            return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(format!(
5242                "unsupported chat role {other:?}"
5243            )))
5244        }
5245        None => return Err(PipelineError::bad_request("chat message has no role".to_owned())),
5246    };
5247    let content = message
5248        .get("content")
5249        .filter(|value| value.is_string() || value.is_array())
5250        .cloned()
5251        .unwrap_or_else(|| Value::String(String::new()));
5252    let tool_calls = message.get("tool_calls").and_then(Value::as_array).map(|calls| {
5253        calls
5254            .iter()
5255            .filter_map(|call| {
5256                let function = call.get("function")?;
5257                let arguments = function
5258                    .get("arguments")
5259                    .cloned()
5260                    .unwrap_or(Value::Object(Default::default()));
5261                let arguments = arguments
5262                    .as_str()
5263                    .and_then(|raw| serde_json::from_str(raw).ok())
5264                    .unwrap_or(arguments);
5265                Some(pv_atif::ToolCall {
5266                    tool_call_id: call
5267                        .get("id")
5268                        .and_then(Value::as_str)
5269                        .map_or_else(pv_core::new_event_uid, str::to_owned),
5270                    function_name: function.get("name")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
5271                    arguments,
5272                    extra: None,
5273                })
5274            })
5275            .collect()
5276    });
5277    let observation = (role == Some("tool")).then(|| pv_atif::Observation {
5278        results: vec![pv_atif::ObservationResult {
5279            source_call_id: None,
5280            content: Some(content.clone()),
5281            subagent_trajectory_ref: None,
5282            extra: message
5283                .get("tool_call_id")
5284                .cloned()
5285                .map(|tool_call_id| serde_json::json!({"tool_call_id": tool_call_id})),
5286        }],
5287    });
5288    Ok(AtifCapture {
5289        source,
5290        message: content,
5291        reasoning_content: if source == pv_atif::Source::Agent {
5292            message
5293                .get("reasoning_content")
5294                .and_then(Value::as_str)
5295                .map(str::to_owned)
5296        } else {
5297            None
5298        },
5299        model_name: if source == pv_atif::Source::Agent {
5300            payload.get("model").and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned)
5301        } else {
5302            None
5303        },
5304        tool_calls: if source == pv_atif::Source::Agent {
5305            tool_calls
5306        } else {
5307            None
5308        },
5309        observation,
5310        llm_call_count: (source == pv_atif::Source::Agent).then_some(1),
5311    })
5312}
5313
5314fn validate_session_binding(
5315    session: &Session,
5316    workflow: Workflow,
5317    identity: &AgentIdentity,
5318) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
5319    if session.workflow != workflow {
5320        return Err(PipelineError::bad_request(
5321            "session workflow cannot change after open".to_owned(),
5322        ));
5323    }
5324    if session.identity.instance_uid != identity.instance_uid
5325        || session.identity.charter != identity.charter
5326        || session.identity.version != identity.version
5327    {
5328        return Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized(
5329            "session is bound to a different agent identity".to_owned(),
5330        ));
5331    }
5332    Ok(())
5333}
5334
5335pub(crate) fn scope_allows(scopes: &[String], required: &str) -> bool {
5336    scopes.iter().any(|scope| {
5337        scope == "*"
5338            || scope == required
5339            // allocation-free prefix match. The old
5340            // `required.starts_with(&format!("{prefix}:"))` allocated a
5341            // String per granted scope per request on the identity hot
5342            // path. `strip_prefix(prefix)` + a `:` head check is the
5343            // same predicate with zero allocations.
5344            || scope.strip_suffix(":*").is_some_and(|prefix| {
5345                required
5346                    .strip_prefix(prefix)
5347                    .is_some_and(|rest| rest.starts_with(':'))
5348            })
5349    })
5350}
5351
5352/// The system message the loop breaker's `inject` action appends.
5353const CORRECTIVE_MESSAGE: &str = "ProofVisor detected a semantic loop. Stop repeating the previous approach, identify new evidence, and choose a materially different next action.";
5354
5355fn inject_corrective_message(payload: &mut Value) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {
5356    let messages = payload
5357        .get_mut("messages")
5358        .and_then(Value::as_array_mut)
5359        .ok_or_else(|| PipelineError::bad_request("chat payload has no messages array".to_owned()))?;
5360    messages.push(serde_json::json!({
5361        "role": "system",
5362        "content": CORRECTIVE_MESSAGE
5363    }));
5364    Ok(())
5365}
5366
5367/// The payload whose last message is the caller's turn, for the audit
5368/// capture. When the breaker appended its corrective message, the turn
5369/// is the message before it: compression keeps the last messages byte
5370/// for byte and transforms rewrite in place, so it is still there, with
5371/// any transform applied. Both capture functions read only the last
5372/// message, so a one-message view is enough.
5373fn caller_turn_view(payload: &Value, injected: bool) -> Option<Value> {
5374    if !injected {
5375        return None;
5376    }
5377    let messages = payload.get("messages").and_then(Value::as_array)?;
5378    let (last, earlier) = messages.split_last()?;
5379    if last.get("content").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some(CORRECTIVE_MESSAGE) {
5380        return None;
5381    }
5382    let turn = earlier.last()?;
5383    Some(serde_json::json!({ "messages": [turn] }))
5384}
5385
5386pub(crate) fn load_exchange_seed(path: &str) -> Result<zeroize::Zeroizing<[u8; 32]>, String> {
5387    use zeroize::Zeroizing;
5388    require_owner_only_secret(std::path::Path::new(path)).map_err(|e| format!("exchange seed file: {e}"))?;
5389    let encoded = Zeroizing::new(
5390        pv_core::fsutil::read_capped_string(std::path::Path::new(path), pv_core::fsutil::MAX_CONTROL_BYTES)
5391            .map_err(|e| format!("read exchange seed {path}: {e}"))?,
5392    );
5393    let bytes =
5394        Zeroizing::new(hex::decode(encoded.trim()).map_err(|e| format!("decode exchange seed as hex: {e}"))?);
5395    let seed: Zeroizing<[u8; 32]> = Zeroizing::new(
5396        <[u8; 32]>::try_from(bytes.as_slice())
5397            .map_err(|_| "exchange seed must contain exactly 32 bytes".to_owned())?,
5398    );
5399    if *seed == [0u8; 32] || *seed == [0xFFu8; 32] {
5400        return Err(format!("exchange seed at {path} is a known-weak value; refusing"));
5401    }
5402    Ok(seed)
5403}

5405#[cfg(test)]
5406mod tests {
5407    #![allow(
5408        clippy::expect_used,
5409        clippy::indexing_slicing,
5410        clippy::panic,
5411        clippy::unwrap_used
5412    )]
5413
5414    /// R285: the twice-registered supervised-loop metrics (eager boot
5415    /// registration here + lazy observation-site registration in
5416    /// main.rs/reconciler.rs) must take their HELP from the shared
5417    /// `supervision_help` consts / `LIFECYCLE_OUTBOX_PENDING_HELP`.
5418    /// Six counter pairs and the outbox gauge had drifted into
5419    /// per-tick "divergent HELP" warn spam; first-wins semantics also
5420    /// let a terse boot HELP shadow the rich forensic text alerting
5421    /// playbooks key on. A string literal beside any of these names
5422    /// reintroduces the drift the consts exist to prevent.
5423    #[test]
5424    fn twice_registered_metrics_use_shared_help_consts() {
5425        let sources = [
5426            ("pipeline.rs", include_str!("pipeline.rs")),
5427            ("main.rs", include_str!("main.rs")),
5428            ("reconciler.rs", include_str!("reconciler.rs")),
5429        ];
5430        let shared = [
5431            ("pv_reconciler_panics_total", "RECONCILER_PANICS"),
5432            ("pv_bridge_maintenance_panics_total", "BRIDGE_MAINTENANCE_PANICS"),
5433            ("pv_bridge_maintenance_errors_total", "BRIDGE_MAINTENANCE_ERRORS"),
5434            (
5435                "pv_bridge_maintenance_join_errors_total",
5436                "BRIDGE_MAINTENANCE_JOIN_ERRORS",
5437            ),
5438            ("pv_atif_retention_panics_total", "ATIF_RETENTION_PANICS"),
5439            ("pv_atif_retention_errors_total", "ATIF_RETENTION_ERRORS"),
5440            ("pv_lifecycle_outbox_pending", "LIFECYCLE_OUTBOX_PENDING_HELP"),
5441        ];
5442        for (file, src) in sources {
5443            for (metric, const_name) in shared {
5444                let needle = format!("\"{metric}\"");
5445                let mut from = 0;
5446                while let Some(pos) = src[from..].find(&needle) {
5447                    let at = from + pos;
5448                    // The ~200 chars after the name must reference the
5449                    // shared const (registration site) — doc comments,
5450                    // alert examples, and scrape asserts don't register
5451                    // and carry no adjacent `.counter(`/`.gauge(` call.
5452                    let window = &src[at..(at + 220).min(src.len())];
5453                    let registers = src[..at]
5454                        .rfind(|c: char| !c.is_whitespace())
5455                        .map(|p| {
5456                            src[p.saturating_sub(20)..at].contains(".counter(")
5457                                || src[p.saturating_sub(20)..at].contains(".gauge(")
5458                        })
5459                        .unwrap_or(false);
5460                    if registers {
5461                        assert!(
5462                            window.contains(const_name),
5463                            "{file}: registration of {metric} does not use \
5464                             supervision_help::{const_name} — string-literal HELP \
5465                             reintroduces the divergent-HELP drift (R285)"
5466                        );
5467                    }
5468                    from = at + needle.len();
5469                }
5470            }
5471        }
5472    }

5474    use super::*;
5475    use axum::http::HeaderValue;
5476    use pv_bridge::{BusError, PublishAck, StoredEvent};
5477    use pv_receipts::Ed25519Signer;
5478    use pv_sandbox::SandboxConfig;

5481
5482    /// `last_message_text`
5483    /// must produce distinct output for two different tool-call
5484    /// argument shapes so the loop breaker's embedding varies.
5485    /// Both string-shaped and object-shaped arguments MUST reach the
5486    /// synthesized text — the first take collapsed
5487    /// object-shaped arguments to `tool_name()`.
5488    #[test]
5489    fn last_message_text_tool_call_synthesis_varies_with_object_arguments() {
5490        let payload_string_args = serde_json::json!({
5491            "messages": [{
5492                "role": "assistant",
5493                "content": null,
5494                "tool_calls": [{
5495                    "id": "c1",
5496                    "type": "function",
5497                    "function": {"name": "read", "arguments": "{\"path\":\"a\"}"}
5498                }]
5499            }]
5500        });
5501        let payload_object_args_a = serde_json::json!({
5502            "messages": [{
5503                "role": "assistant",
5504                "content": null,
5505                "tool_calls": [{
5506                    "id": "c1",
5507                    "type": "function",
5508                    "function": {"name": "read", "arguments": {"path": "a"}}
5509                }]
5510            }]
5511        });
5512        let payload_object_args_b = serde_json::json!({
5513            "messages": [{
5514                "role": "assistant",
5515                "content": null,
5516                "tool_calls": [{
5517                    "id": "c1",
5518                    "type": "function",
5519                    "function": {"name": "read", "arguments": {"path": "b"}}
5520                }]
5521            }]
5522        });
5523        let t_string = last_message_text(&payload_string_args);
5524        let t_obj_a = last_message_text(&payload_object_args_a);
5525        let t_obj_b = last_message_text(&payload_object_args_b);
5526        assert!(!t_string.is_empty(), "string-arg synthesis must not be empty");
5527        assert!(!t_obj_a.is_empty(), "object-arg synthesis must not be empty");
5528        assert_ne!(
5529            t_obj_a, t_obj_b,
5530            "object-shape args with different content must produce different text; \
5531             got identical {t_obj_a:?}"
5532        );
5533        assert!(
5534            t_obj_a.contains("\"path\":\"a\"") || t_obj_a.contains("path"),
5535            "object-arg synthesis must include the argument content, got {t_obj_a:?}"
5536        );
5537    }
5538
5539    /// A tool-call message with EMPTY arguments still synthesizes to
5540    /// `tool_name()` and reaches the breaker as a non-empty string
5541    /// — otherwise every tool call with no arguments would false-trip
5542    /// the breaker via zero-vector embedding.
5543    #[test]
5544    fn last_message_text_tool_call_with_empty_arguments_still_synthesizes() {
5545        let payload = serde_json::json!({
5546            "messages": [{
5547                "role": "assistant",
5548                "content": null,
5549                "tool_calls": [{
5550                    "id": "c1",
5551                    "type": "function",
5552                    "function": {"name": "ping"}
5553                }]
5554            }]
5555        });
5556        let text = last_message_text(&payload);
5557        assert_eq!(text, "ping()");
5558    }

5560    struct NullBus;
5561
5562    struct BlockingSink {
5563        entered: AtomicBool,
5564        release: tokio::sync::Notify,
5565    }
5566
5567    impl VectorSink for BlockingSink {
5568        fn record<'a>(
5569            &'a self,
5570            _session_id: &'a str,
5571            _vector: &'a [f32],
5572        ) -> pv_loopdetect::VectorSinkFuture<'a> {
5573            Box::pin(async move {
5574                self.entered.store(true, AtomicOrdering::Release);
5575                self.release.notified().await;
5576                Ok(())
5577            })
5578        }
5579    }
5580
5581    impl EventBus for NullBus {
5582        fn publish(&self, topic: &str, _key: &str, _value: &Value) -> Result<PublishAck, BusError> {
5583            Ok(PublishAck {
5584                topic: topic.to_owned(),
5585                partition: 0,
5586                offset: 0,
5587            })
5588        }
5589
5590        fn fetch(
5591            &self,
5592            _topic: &str,
5593            _partition: u32,
5594            _offset: u64,
5595            _max: usize,
5596        ) -> Result<Vec<StoredEvent>, BusError> {
5597            Ok(Vec::new())
5598        }
5599
5600        fn partitions(&self, _topic: &str) -> Result<u32, BusError> {
5601            Ok(1)
5602        }
5603
5604        fn topics(&self) -> Vec<String> {
5605            EventClass::all()
5606                .iter()
5607                .map(|class| class.topic().to_owned())
5608                .collect()
5609        }
5610    }

5612    fn state(mut config: HarnessConfig) -> AppState {
5613        config.atif_spool_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap().keep().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
5614        AppState::new(
5615            config,
5616            Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new()),
5617            Arc::new(Sandbox::new(SandboxConfig::default(), Vec::new()).unwrap()),
5618            Arc::new(NullBus),
5619            None,
5620            Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::from_seed(&[9; 32])),
5621        )
5622        .unwrap()
5623    }

5640
5641    async fn occupy_admission_blocking_pool(
5642    ) -> (tokio::sync::oneshot::Sender<()>, tokio::task::JoinHandle<()>) {
5643        let (started_tx, started_rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel();
5644        let (release_tx, release_rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel();
5645        let task = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
5646            let _ = started_tx.send(());
5647            // Dropping the sender also releases this thread if a test panics.
5648            let _ = release_rx.blocking_recv();
5649        });
5650        started_rx.await.unwrap();
5651        (release_tx, task)
5652    }
5653
5654    async fn poll_admission_once<F: std::future::Future>(
5655        mut future: std::pin::Pin<&mut F>,
5656    ) -> std::task::Poll<F::Output> {
5657        std::future::poll_fn(|context| std::task::Poll::Ready(future.as_mut().poll(context))).await
5658    }
5659
5660    #[test]
5661    fn small_chat_admission_bypasses_saturated_blocking_pool() {
5662        admission_test_runtime().block_on(async {
5663            for authenticated in [false, true] {
5664                let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
5665                config.require_identity = authenticated;
5666                config.enforce_identity_scopes = authenticated;
5667                let mut state = state(config);
5668                let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
5669                headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("small-local-admission"));
5670                if authenticated {
5671                    let validator = IdentityValidator::new("proofvisor");
5672                    validator
5673                        .add_key(
5674                            "scope-key",
5675                            pv_identity::KeyMaterial::HmacSecret(b"scope-secret".to_vec()),
5676                        )
5677                        .unwrap();
5678                    state.identity = Some(Arc::new(validator));
5679                    headers.insert(
5680                        axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
5681                        format!("Bearer {}", scoped_token(&["chat:write"]))
5682                            .parse()
5683                            .unwrap(),
5684                    );
5685                }
5686                let caller = state.authenticate_chat_nonblocking(&headers).await.unwrap();
5687                let principal = caller.principal_digest();
5688                let mut other_principal = caller.clone();
5689                if let Some(validated) = &mut other_principal.validated {
5690                    validated.claims.sub = "another-principal".into();
5691                }
5692                let (release, blocker) = occupy_admission_blocking_pool().await;
5693                if !authenticated {
5694                    let mut authenticate = Box::pin(state.authenticate_chat_nonblocking(&headers));
5695                    assert!(matches!(
5696                        poll_admission_once(authenticate.as_mut()).await,
5697                        std::task::Poll::Ready(Ok(_))
5698                    ));
5699                    let mut duplicated = headers.clone();
5700                    duplicated.append(
5701                        axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
5702                        HeaderValue::from_static("Bearer a"),
5703                    );
5704                    duplicated.append(
5705                        axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
5706                        HeaderValue::from_static("Bearer b"),
5707                    );
5708                    let mut authenticate = Box::pin(state.authenticate_chat_nonblocking(&duplicated));
5709                    assert!(matches!(
5710                        poll_admission_once(authenticate.as_mut()).await,
5711                        std::task::Poll::Ready(Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { .. }))
5712                    ));
5713                }
5714                let body = payload();
5715                let body_bytes = body.to_string().len();
5716                let mut prepare =
5717                    Box::pin(state.prepare_authenticated_chat(&headers, body, body_bytes, caller));
5718                let outcome = poll_admission_once(prepare.as_mut()).await;
5719                let completed_inline = outcome.is_ready();
5720                release.send(()).unwrap();
5721                blocker.await.unwrap();
5722                let prepared = match outcome {
5723                    std::task::Poll::Ready(result) => result.unwrap(),
5724                    std::task::Poll::Pending => prepare.await.unwrap(),
5725                };
5726                assert!(
5727                    completed_inline,
5728                    "small local admission queued behind unrelated blocking work"
5729                );
5730                assert_eq!(prepared.session.principal_binding(), principal);
5731                if authenticated {
5732                    assert_eq!(
5733                        state
5734                            .metrics
5735                            .counter(
5736                                "pv_identity_validations_total",
5737                                "Full inbound identity validations"
5738                            )
5739                            .get(),
5740                        1
5741                    );
5742                    // The inline path must still refuse another principal on
5743                    // this session even when agent instance claims coincide.
5744                    let refused = state
5745                        .prepare_authenticated_chat(&headers, payload(), body_bytes, other_principal)
5746                        .await;
5747                    assert!(matches!(refused, Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized(_))));
5748                }
5749                drop(prepared);
5750                state.worker.wait_idle().await;
5751            }
5752        });
5753    }

5756    fn chat_preparation_offloads_large_bodies_and_both_quota_classes() {
5757        admission_test_runtime().block_on(async {
5758            for (session_quota, principal_quota, large_body) in [(true, false, false), (false, true, false), (false, false, true)] {
5759                let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
5760                if session_quota {
5761                    config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
5762                }
5763                if principal_quota {
5764                    config.principal_budget = Some(pv_state::BudgetSpec { max_tokens: Some(1_000_000), ..Default::default() });
5765                }
5766                let state = state(config);
5767                let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
5768                headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("offloaded-admission"));
5769                let caller = state.authenticate_chat_nonblocking(&headers).await.unwrap();
5770                let body = if large_body {
5771                    serde_json::json!({"model":"test", "messages":[{"role":"user", "content":"x".repeat(32 * 1024)}]})
5772                } else {
5773                    payload()
5774                };
5775                let body_bytes = body.to_string().len();
5776                let (release, blocker) = occupy_admission_blocking_pool().await;
5777                let mut prepare = Box::pin(state.prepare_authenticated_chat(&headers, body, body_bytes, caller));
5778                let outcome = poll_admission_once(prepare.as_mut()).await;
5779                let offloaded = outcome.is_pending();
5780                let sessions_before_release = state.sessions.len();
5781                release.send(()).unwrap();
5782                blocker.await.unwrap();
5783                let prepared = match outcome {
5784                    std::task::Poll::Ready(result) => result.unwrap(),
5785                    std::task::Poll::Pending => prepare.await.unwrap(),
5786                };
5787                assert!(offloaded, "large-body or quota preparation ran inline");
5788                assert_eq!(sessions_before_release, 0, "preparation ran before the blocking worker was available");
5789                drop(prepared);
5790                state.worker.wait_idle().await;
5791            }
5792        });
5793    }

5795    #[test]
5796    fn chat_route_passes_actual_body_size_to_admission() {
5797        use tower::ServiceExt as _;
5798
5799        admission_test_runtime().block_on(async {
5800            let state = state(HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp"));
5801            let body = serde_json::json!({
5802                "model": "test",
5803                "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "x".repeat(32 * 1024)}],
5804            })
5805            .to_string();
5806            let request = axum::http::Request::builder()
5807                .method("POST")
5808                .uri("/v1/chat/completions")
5809                .header("content-type", "application/json")
5810                .header(SESSION_HEADER, "large-route-admission")
5811                .body(axum::body::Body::from(body))
5812                .unwrap();
5813            let (release, blocker) = occupy_admission_blocking_pool().await;
5814            let mut request = Box::pin(crate::build_router(state.clone()).oneshot(request));
5815            let outcome = poll_admission_once(request.as_mut()).await;
5816            let offloaded = outcome.is_pending();
5817            // If the route passed zero instead of body.len(), preparation
5818            // would already have opened a session before forwarding blocked.
5819            let sessions_before_release = state.sessions.len();
5820            release.send(()).unwrap();
5821            blocker.await.unwrap();
5822            let response = match outcome {
5823                std::task::Poll::Ready(result) => result.unwrap(),
5824                std::task::Poll::Pending => request.await.unwrap(),
5825            };
5826            assert!(offloaded);
5827            assert_eq!(sessions_before_release, 0);
5828            assert_eq!(response.status(), axum::http::StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY);
5829            assert_eq!(state.sessions.len(), 1);
5830            state.worker.wait_idle().await;
5831        });
5832    }
5833
5834    #[test]
5835    fn unresolved_identity_never_runs_revocation_on_the_admission_task() {
5836        struct CountRevocations(std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize);
5837        impl pv_identity::RevocationStore for CountRevocations {
5838            fn revoke(&self, _jti: &str, _expires_at: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
5839                Ok(())
5840            }
5841            fn is_revoked(&self, _jti: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
5842                self.0.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
5843                Ok(false)
5844            }
5845        }
5846        admission_test_runtime().block_on(async {
5847            let revocations = Arc::new(CountRevocations(std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0)));
5848            let mut validator = IdentityValidator::new("proofvisor");
5849            validator
5850                .add_key(
5851                    "scope-key",
5852                    pv_identity::KeyMaterial::HmacSecret(b"scope-secret".to_vec()),
5853                )
5854                .unwrap();
5855            validator.set_revocation_store(revocations.clone());
5856            let mut state = state(HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp"));
5857            state.identity = Some(Arc::new(validator));
5858            let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
5859            headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("unresolved-admission"));
5860            headers.insert(
5861                axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
5862                format!("Bearer {}", scoped_token(&["chat:write"]))
5863                    .parse()
5864                    .unwrap(),
5865            );
5866            let (release, blocker) = occupy_admission_blocking_pool().await;
5867            let mut authenticate = Box::pin(state.authenticate_chat_nonblocking(&headers));
5868            let outcome = poll_admission_once(authenticate.as_mut()).await;
5869            let offloaded = outcome.is_pending();
5870            let calls_before_release = revocations.0.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
5871            release.send(()).unwrap();
5872            blocker.await.unwrap();
5873            let _caller = match outcome {
5874                std::task::Poll::Ready(result) => result.unwrap(),
5875                std::task::Poll::Pending => authenticate.await.unwrap(),
5876            };
5877            assert!(offloaded);
5878            assert_eq!(calls_before_release, 0);
5879            assert_eq!(revocations.0.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst), 1);
5880
5881            let (release, blocker) = occupy_admission_blocking_pool().await;
5882            let body = payload();
5883            let body_bytes = body.to_string().len();
5884            let mut prepare = Box::pin(state.prepare_chat_nonblocking(&headers, body, body_bytes, None));
5885            let outcome = poll_admission_once(prepare.as_mut()).await;
5886            let offloaded = outcome.is_pending();
5887            let calls_before_release = revocations.0.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
5888            release.send(()).unwrap();
5889            blocker.await.unwrap();
5890            let prepared = match outcome {
5891                std::task::Poll::Ready(result) => result.unwrap(),
5892                std::task::Poll::Pending => prepare.await.unwrap(),
5893            };
5894            assert!(offloaded);
5895            assert_eq!(calls_before_release, 1);
5896            assert_eq!(revocations.0.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst), 2);
5897            drop(prepared);
5898            state.worker.wait_idle().await;
5899        });
5900    }
5901
5902    async fn trip_loop(state: &AppState, headers: &HeaderMap, repeated: &Value) {
5903        for expected in 1..=4u64 {
5904            let mut prepared = state.prepare_chat(headers, repeated.clone(), None).unwrap();
5905            // This helper abandons the prepared request without forwarding.
5906            // Defuse the capture guard so its terminal failure job does not
5907            // pad the chain — the chain-count waits below must count only
5908            // the admission jobs, or the analyze jobs race the assertion.
5909            prepared.capture_guard.defuse();
5910            drop(prepared);
5911            let session = state.sessions.get("loop-session").unwrap();
5912            // Generous budget: the chain append rides an async worker job and
5913            // must survive heavily loaded parallel test runs.
5914            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
5915                while session.chain.lock().count() < expected {
5916                    tokio::task::yield_now().await;
5917                }
5918            })
5919            .await
5920            .unwrap();
5921        }
5922    }

5924    #[tokio::test]
5925    async fn prepare_binds_session_and_stays_below_hot_path_budget() {
5926        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
5927        let state = state(config);
5928        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
5929        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("session-1"));
5930
5931        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
5932        assert_eq!(prepared.session.id, "session-1");
5933        // Debug-build ceiling: 100ms is 3 orders of magnitude above the release
5934        // SLA (~33us p95); a regression an order of magnitude worse still trips
5935        // this. The strict perf gate lives in the SLA bench suite.
5936        assert!(
5937            prepared.middleware_us < 100_000,
5938            "middleware took {}us",
5939            prepared.middleware_us
5940        );
5941        assert!(state.metrics.render().contains("pv_stage_duration_seconds"));
5942    }

5955    #[tokio::test]
5956    async fn token_quota_blocks_before_dispatch() {
5957        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
5958        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1);
5959        let state = state(config);
5960        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
5961        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("quota-failure"));
5962        let error = match state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None) {
5963            Ok(_) => panic!("over-budget request was accepted"),
5964            Err(error) => error,
5965        };
5966        assert!(matches!(error, PipelineError::Blocked { .. }));
5967        assert_eq!(state.sessions.len(), 1);
5968        let session = state.sessions.get("quota-failure").unwrap();
5969        tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
5970            while session.atif_steps_count() == 0 {
5971                tokio::task::yield_now().await;

5974        .await
5975        .unwrap();
