5977
5978    /// The principal-scoped budget (§3.2 fix) binds across rotating
5979    /// X-PV-Session headers: sessions A and B pointed at the same
5980    /// principal share one ledger. Without the principal budget, a
5981    /// caller could reset the session bucket per request and never
5982    /// hit `max_tokens`.
5983    #[tokio::test]
5984    async fn principal_budget_binds_across_rotated_session_headers() {
5985        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
5986        // Wide session budget so it never binds — we're proving the
5987        // *principal* ledger is what refuses the second request.
5988        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
5989        // The test payload approximates to ~30 tokens (roughly 2 tokens per
5990        // ASCII word + one per punctuation glyph, on the ~60-char JSON body).
5991        // A cap of 40 admits the first request and refuses the second under
5992        // the same principal — the anti-rotation property in one assertion.
5993        config.principal_budget = Some(pv_state::BudgetSpec {
5994            max_tokens: Some(40),
5995            ..pv_state::BudgetSpec::default()
5996        });
5997        let state = state(config);
5998
5999        // First request under session A: fits the principal budget.
6000        let mut headers_a = HeaderMap::new();
6001        headers_a.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("rotated-a"));
6002        let first = state.prepare_chat(&headers_a, payload(), None);
6003        assert!(first.is_ok(), "first principal request must be admitted");
6004        // Mark the admission dispatched before dropping: a dropped
6005        // UNdispatched request now (correctly) refunds via
6006        // `AdmissionDebit`'s cancellation guard, which would empty the
6007        // very ledger this test needs to stay charged.
6008        if let Ok(mut prepared) = first {
6009            let _ = prepared.admission_debit.take_for_dispatch();
6010        }
6011
6012        // Second request under a DIFFERENT session id but the same
6013        // principal (anonymous — both requests unauthenticated). The
6014        // session ledger for `rotated-b` is virgin; only the principal
6015        // ledger can refuse. Refusal proves the header-rotation attack
6016        // is closed.
6017        let mut headers_b = HeaderMap::new();
6018        headers_b.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("rotated-b"));
6019        match state.prepare_chat(&headers_b, payload(), None) {
6020            Ok(_) => panic!("header rotation defeated principal budget — the fix from §3.2 has regressed"),
6021            Err(PipelineError::Blocked { context: msg, .. }) => {
6022                assert!(
6023                    msg.contains("principal."),
6024                    "refusal must name the principal-scoped limit, got {msg:?}"
6025                );
6026            }
6027            Err(other) => panic!("unexpected refusal shape {other:?}"),
6028        }
6029    }
6030
6031    /// `abandon_prepared` runs strictly pre-dispatch, so its refund must
6032    /// mirror the DUAL-ledger admission debit. Pre-fix it refunded only
6033    /// the session ledger: every pre-dispatch abort (durable-capture
6034    /// failure, breaker-open, marker-write failure) permanently drained
6035    /// principal tokens for zero LLM work — under header rotation the
6036    /// principal ledger is the only one that binds, so aborts alone
6037    /// could exhaust an identity's budget.
6038    #[tokio::test]
6039    async fn abandon_prepared_refunds_the_principal_ledger_too() {
6040        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6041        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
6042        // Fits ONE ~30-token payload; a leaked (unrefunded) debit makes
6043        // the next same-principal request refuse.
6044        config.principal_budget = Some(pv_state::BudgetSpec {
6045            max_tokens: Some(40),
6046            ..pv_state::BudgetSpec::default()
6047        });
6048        let state = state(config);
6049
6050        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6051        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("abandon-a"));
6052        let mut prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
6053        state.abandon_prepared(&mut prepared, StopReason::Other, "test abandon");
6054        drop(prepared);
6055
6056        // Same (anonymous) principal, different session: only the
6057        // principal ledger can refuse here. It must not — the abandoned
6058        // request's debit was refunded on both ledgers.
6059        let mut headers_b = HeaderMap::new();
6060        headers_b.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("abandon-b"));
6061        let second = state.prepare_chat(&headers_b, payload(), None);
6062        assert!(
6063            second.is_ok(),
6064            "an abandoned request must not consume principal budget; got {:?}",
6065            second.err()
6066        );
6067    }
6068
6069    /// Axum drops the request future wholesale on client disconnect —
6070    /// no explicit abort path runs. The admission debit must refund via
6071    /// `AdmissionDebit`'s Drop (both ledgers), or every mid-admission
6072    /// disconnect permanently drains the never-healing principal
6073    /// ledger with zero LLM work.
6074    #[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 2)]
6075    async fn dropping_a_prepared_request_refunds_both_ledgers() {
6076        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6077        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
6078        // Fits ONE ~30-token payload; a leaked (unrefunded) debit makes
6079        // the next same-principal request refuse.
6080        config.principal_budget = Some(pv_state::BudgetSpec {
6081            max_tokens: Some(40),
6082            ..pv_state::BudgetSpec::default()
6083        });
6084        let state = state(config);
6085
6086        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6087        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("cancelled-a"));
6088        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
6089        // Simulate the cancelled future: drop with no explicit abort.
6090        drop(prepared);
6091
6092        // The Drop refund runs on the blocking pool; poll until it lands.
6093        let mut headers_b = HeaderMap::new();
6094        headers_b.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("cancelled-b"));
6095        let mut second = state.prepare_chat(&headers_b, payload(), None);
6096        for _ in 0..200 {
6097            if second.is_ok() {
6098                break;
6099            }
6100            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
6101            second = state.prepare_chat(&headers_b, payload(), None);
6102        }
6103        assert!(
6104            second.is_ok(),
6105            "a dropped (cancelled) prepared request must refund the principal ledger; got {:?}",
6106            second.err()
6107        );
6108    }
6109
6110    /// The refund window ends at upstream dispatch: once
6111    /// `take_for_dispatch` ran (immediately before `send()`), dropping
6112    /// the request must NOT refund — the request may have reached the
6113    /// provider, and the debit conservatively stands (mirroring the
6114    /// `is_connect`-only refund on send errors).
6115    #[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 2)]
6116    async fn dispatched_debit_stands_when_the_request_drops() {
6117        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6118        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
6119        config.principal_budget = Some(pv_state::BudgetSpec {
6120            max_tokens: Some(40),
6121            ..pv_state::BudgetSpec::default()
6122        });
6123        let state = state(config);
6124
6125        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6126        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("dispatched-a"));
6127        let mut prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
6128        let debited = prepared.admission_debit.take_for_dispatch();
6129        assert!(debited > 0, "admission must have debited tokens");
6130        drop(prepared);
6131
6132        // Give a hypothetical (buggy) Drop refund time to land, then
6133        // verify the principal ledger still holds the debit.
6134        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
6135        let mut headers_b = HeaderMap::new();
6136        headers_b.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("dispatched-b"));
6137        assert!(
6138            matches!(
6139                state.prepare_chat(&headers_b, payload(), None),
6140                Err(PipelineError::Blocked { .. })
6141            ),
6142            "a dispatched request's debit must stand after drop"
6143        );
6144    }
6145
6146    /// The relayed-header single-value checks must run BEFORE the
6147    /// budget debit: pre-fix, `single_header`'s `?` fired from the
6148    /// `PreparedRequest` construction after BOTH ledgers were debited,
6149    /// with no refund arm — a client repeating a duplicate
6150    /// `openai-beta` header drained budgets without one upstream call.
6151    #[tokio::test]
6152    async fn duplicate_passthrough_header_is_refused_without_consuming_budget() {
6153        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6154        // Fits ONE ~30-token payload: a pre-refusal debit leak makes the
6155        // follow-up legitimate request refuse.
6156        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(40);
6157        let state = state(config);
6158
6159        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6160        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("dup-header"));
6161        headers.append("openai-beta", HeaderValue::from_static("assistants=v1"));
6162        headers.append("openai-beta", HeaderValue::from_static("assistants=v2"));
6163        assert!(
6164            matches!(
6165                state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None),
6166                Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { .. })
6167            ),
6168            "duplicate allowlisted headers must be refused"
6169        );
6170
6171        // The refusal must not have consumed the session budget.
6172        let mut clean = HeaderMap::new();
6173        clean.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("dup-header"));
6174        clean.insert("openai-beta", HeaderValue::from_static("assistants=v2"));
6175        let second = state.prepare_chat(&clean, payload(), None);
6176        assert!(
6177            second.is_ok(),
6178            "a header-refused request must not consume budget; got {:?}",
6179            second.err()
6180        );
6181    }

6234        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");

6236        config.breaker.action = BreakerAction::Inject;

6239        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("loop-session"));
6240        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));

6255        assert_eq!(prepared.session.loop_state.state(), BreakerState::Closed);

6276
6277    /// A breaker trip used to replace the job's Compression class with
6278    /// StopReason *before* the accounting decisions ran, so the tripped
6279    /// admission's prompt tokens vanished from the session totals (and the
6280    /// journal record) — receipts undercounted exactly the runaway sessions
6281    /// the breaker exists to attest. Accounting must key on the submitted
6282    /// class, not the swapped one.
6283    #[tokio::test]
6284    async fn breaker_trip_does_not_drop_the_admissions_prompt_token_accounting() {
6285        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6286        config.breaker.min_tokens = 0;
6287        let state = state(config);
6288        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6289        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("loop-session"));
6290        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
6291        let repeated = serde_json::json!({
6292            "model": "test",
6293            "messages": [{
6294                "role": "assistant",
6295                "content": "I should check the database again for pending orders"
6296            }]
6297        });
6298        // Four identical admissions, tracking chain growth like `trip_loop`;
6299        // the breaker trips on one of the later worker jobs. Capture the
6300        // per-admission token amount after the first job lands.
6301        let mut per_request = 0u64;
6302        for expected in 1..=4u64 {
6303            state.prepare_chat(&headers, repeated.clone(), None).unwrap();
6304            let session = state.sessions.get("loop-session").unwrap();
6305            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
6306                while session.chain.lock().count() < expected {
6307                    tokio::task::yield_now().await;
6308                }
6309            })
6310            .await
6311            .unwrap();
6312            if expected == 1 {
6313                tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), session.wait_for_worker_jobs())
6314                    .await
6315                    .unwrap();
6316                per_request = session
6317                    .totals
6318                    .prompt_tokens
6319                    .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire);
6320                assert!(per_request > 0, "admission must account prompt tokens");
6321            }
6322        }
6323        let session = state.sessions.get("loop-session").unwrap();
6324        tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), session.wait_for_worker_jobs())
6325            .await
6326            .unwrap();
6327        assert_eq!(
6328            session.loop_state.state(),
6329            BreakerState::Open,
6330            "precondition: the breaker must have tripped during the four admissions",
6331        );
6332        assert_eq!(
6333            session
6334                .totals
6335                .prompt_tokens
6336                .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire),
6337            per_request * 4,
6338            "every admission's prompt tokens must be accounted, including the one whose \
6339             worker job carried the breaker trip",
6340        );
6341    }
6342
6343    /// Budget counters are dead weight once a session is sealed (admission
6344    /// rejects closed sessions before any quota check); finalization must
6345    /// clear them or the in-memory state store grows by a few cells per
6346    /// client-chosen session id forever.
6347    #[tokio::test]
6348    async fn finalization_clears_the_sessions_budget_counters() {
6349        let store: Arc<dyn StateStore> = Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new());
6350        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6351        config.budget.max_tokens = Some(1_000_000);
6352        config.atif_spool_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap().keep().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
6353        let state = AppState::new(
6354            config,
6355            Arc::clone(&store),
6356            Arc::new(Sandbox::new(SandboxConfig::default(), Vec::new()).unwrap()),
6357            Arc::new(NullBus),
6358            None,
6359            Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::from_seed(&[9; 32])),
6360        )
6361        .unwrap();
6362        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6363        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("budget-cleanup"));
6364        let mut prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
6365        // Root cause of the intermittent CI-only failure (3× as of
6366        // 2026-09-08, never in 40+ local runs): dropping the prepared
6367        // request arms AdmissionDebit::drop, whose refund runs on the
6368        // blocking pool and races the precondition read below — on a
6369        // loaded runner the refund won and `observed` read Ok(0). The
6370        // race also made the FINAL assert vacuous: a drop-refund zeroes
6371        // the cells with no help from the finalizer. Disarm the debit
6372        // exactly like a real dispatch (`take_for_dispatch`, the
6373        // :4021 idiom) so the cells stay genuinely spent until
6374        // `close_session` clears them — the behavior this test pins.
6375        let _ = prepared.admission_debit.take_for_dispatch();
6376        drop(prepared);
6377        let tokens_key = format!(
6378            "{}tokens",
6379            pv_state::ActionBudget::session_prefix("budget-cleanup")
6380        );
6381        let observed = store.get(&tokens_key);
6382        assert!(
6383            observed.as_ref().copied().unwrap_or(0) > 0,
6384            "precondition: admission must have spent from the token budget \
6385             (key {tokens_key:?}, observed {observed:?})",
6386        );
6387        let session = state.sessions.get("budget-cleanup").unwrap();
6388        session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
6389        state
6390            .finalizer
6391            .close_session(session, StopReason::SessionClosed)
6392            .await
6393            .unwrap();
6394        assert_eq!(
6395            store.get(&tokens_key).unwrap(),
6396            0,
6397            "finalization must remove the sealed session's budget counters",
6398        );
6399    }

6425    /// The semantic-loop breaker's hard-stop actions (Reject/Abort) must
6426    /// gate TOOL admission exactly like they gate chat: pre-fix, a
6427    /// breaker-open session — whose chat requests were already refused —
6428    /// kept executing otherwise-permitted MCP tool calls on the same
6429    /// session until idle close, so the "stop the runaway agent"
6430    /// decision only stopped half the agent.
6431    #[tokio::test]
6432    async fn open_breaker_hard_stop_refuses_tool_calls() {
6433        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6434        let state = state(config);
6435        let identity = pv_events::AgentIdentity {
6436            version: "dev".into(),
6437            charter: "anonymous".into(),
6438            instance_uid: "anonymous".into(),
6439            ttl_remaining_s: None,
6440        };
6441        let session = state.sessions.get_or_open(
6442            "breaker-tools",
6443            crate::session::Workflow::Unsigned,
6444            &identity,
6445            &state.config.breaker,
6446        );
6447        // Trip the breaker directly: identical embeddings above the
6448        // min-token floor are zero-novelty steps; the default window
6449        // trips after three.
6450        for _ in 0..4 {
6451            session.loop_state.observe_embedding(vec![1.0, 2.0, 3.0], 2_000);
6452        }
6453        assert_eq!(
6454            session.loop_state.state(),
6455            BreakerState::Open,
6456            "precondition: breaker must be open",
6457        );
6458
6459        let raw = serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::json!({
6460            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
6461            "id": 1,
6462            "method": "tools/call",
6463            "params": {"name": "db_read", "arguments": {}}
6464        }))
6465        .unwrap();
6466        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6467        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("breaker-tools"));
6468        let refusal = state.intercept_tool(&headers, &raw);
6469        assert!(
6470            matches!(&refusal, Err(PipelineError::Blocked { context, .. }) if context.contains("circuit breaker")),
6471            "an open hard-stop breaker must refuse tool execution, got {refusal:?}",
6472        );
6473        assert!(
6474            session.enforcement_tripped(),
6475            "the tool-path refusal latches enforcement like the chat path",
6476        );
6477    }

6499    /// `intercept_tool_durable` used to re-derive the session id from headers

6502    /// session that never existed and the call always failed with "tool
6503    /// session disappeared" — after the verdict had already been computed and
6504    /// audited under the real (generated) session.
6505    #[tokio::test]
6506    async fn intercept_tool_durable_works_without_a_session_header() {

6525
6526    /// When `prepare_chat_durable` refuses a request AFTER admission (the
6527    /// request's own loop analysis opened the breaker during the audit wait),
6528    /// it must submit the terminal failure record for the response attempt
6529    /// journaled at admission. Otherwise the active journal ends with a
6530    /// dangling non-terminal attempt and a later crash-recovery scan
6531    /// quarantines the session over a request the client already saw fail.
6532    #[tokio::test]
6533    async fn durable_breaker_refusal_journals_a_terminal_response_attempt() {
6534        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6535        config.breaker.min_tokens = 0;
6536        let state = state(config);
6537        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6538        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("durable-loop"));
6539        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
6540        let repeated = serde_json::json!({
6541            "model": "test",
6542            "messages": [{
6543                "role": "assistant",
6544                "content": "I should check the database again for pending orders"
6545            }],
6546        });
6547        // Attempts the test itself abandons after a successful prepare: the
6548        // capture guard terminates them on drop (same as a client
6549        // disconnect), so the journal must end with NO dangling attempts.
6550        let mut abandoned_by_test = std::collections::HashSet::new();
6551        let mut refusal = None;
6552        for _ in 0..8 {
6553            match state.prepare_chat_durable(&headers, repeated.clone()).await {
6554                Ok(prepared) => {
6555                    abandoned_by_test.insert(prepared.capture_guard.attempt_id().to_owned());
6556                }
6557                Err(error) => {
6558                    refusal = Some(error);
6559                    break;
6560                }
6561            }
6562        }
6563        assert!(
6564            matches!(refusal, Some(PipelineError::Blocked { .. })),
6565            "the breaker must refuse during the audit wait, got {refusal:?}",
6566        );
6567        assert!(
6568            !abandoned_by_test.is_empty(),
6569            "at least one prepare must succeed first"
6570        );
6571        let session = state.sessions.get("durable-loop").unwrap();
6572        session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
6573
6574        let digest = pv_core::digest::sha256_hex("durable-loop".as_bytes());
6575        let stem = digest.get(..32).unwrap();
6576        let journal_path =
6577            std::path::Path::new(&state.config.atif_spool_dir).join(format!("{stem}.events.ndjson"));
6578        let journal = std::fs::read_to_string(&journal_path).unwrap();
6579        let mut dangling = std::collections::HashSet::new();
6580        for (index, line) in journal.lines().enumerate() {
6581            let record: crate::worker::ActiveJournalRecord = crate::journal::open(
6582                &state.journal_key,
6583                "durable-loop:active",
6584                index as u64,
6585                line.as_bytes(),
6586            )
6587            .unwrap();
6588            if let Some(attempt) = record.response_attempt {
6589                if attempt.terminal {
6590                    assert!(
6591                        dangling.remove(&attempt.id),
6592                        "terminal record for attempt {} has no admission record",
6593                        attempt.id,
6594                    );
6595                } else {
6596                    dangling.insert(attempt.id);
6597                }
6598            }
6599        }
6600        assert!(
6601            dangling.is_empty(),
6602            "every response attempt must be terminated: the refusal terminates its own \
6603             attempt, and dropping a PreparedRequest (client disconnect) terminates via \
6604             the capture guard; still dangling: {dangling:?} (test dropped: {abandoned_by_test:?})",
6605        );
6606    }
6607
6608    /// `forward_chat` must fail closed when the in-flight response marker
6609    /// cannot be persisted: the marker is what lets the restart-time scan
6610    /// (`inflight_response_sessions`) quarantine sessions whose provider
6611    /// response may have been observed but never captured. Dispatching
6612    /// without it would silently drop that crash-durability guarantee.
6613    /// The refusal must also terminate the journaled response attempt so
6614    /// no dangling non-terminal record quarantines the session later.
6615    #[tokio::test]
6616    async fn forward_chat_fails_closed_when_marker_cannot_persist() {
6617        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6618        let state = state(config);
6619        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6620        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("marker-outage"));
6621        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
6622        let attempt_id = prepared.capture_guard.attempt_id().to_owned();
6623        // Sabotage the marker directory: a regular file at
6624        // <spool>/inflight-responses makes `create_dir_all` inside
6625        // `write_atomic` fail for every marker write.
6626        std::fs::write(
6627            std::path::Path::new(&state.config.atif_spool_dir).join(crate::spool::INFLIGHT_RESPONSES),
6628            b"",
6629        )
6630        .unwrap();
6631        let error = state.forward_chat(prepared).await.err().unwrap();
6632        // Unavailable (not Upstream) proves the refusal happened BEFORE
6633        // any upstream dispatch was attempted.
6634        assert!(
6635            matches!(error, PipelineError::Unavailable { .. }),
6636            "marker persist failure must map to Unavailable, got {error:?}",
6637        );
6638
6639        let session = state.sessions.get("marker-outage").unwrap();
6640        session.wait_for_worker_jobs().await;
6641        let digest = pv_core::digest::sha256_hex("marker-outage".as_bytes());
6642        let stem = digest.get(..32).unwrap();
6643        let journal_path =
6644            std::path::Path::new(&state.config.atif_spool_dir).join(format!("{stem}.events.ndjson"));
6645        let journal = std::fs::read_to_string(&journal_path).unwrap();
6646        let mut saw_admission = false;
6647        let mut saw_terminal = false;
6648        for (index, line) in journal.lines().enumerate() {
6649            let record: crate::worker::ActiveJournalRecord = crate::journal::open(
6650                &state.journal_key,
6651                "marker-outage:active",
6652                index as u64,
6653                line.as_bytes(),
6654            )
6655            .unwrap();
6656            if let Some(attempt) = record.response_attempt {
6657                if attempt.id == attempt_id {
6658                    if attempt.terminal {
6659                        saw_terminal = true;
6660                    } else {
6661                        saw_admission = true;
6662                    }
6663                }
6664            }
6665        }
6666        assert!(saw_admission, "admission must have journaled the attempt");
6667        assert!(
6668            saw_terminal,
6669            "the refused request's response attempt must be terminated in the journal",
6670        );
6671    }
6672
6673    /// A session that only routes MCP tool calls (no chat completions) must
6674    /// still be considered active by `idle_sessions` — otherwise the
6675    /// reconciler tick force-closes tool-only sessions after
6676    /// `session_idle_close_s` and every subsequent tool call fails with
6677    /// "session is already closed". `prepare_chat` refreshes
6678    /// `last_activity_ms` via `touch()`; `intercept_tool` did not.
6679    #[tokio::test]
6680    async fn intercept_tool_refreshes_session_activity() {
6681        use std::sync::atomic::Ordering as AtomicOrdering;
6682
6683        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6684        let state = state(config);
6685        let raw = serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::json!({
6686            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
6687            "id": 1,
6688            "method": "tools/call",
6689            "params": {"name": "safe_tool", "arguments": {}}
6690        }))
6691        .unwrap();
6692        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6693        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("tool-only-session"));
6694        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
6695
6696        state.intercept_tool(&headers, &raw).unwrap();
6697        let session = state.sessions.get("tool-only-session").unwrap();
6698        // Backdate the session to before the epoch's first second — this stands
6699        // in for a real session that has been quiet long enough to hit the
6700        // idle sweeper's cutoff.
6701        session.last_activity_ms.store(0, AtomicOrdering::Release);
6702
6703        state.intercept_tool(&headers, &raw).unwrap();
6704
6705        let refreshed = session.last_activity_ms.load(AtomicOrdering::Acquire);
6706        assert!(
6707            refreshed > 0,
6708            "intercept_tool must refresh last_activity_ms so a tool-only session is not force-closed by the idle sweeper — got {refreshed}",
6709        );
6710    }
6711
6712    /// `intercept_tool` used to call `sandbox.check` unconditionally and then
6713    /// post-hoc override an `Allowed` verdict to `Blocked` when the workflow
6714    /// gate vetoes a consequential tool on an unsigned session. But
6715    /// `sandbox.check`'s budget gate (`try_spend_many`) spends before the
6716    /// override runs, so a legitimate client asking for a consequential tool
6717    /// on the wrong workflow got their `max_total_tool_calls` counter
6718    /// decremented for a call the sandbox never actually authorized. Once the
6719    /// client upgraded to a signed session to make the call for real, they
6720    /// hit the cap short of the number they were configured for.
6721    #[tokio::test]
6722    async fn unsigned_consequential_veto_does_not_spend_budget() {
6723        let store: Arc<dyn StateStore> = Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new());
6724        let sandbox_config = SandboxConfig {
6725            budget: pv_state::BudgetSpec {
6726                max_total_tool_calls: Some(5),
6727                ..pv_state::BudgetSpec::default()
6728            },
6729            ..SandboxConfig::default()
6730        };
6731        let sandbox = Arc::new(Sandbox::new(sandbox_config, Vec::new()).unwrap());
6732        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6733        config.atif_spool_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap().keep().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
6734        let state = AppState::new(
6735            config,
6736            Arc::clone(&store),
6737            sandbox,
6738            Arc::new(NullBus),
6739            None,
6740            Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::from_seed(&[9; 32])),
6741        )
6742        .unwrap();
6743
6744        let raw = serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::json!({
6745            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
6746            "id": 1,
6747            "method": "tools/call",
6748            "params": {"name": "db_write", "arguments": {}}
6749        }))
6750        .unwrap();
6751        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6752        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("unsigned-consequential"));
6753        // Default workflow is unsigned; no explicit x-pv-workflow header.
6754
6755        let verdict = state.intercept_tool(&headers, &raw).unwrap();
6756        assert!(
6757            matches!(verdict, ToolVerdict::Blocked { stage: "policy", .. }),
6758            "consequential tool on unsigned workflow must block at the policy gate; got {verdict:?}",
6759        );
6760
6761        let session_digest = pv_core::digest::sha256_hex("unsigned-consequential".as_bytes());
6762        let key = format!("budget:{{{}}}:total_calls", session_digest.get(..32).unwrap());
6763        let count = store.get(&key).unwrap();
6764        assert_eq!(
6765            count, 0,
6766            "sandbox.check must not spend the budget for a call the workflow gate vetoes — otherwise a legitimate client that upgrades to signed and retries hits the cap short of the configured limit",
6767        );
6768    }
6769
6770    #[tokio::test]
6771    async fn full_capture_queue_fails_closed_before_upstream() {
6772        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6773        // The fused permit split (worker_capacity + response_capacity)
6774        // gives distinct exhaustion counters but keeps the *effective*
6775        // total admission the same as before, because response
6776        // capture doesn't hold an mpsc slot up front — only the
6777        // response semaphore. So with worker_channel_capacity=2 the
6778        // first request consumes 1 worker semaphore + 1 response
6779        // semaphore + 1 mpsc slot; the second request consumes the
6780        // remaining worker semaphore + response semaphore + mpsc slot;
6781        // the third would fail. Set to 1 for a clean single-request
6782        // saturation.
6783        config.worker_channel_capacity = 1;
6784        config.breaker.min_tokens = u64::MAX;
6785        let sink = Arc::new(BlockingSink {
6786            entered: AtomicBool::new(false),
6787            release: tokio::sync::Notify::new(),
6788        });
6789        let state = AppState::new_with_backends(
6790            config,
6791            Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new()),
6792            Arc::new(Sandbox::new(SandboxConfig::default(), Vec::new()).unwrap()),
6793            Arc::new(NullBus),
6794            None,
6795            Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::from_seed(&[10; 32])),
6796            Arc::new(HashEmbedder::default()),
6797            sink.clone(),
6798        )
6799        .unwrap();
6800        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6801        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("overload"));
6802        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
6803        let mut agent_payload = payload();
6804        agent_payload["messages"][0]["role"] = Value::String("assistant".to_owned());
6805        let mut first = state.prepare_chat(&headers, agent_payload.clone(), None).unwrap();
6806        // Abandoning without forwarding: defuse the guard, or its terminal
6807        // job (refused by the size-1 queue) marks the session
6808        // capture-failed and the worker skips the sink this test blocks on.
6809        first.capture_guard.defuse();
6810        drop(first);
6811        tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
6812            while !sink.entered.load(AtomicOrdering::Acquire) {
6813                tokio::task::yield_now().await;
6814            }
6815        })
6816        .await
6817        .unwrap();
6818        assert!(matches!(
6819            state.prepare_chat(&headers, agent_payload, None),
6820            Err(PipelineError::Unavailable { .. })
6821        ));
6822        sink.release.notify_waiters();
6823    }

6851    /// `messages` field with the wrong type used to return
6852    /// the same "chat payload has no messages" as an absent field,
6853    /// steering support tickets at a phantom bug. Verify each class
6854    /// now returns a discriminating error message.
6855    #[test]
6856    fn atif_capture_rejects_non_array_messages_with_precise_diagnostics() {
6857        fn bad_request_message(payload: serde_json::Value) -> String {
6858            match atif_capture_from_request(&payload) {
6859                Ok(_) => panic!("expected BadRequest, got Ok"),
6860                Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { context: msg, .. }) => msg,
6861                Err(other) => panic!("expected BadRequest, got {other:?}"),
6862            }
6863        }
6864        // (1) Field entirely missing (e.g., OpenAI Responses API caller
6865        //     who used `input` instead of `messages`).
6866        let msg = bad_request_message(serde_json::json!({
6867            "input": [{ "role": "user", "content": "hi" }]
6868        }));
6869        assert!(msg.contains("missing 'messages'"), "got {msg}");
6870        // (2) Field present but wrong JSON shape — object.
6871        let msg = bad_request_message(serde_json::json!({
6872            "messages": { "0": { "role": "user", "content": "hi" } }
6873        }));
6874        assert!(msg.contains("must be a JSON array"), "got {msg}");
6875        assert!(msg.contains("object"), "got {msg}");
6876        // (3) Field present, is an array, but empty.
6877        let msg = bad_request_message(serde_json::json!({ "messages": [] }));
6878        assert!(msg.contains("empty"), "got {msg}");
6879    }

6881    /// Multiple X-PV-Session (or X-PV-Workflow) headers on
6882    /// a single request must be refused. HeaderMap::get() returns
6883    /// only the first, so an intermediary that merges duplicates on
6884    /// the wire and downstream code that reads them separately can
6885    /// disagree on which session id was in effect — a header
6886    /// smuggling desync. Refuse loudly at ingress.
6887    #[test]
6888    fn duplicate_x_pv_session_header_is_refused() {
6889        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6890        headers.append(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("sessA"));
6891        headers.append(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("sessB"));
6892        match session_id(&headers) {
6893            Ok(id) => panic!("expected duplicate-header refusal, got session_id {id:?}"),
6894            Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { context: msg, .. }) => {
6895                assert!(msg.contains("more than one"), "got {msg}");
6896                assert!(msg.contains("x-pv-session"), "got {msg}");
6897            }
6898            Err(other) => panic!("expected BadRequest, got {other:?}"),
6899        }
6900    }

6903    fn duplicate_x_pv_workflow_header_is_refused() {
6904        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6905        headers.append(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
6906        headers.append(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("unsigned"));
6907        match workflow(&headers, "signed") {
6908            Ok(w) => panic!("expected duplicate-header refusal, got workflow {w:?}"),
6909            Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { context: msg, .. }) => {
6910                assert!(msg.contains("more than one"), "got {msg}");
6911                assert!(msg.contains("x-pv-workflow"), "got {msg}");
6912            }
6913            Err(other) => panic!("expected BadRequest, got {other:?}"),
6914        }
6915    }

6926    /// Identity hot path (`resolve_identity`) must refuse
6927    /// duplicate `Authorization` headers, symmetric with X-PV-Session.
6928    /// Previously `HeaderMap::get(AUTHORIZATION)` returned the first
6929    /// value only — the harness would authenticate as `A` while
6930    /// log aggregators / WAFs seeing a merged `A, B` form would
6931    /// attribute the request to `B`. This is the same identity split-brain
6932    /// already closed for session headers.

6934    async fn resolve_identity_refuses_duplicate_authorization_header() {

6938            axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
6939            HeaderValue::from_static("Bearer aaaa"),
6940        );
6941        headers.append(
6942            axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
6943            HeaderValue::from_static("Bearer bbbb"),
6944        );
6945        match state.resolve_identity(&headers, None) {
6946            Ok(_) => panic!("expected duplicate-header refusal, got Ok"),
6947            Err(PipelineError::BadRequest { context: msg, .. }) => {
6948                assert!(msg.contains("more than one"), "got {msg}");
6949                assert!(msg.contains("authorization"), "got {msg}");
6950            }
6951            Err(other) => panic!("expected BadRequest, got {other:?}"),
6952        }
6953    }

6975    /// With `ignore_client_authorization`
6976    /// opted in and no validator configured, a stock OpenAI SDK's
6977    /// mandatory `Authorization` header must be accepted-and-discarded
6978    /// — the request proceeds anonymously instead of hard-401ing on
6979    /// request one. Without the opt-in, the diagnostic 401 stays.
6980    #[tokio::test]
6981    async fn ignore_client_authorization_accepts_and_discards_sdk_bearer() {
6982        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
6983        headers.insert(
6984            axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
6985            HeaderValue::from_static("Bearer sk-test-openai-sdk-key"),
6986        );
6987
6988        // Default (flag off): diagnostic 401.
6989        let refused = null_state().resolve_identity(&headers, None);
6990        assert!(
6991            matches!(&refused, Err(PipelineError::Unauthorized(_))),
6992            "bearer with no validator and no opt-in must stay 401; got {refused:?}"
6993        );
6994
6995        // Flag on: accepted as anonymous.
6996        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
6997        config.require_identity = false;
6998        config.ignore_client_authorization = true;
6999        let accepted = state(config).resolve_identity(&headers, None);
7000        match accepted {
7001            Ok(identity) => {
7002                assert_eq!(identity.instance_uid, "anonymous");
7003            }
7004            Err(error) => {
7005                panic!("ignore_client_authorization must accept the SDK bearer anonymously; got {error:?}")
7006            }
7007        }

7013    #[test]
7014    fn ignore_client_authorization_is_refused_with_identity_or_passthrough() {

7031    /// A credential-free 401 flood must not mint an
7032    /// unbounded stream of durable signed audit sessions. The first
7033    /// 60 rejections per minute get full audit records; the rest are
7034    /// counted only. Proven by driving 100 rejections and asserting
7035    /// the overflow counter carries the surplus.
7036    #[tokio::test]
7037    async fn identity_rejection_audit_records_are_bounded_per_minute() {
7038        let state = null_state();
7039        for i in 0..100u32 {
7040            state
7041                .enqueue_transient_failure(
7042                    &format!("probe-{i}"),
7043                    StopReason::IdentityRejected,
7044                    "missing bearer token".to_owned(),
7045                )
7046                .unwrap();
7047        }
7048        let total = state
7049            .metrics
7050            .counter(
7051                "pv_identity_rejections_total",
7052                "Requests refused by identity validation",
7053            )
7054            .get();
7055        let unaudited = state
7056            .metrics
7057            .counter(
7058                "pv_identity_rejections_unaudited_total",
7059                "Identity rejections beyond the per-minute audit-record budget (counted only)",
7060            )
7061            .get();
7062        assert_eq!(total, 100, "every rejection must be counted");
7063        assert_eq!(
7064            unaudited, 40,
7065            "rejections beyond the 60/min budget must be count-only, not signed audit sessions"
7066        );
7067    }
7068
7069    /// RFC 7235 §2.1 auth-scheme is case-insensitive.
7070    /// A caller sending `Authorization: bearer eyJ...` or `BEARER`
7071    /// used to be silently downgraded to anonymous (when
7072    /// require_identity=false, the shipped default) — repudiation
7073    /// class. Verify each case now parses to the same credential.
7074    #[test]
7075    fn strip_bearer_scheme_matches_case_insensitively() {
7076        for scheme in ["Bearer aaaa", "bearer aaaa", "BEARER aaaa", "BeArEr aaaa"] {
7077            assert_eq!(
7078                strip_bearer_scheme(scheme),
7079                Some("aaaa"),
7080                "scheme {scheme:?} did not parse to `aaaa`"
7081            );
7082        }
7083        // Tab between scheme and credential (per the RFC's `SP / HTAB` grammar).
7084        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("Bearer\taaaa"), Some("aaaa"));
7085        // Multiple spaces are allowed.
7086        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("Bearer   aaaa"), Some("aaaa"));
7087        // Missing whitespace or empty credential — refused.
7088        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("BearerXYZ"), None);
7089        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("Bearer "), None);
7090        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("Bearer"), None);
7091        // Different scheme — refused.
7092        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme("Basic ZmY6bGFyZQ=="), None);
7093        // Too short to hold the scheme name — refused.
7094        assert_eq!(strip_bearer_scheme(""), None);
7095    }

7097    fn null_state() -> AppState {
7098        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
7099        config.require_identity = false;
7100        let sandbox = Sandbox::new(SandboxConfig::default(), Vec::new()).unwrap();
7101        let store = Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new());
7102        let bridge: Arc<dyn EventBus> = Arc::new(NullBus);
7103        let signer: Arc<dyn Signer> = Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::generate());
7104        let embedder = Arc::new(HashEmbedder::default());
7105        let vector_sink: Arc<dyn VectorSink> = Arc::new(NoopVectorSink);
7106        AppState::new_with_backends(
7107            config,
7108            store,
7109            Arc::new(sandbox),
7110            bridge,
7111            None,
7112            signer,
7113            embedder,
7114            vector_sink,
7115        )
7116        .unwrap()
7117    }
7118
7119    fn scoped_token(scopes: &[&str]) -> String {
7120        let now = pv_core::time::now_ms() / pv_core::units::MS_PER_SEC;
7121        let claims = pv_identity::NhiClaims {
7122            sub: "agent:test".into(),
7123            iss: "https://idp.example".into(),
7124            aud: "proofvisor".into(),
7125            iat: now,
7126            nbf: None,
7127            exp: now + 600,
7128            jti: pv_core::new_event_uid(),
7129            azp: None,
7130            act: None,
7131            instance_uid: "scoped-instance".into(),
7132            charter: "scoped-charter".into(),
7133            version: "1".into(),
7134            scopes: scopes.iter().map(|scope| (*scope).to_owned()).collect(),
7135            parent_token: None,
7136        };
7137        let mut header = jsonwebtoken::Header::new(jsonwebtoken::Algorithm::HS256);
7138        header.kid = Some("scope-key".into());
7139        jsonwebtoken::encode(
7140            &header,
7141            &claims,
7142            &jsonwebtoken::EncodingKey::from_secret(b"scope-secret"),
7143        )
7144        .unwrap()
7145    }
7146
7147    fn scoped_state() -> AppState {
7148        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
7149        config.require_identity = true;
7150        config.enforce_identity_scopes = true;
7151        let validator = pv_identity::IdentityValidator::new("proofvisor");
7152        validator
7153            .add_key(
7154                "scope-key",
7155                pv_identity::KeyMaterial::HmacSecret(b"scope-secret".to_vec()),
7156            )
7157            .unwrap();
7158        AppState::new(
7159            config,
7160            Arc::new(InMemoryStore::new()),
7161            Arc::new(Sandbox::new(SandboxConfig::default(), Vec::new()).unwrap()),
7162            Arc::new(NullBus),
7163            Some(Arc::new(validator)),
7164            Arc::new(Ed25519Signer::from_seed(&[12; 32])),
7165        )
7166        .unwrap()
7167    }

7169    #[tokio::test]
7170    async fn real_jwt_scopes_gate_chat_and_lifecycle_operations() {
7171        let state = scoped_state();
7172        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
7173        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("scoped-session"));
7174        headers.insert(
7175            axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
7176            HeaderValue::from_str(&format!("Bearer {}", scoped_token(&["chat:write"]))).unwrap(),
7177        );
7178        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
7179        // RFC 6750 §3.1: missing scope on a valid token is 403
7180        // insufficient_scope (Blocked), NOT 401 — a refreshed token would
7181        // carry the same grant, so telling the SDK to re-authenticate
7182        // (401's semantic) is wrong and loops.
7183        assert!(matches!(
7184            state.authorize_session(&headers, &prepared.session, "session:close"),
7185            Err(PipelineError::Blocked { .. })
7186        ));
7187        headers.insert(
7188            axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION,
7189            HeaderValue::from_str(&format!(
7190                "Bearer {}",
7191                scoped_token(&["chat:write", "session:close"])
7192            ))
7193            .unwrap(),
7194        );
7195        state
7196            .authorize_session(&headers, &prepared.session, "session:close")
7197            .unwrap();
7198    }

7200    #[tokio::test]
7201    async fn upstream_failure_is_added_to_signed_chain() {
7202        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://127.0.0.1:9", "/tmp", "/tmp");
7203        let state = state(config);
7204        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
7205        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("upstream-failure"));
7206        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
7207        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
7208        let session = Arc::clone(&prepared.session);
7209        assert!(matches!(
7210            state.forward_chat(prepared).await,
7211            Err(PipelineError::Upstream { .. })
7212        ));
7213        tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
7214            while session.chain.lock().count() < 2 {
7215                tokio::task::yield_now().await;
7216            }
7217        })
7218        .await
7219        .unwrap();
7220    }
7221
7222    /// Regression lock for CWE-209 information exposure. Before the fix,
7223    /// `PipelineError::upstream(reqwest_err.to_string())` embedded the
7224    /// operator-configured upstream URL in the JSON error body — any client
7225    /// that triggered an upstream failure could discover the URL, potentially
7226    /// leaking an internal hostname. The client-facing message must now be a
7227    /// stable category ("upstream unreachable", "upstream timed out", …) that
7228    /// does not depend on the URL.
7229    #[tokio::test]
7230    async fn upstream_failure_message_does_not_leak_configured_url() {
7231        // Use a distinctive private-network host so a regression is unmissable
7232        // in the assertion below.
7233        let sentinel_url = "http://internal-sentinel-host.corp.example:65001";
7234        let config = HarnessConfig::for_tests(sentinel_url, "/tmp", "/tmp");
7235        let state = state(config);
7236        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
7237        headers.insert(SESSION_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("cwe-209-check"));
7238        headers.insert(WORKFLOW_HEADER, HeaderValue::from_static("signed"));
7239        let prepared = state.prepare_chat(&headers, payload(), None).unwrap();
7240        let error = match state.forward_chat(prepared).await {
7241            Ok(_) => panic!("connect to unroutable host must fail"),
7242            Err(error) => error,
7243        };
7244        let message = error.to_string();
7245        assert!(
7246            !message.contains("internal-sentinel-host"),
7247            "upstream error message {message:?} leaks the configured URL"
7248        );
7249        assert!(
7250            !message.contains("65001"),
7251            "upstream error message {message:?} leaks the configured port"
7252        );
7253        assert!(
7254            !message.contains("corp.example"),
7255            "upstream error message {message:?} leaks the configured domain"
7256        );
7257    }
7258
7259    /// Regression lock for CWE-209 information exposure. Before the fix,
7260    /// `PipelineError::Unauthorized(identity_err.to_string())` embedded the
7261    /// specific `IdentityError` variant's `Display` text in the JSON error
7262    /// body. Because that text distinguishes at least sixteen distinct
7263    /// failure modes — and echoes the attacker-supplied `kid`, `iss`, and
7264    /// `alg` values — each response became an enumeration oracle: an
7265    /// attacker could iterate candidate `kid`s and read the response body
7266    /// to discover which are configured on our validator. The client-facing
7267    /// message must now be a single stable string, and must never echo
7268    /// attacker-controlled token fields.
7269    #[test]
7270    fn identity_validation_failure_does_not_leak_kid_or_issuer() {
7271        // Every variant that an attacker can reach via a crafted token must
7272        // classify to exactly the same client-facing string. Any divergence
7273        // is an enumeration oracle for validator configuration.
7274        //
7275        // Sentinels that would appear in the raw `Display` output are
7276        // asserted absent — this catches accidental future variants that
7277        // slip through the `_` catch-all with a different classification.
7278        let sentinel_kid = "sentinel-attacker-kid";
7279        let sentinel_iss = "https://sentinel-attacker-iss.example.invalid";
7280        let sentinel_alg = "HS512";
7281        let cases: Vec<(&'static str, pv_identity::IdentityError)> = vec![
7282            (
7283                "Malformed",
7284                pv_identity::IdentityError::Malformed("jwt parse".into()),
7285            ),
7286            ("MissingKid", pv_identity::IdentityError::MissingKid),
7287            (
7288                "UnknownKid",
7289                pv_identity::IdentityError::UnknownKid(sentinel_kid.into()),
7290            ),
7291            (
7292                "AlgorithmRejected",
7293                pv_identity::IdentityError::AlgorithmRejected {
7294                    alg: sentinel_alg.into(),
7295                    kid: sentinel_kid.into(),
7296                },
7297            ),
7298            (
7299                "Verification",
7300                pv_identity::IdentityError::Verification("bad sig".into()),
7301            ),
7302            ("TtlTooLong", pv_identity::IdentityError::TtlTooLong(9999)),
7303            (
7304                "BadTimestamps",
7305                pv_identity::IdentityError::BadTimestamps { iat: 10, exp: 5 },
7306            ),
7307            (
7308                "FutureIat",
7309                pv_identity::IdentityError::FutureIat {
7310                    iat: 999_999_999,
7311                    now: 1,
7312                },
7313            ),
7314            ("EmptyField", pv_identity::IdentityError::EmptyField("charter")),
7315            (
7316                "SpoofingCharacter",
7317                pv_identity::IdentityError::SpoofingCharacter("charter"),
7318            ),
7319            (
7320                "BadIssuer",
7321                pv_identity::IdentityError::BadIssuer(sentinel_iss.into()),
7322            ),
7323            (
7324                "ScopeEscalation",
7325                pv_identity::IdentityError::ScopeEscalation {
7326                    scope: "chat:write".into(),
7327                },
7328            ),
7329            (
7330                "ExpEscalation",
7331                pv_identity::IdentityError::ExpEscalation {
7332                    child: 100,
7333                    parent: 50,
7334                },
7335            ),
7336            ("ChainTooDeep", pv_identity::IdentityError::ChainTooDeep(5)),
7337        ];
7338
7339        let mut classifications: std::collections::BTreeSet<&'static str> = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
7340        for (name, error) in &cases {
7341            let client_msg = super::classify_identity_error(error);
7342            classifications.insert(client_msg);
7343            assert!(
7344                !client_msg.contains(sentinel_kid),
7345                "{name}: client message {client_msg:?} echoes attacker kid"
7346            );
7347            assert!(
7348                !client_msg.contains(sentinel_iss),
7349                "{name}: client message {client_msg:?} echoes attacker iss"
7350            );
7351            assert!(
7352                !client_msg.contains(sentinel_alg),
7353                "{name}: client message {client_msg:?} echoes attacker alg"
7354            );
7355            // The full PipelineError as rendered to the client must also
7356            // not leak — this is what actually goes on the wire via
7357            // `pipeline_error()` -> `Json(json!({"error": err.to_string()}))`.
7358            let wire = PipelineError::Unauthorized(client_msg.to_owned()).to_string();
7359            assert!(
7360                !wire.contains(sentinel_kid),
7361                "{name}: wire error {wire:?} echoes attacker kid"
7362            );
7363            assert!(
7364                !wire.contains(sentinel_iss),
7365                "{name}: wire error {wire:?} echoes attacker iss"
7366            );
7367            assert!(
7368                !wire.contains(sentinel_alg),
7369                "{name}: wire error {wire:?} echoes attacker alg"
7370            );
7371        }
7372        // All attacker-reachable variants collapse to exactly one class —
7373        // the `Jwks` variant (server-side misconfig only, not on the
7374        // request path) may distinctly classify.
7375        assert_eq!(
7376            classifications.len(),
7377            1,
7378            "attacker-reachable IdentityError variants classify to multiple messages {classifications:?}: this is an enumeration oracle"
7379        );
7380
7381        // And Jwks classifies distinctly (server-side, not attacker-reachable).
7382        let jwks_msg = super::classify_identity_error(&pv_identity::IdentityError::Jwks("bad jwks".into()));
7383        assert_ne!(
7384            jwks_msg,
7385            *classifications.iter().next().unwrap(),
7386            "server-side misconfig should classify distinctly for operator dashboards"
7387        );
7388    }
7389
7390    /// The secret reader must fail loudly on missing/empty sources and
7391    /// trim whitespace — silent unauthenticated proxying is the worst
7392    /// failure mode because the operator sees only upstream 401s.
7393    #[test]
7394    fn read_secret_source_handling() {
7395        let env = |name: &str| -> Option<String> {
7396            match name {
7397                "GOOD_KEY" => Some("sk-live-123\n".into()),
7398                "EMPTY_KEY" => Some("   ".into()),
7399                _ => None,
7400            }
7401        };
7402        assert_eq!(
7403            super::read_secret_from(env, Some("GOOD_KEY"), None, "upstream API key")
7404                .unwrap()
7405                .as_deref(),
7406            Some("sk-live-123"),
7407            "value must be trimmed"
7408        );
7409        assert!(super::read_secret_from(env, Some("MISSING_KEY"), None, "upstream API key").is_err());
7410        assert!(super::read_secret_from(env, Some("EMPTY_KEY"), None, "upstream API key").is_err());
7411        assert!(super::read_secret_from(env, None, None, "upstream API key")
7412            .unwrap()
7413            .is_none());
7414
7415        // File source: owner-only accepted (with trim), world-readable refused.
7416        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
7417        let path = dir.path().join("key");
7418        std::fs::write(&path, "sk-file-456\n").unwrap();
7419        #[cfg(unix)]
7420        {
7421            use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt as _;
7422            std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o644)).unwrap();
7423            let error = super::read_secret_from(env, None, path.to_str(), "upstream API key").unwrap_err();
7424            assert!(error.to_string().contains("must be owner-only"), "{error}");
7425            std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
7426        }
7427        assert_eq!(
7428            super::read_secret_from(env, None, path.to_str(), "upstream API key")
7429                .unwrap()
7430                .as_deref(),
7431            Some("sk-file-456")
7432        );
7433        // Missing file is a hard error, not silent None.
7434        assert!(
7435            super::read_secret_from(env, None, dir.path().join("absent").to_str(), "upstream API key")
7436                .is_err()
7437        );
7438    }
7439
7440    /// Auth resolution renders scheme-prefixed and raw header values,
7441    /// marks them sensitive, and never leaks the key through `describe`.
7442    #[test]
7443    fn upstream_auth_resolution_and_description() {
7444        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
7445        let key_path = dir.path().join("azure.key");
7446        std::fs::write(&key_path, "azure-raw-key").unwrap();
7447        #[cfg(unix)]
7448        {
7449            use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt as _;
7450            std::fs::set_permissions(&key_path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
7451        }
7452
7453        // Bearer scheme.
7454        let mut config = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://up", "spool", "bridge");
7455        config.upstream_api_key_file = Some(key_path.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
7456        let (name, value) = super::resolve_upstream_auth(&config).unwrap().unwrap();
7457        assert_eq!(name.as_str(), "authorization");
7458        assert_eq!(value.to_str().unwrap(), "Bearer azure-raw-key");
7459        assert!(value.is_sensitive(), "credential must redact in Debug output");
7460
7461        // Raw scheme (Azure api-key style).
7462        config.upstream_auth_header = "api-key".into();
7463        config.upstream_auth_scheme = String::new();
7464        let (name, value) = super::resolve_upstream_auth(&config).unwrap().unwrap();
7465        assert_eq!(name.as_str(), "api-key");
7466        assert_eq!(value.to_str().unwrap(), "azure-raw-key");
7467
7468        // Description names the source but never the value.
7469        let described = super::describe_upstream_auth(&config);
7470        assert!(described.contains("api-key from file"), "{described}");
7471        assert!(!described.contains("azure-raw-key"), "{described}");
7472
7473        // No auth configured resolves to None and describes as none.
7474        let bare = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://up", "spool", "bridge");
7475        assert!(super::resolve_upstream_auth(&bare).unwrap().is_none());
7476        assert_eq!(super::describe_upstream_auth(&bare), "none");
7477
7478        // Tool bearer renders Bearer form from a file source.
7479        let mut tooling = HarnessConfig::for_tests("http://up", "spool", "bridge");
7480        tooling.tool_upstream_url = Some("http://tools/mcp".into());
7481        tooling.tool_upstream_bearer_file = Some(key_path.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
7482        let bearer = super::resolve_tool_auth(&tooling).unwrap().unwrap();
7483        assert_eq!(bearer.to_str().unwrap(), "Bearer azure-raw-key");
7484        assert!(bearer.is_sensitive());
7485    }
7486}
